              '// On-device colorize decode moved to native ImageIO downscale (see\n'
              '// _decodeJpegNative + AetherARKitPlugin decodeJpegForColor). Pure-Dart\n'
              '// full-res decode (img.decodeImage over 4K × N frames) was the "white cloud"\n'
              '// regression — 1.5-4 s/frame on a throttled A16, never finished. Full-res\n'
              '// stays host-regen-only.\n'
              '\n'
              'Uint8List? _buildCaptureCardThumbnailBytes(String sourcePath) {\n'
              '  final decoded = img.decodeImage(File(sourcePath).readAsBytesSync());\n'
              '  if (decoded == null) return null;\n'
              '\n'
              '  var image = img.bakeOrientation(decoded);\n'
              '  if (image.width > image.height) {\n'
              '    image = img.copyRotate(image, angle: 90);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  const maxEdge = 1024;\n'
              '  final longEdge = math.max(image.width, image.height);\n'
              '  if (longEdge > maxEdge) {\n'
              '    image = img.copyResize(\n'
              '      image,\n'
              '      width: image.width >= image.height ? maxEdge : null,\n'
              '      height: image.height > image.width ? maxEdge : null,\n'
              '      interpolation: img.Interpolation.average,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  // EXIF 剥离:实测确认元数据会穿过 resize/rotate 链路(见\n'
              '  // util/image_sanitize.dart 与 test/image_sanitize_test.dart)。\n'
              '  return encodeSanitizedJpg(image, quality: 88);\n'
              '}\n'

              '  // 苹果自带热降帧要等到真热才动;这里更早出手:fair 持续 ≥10s → 30fps,\n'
              '  // 回 nominal 持续 ≥30s → 回 60fps(滞回防抖)。取景流不进重建(重建只吃\n'
              '  // 快门 12MP 静照),纯功耗刀,目标=把 serious(帧税 3.5×)推得更远。\n'
              '  // 执行器各端一个薄调用(iOS=setPreviewFps 会话内调帧间隔,跟踪不断;\n'
              '  // Android ARCore/鸿蒙待接,能力差异实测申报)。\n'
              '  // env OFFICIAL_AETHER_ADAPTIVE_FPS=0(launch)可关。\n'
              '  static final bool _adaptiveFpsEnabled =\n'
              '      Platform.environment[\'OFFICIAL_AETHER_ADAPTIVE_FPS\'] != \'0\';\n'
              '  Timer? _adaptiveFpsTimer;\n'
              '  int _adaptiveFpsCurrent = 60;\n'
              '  int _adaptiveHotSinceMs = 0;\n'
              '  int _adaptiveCoolSinceMs = 0;\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _adaptiveFpsTick() {\n'
              '    if (!_adaptiveFpsEnabled) return;\n'
              '    final tel = PwTelemetry.sample();\n'
              '    if (tel == null) return;\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;\n'
              '    final hot = tel.thermalState >= 1; // fair 即算热(比苹果更早)\n'
              '    if (hot) {\n'
              '      _adaptiveCoolSinceMs = 0;\n'
              '      _adaptiveHotSinceMs = _adaptiveHotSinceMs == 0\n'
              '          ? now\n'
              '          : _adaptiveHotSinceMs;\n'
              '      if (_adaptiveFpsCurrent != 30 && now - _adaptiveHotSinceMs >= 10000) {\n'
              '        _adaptiveSetFps(30, tel.thermalState);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } else {\n'
              '      _adaptiveHotSinceMs = 0;\n'
              '      _adaptiveCoolSinceMs = _adaptiveCoolSinceMs == 0\n'
              '          ? now\n'
              '          : _adaptiveCoolSinceMs;\n'
              '      if (_adaptiveFpsCurrent != 60 && now - _adaptiveCoolSinceMs >= 30000) {\n'
              '        _adaptiveSetFps(60, tel.thermalState);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _adaptiveSetFps(int fps, int thermal) {\n'
              '    _adaptiveFpsCurrent = fps;\n'
              '    // [YIELD-FPS-LINK] 匹配器让路档随帧率联动(30fps=让路减半)。\n'
              '    AetherMatchFlags.setPreviewFps30(fps <= 30);\n'
              '    DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '      \'OfficialARCapturePage\',\n'
              '      \'adaptive-fps: → ${fps}fps (thermal=$thermal)\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    unawaited(\n'
              '      _arKitChannel\n'
              '          .invokeMethod<bool>(\'setPreviewFps\', {\'fps\': fps})\n'
              '          .then((ok) {\n'
              '            if (ok != true) {\n'
              '              DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '                \'OfficialARCapturePage\',\n'
              '                \'adaptive-fps: 执行器拒绝 fps=$fps(能力申报)\',\n'
              '              );\n'
              '            }\n'
              '            return null;\n'
              '          })\n'
              '          .catchError((_) => null),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  // ── [AF-SELFHEAL 2026-08-10 用户签] 失焦死锁自愈(无 UI 零操作)─────\n'
              '  // 病灶:糊掉的低纹理画面无相位信号无反差梯度 → 连续 AF 收不到失焦证据\n'
              '  // 不触发扫描(健身房实测 10s+)。判定(跨端同式,各端只执行 focusNudge):\n'
              '  // 持续糊 ≥1.8s(sharpnessConsensus<100,6Hz 画质样本)且相机基本静止\n'
              '  // (500ms 窗口 <6cm/<6°)且距上次 ≥5s → 踢一脚中心单次对焦。\n'
              '  int _afBlurSinceMs = 0;\n'
              '  int _afLastNudgeMs = 0;\n'
              '  Vector3? _afPrevPos;\n'
              '  Quaternion? _afPrevOrient;\n'
              '  int _afPrevPoseMs = 0;\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _afSelfHealCheck(ARPose p) {\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;\n'
              '    bool stationary = false;\n'
              '    final prevPos = _afPrevPos;\n'
              '    final prevOri = _afPrevOrient;\n'
              '    if (prevPos != null && prevOri != null && now - _afPrevPoseMs <= 900) {\n'
              '      final moved = (p.position - prevPos).length;\n'
              '      final dot =\n'
              '          (p.orientation.w * prevOri.w +\n'
              '                  p.orientation.x * prevOri.x +\n'
              '                  p.orientation.y * prevOri.y +\n'
              '                  p.orientation.z * prevOri.z)\n'
              '              .abs()\n'
              '              .clamp(0.0, 1.0);\n'
              '      final angle = 2 * math.acos(dot);\n'
              '      stationary = moved < 0.06 && angle < 0.10;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (now - _afPrevPoseMs > 400) {\n'
              '      _afPrevPos = p.position.clone();\n'
              '      _afPrevOrient = Quaternion.copy(p.orientation);\n'
              '      _afPrevPoseMs = now;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final q = p.quality;\n'
              '    if (q == null) return;\n'
              '    final blurred = q.sharpnessConsensus < 100.0;\n'
              '    if (!blurred) {\n'
              '      _afBlurSinceMs = 0;\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!stationary) return; // 移动中的糊是运动模糊,不踢\n'
              '    _afBlurSinceMs = _afBlurSinceMs == 0 ? now : _afBlurSinceMs;\n'
              '    if (now - _afBlurSinceMs >= 1800 && now - _afLastNudgeMs >= 5000) {\n'
              '      _afLastNudgeMs = now;\n'
              '      _afBlurSinceMs = 0;\n'
              '      DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '        \'OfficialARCapturePage\',\n'
              '        \'af-selfheal: nudge fired \'\n'
              '            \'(sharpC=${q.sharpnessConsensus.toStringAsFixed(0)})\',\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      unawaited(\n'
              '        _arKitChannel\n'
              '            .invokeMethod<bool>(\'focusNudge\')\n'
              '            .then((_) => null)\n'
              '            .catchError((_) => null),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _maybePushEngineDraft() {\n'
              '    if (!_recording || _projectPhotos.count < 1) return;\n'
              '    final sfm = _officialSfmArCloud;\n'
              '    if (sfm != null && sfm.xyz.isNotEmpty) return; // SfM 云已接管显示\n'
              '    final nowMs = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;\n'
              '    if (nowMs - _engineDraftLastBuildMs < _engineDraftThrottleMs) return;\n'
              '    _engineDraftLastBuildMs = nowMs;\n'
              '    // voxels getter 已按 observations 降序 —— 截断即"最稳的前 N 个"。\n'
              '    final voxels = _previewModel.voxels;\n'
              '    var n = 0;\n'
              '    for (final v in voxels) {\n'
              '      if (v.observations >= _engineDraftMinObservations) n++;\n'
              '      if (n >= _engineDraftMaxPoints) break;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (n == 0) return;\n'
              '    final xyz = Float32List(n * 3);\n'
              '    final rgb = Uint8List(n * 3);\n'
              '    var i = 0;\n'
              '    for (final v in voxels) {\n'
              '      if (v.observations < _engineDraftMinObservations) continue;\n'
              '      final base = i * 3;\n'
              '      xyz[base] = v.position.x;\n'
              '      xyz[base + 1] = v.position.y;\n'
              '      xyz[base + 2] = v.position.z;\n'
              '      // [2026-08-09 用户签决"全部都是白色"] 草稿云原取体素真彩(相机采样均值),\n'
              '      // 与 SfM 云统一改白 —— 不然开头几秒彩色、SfM 云一到全白,肉眼一跳。\n'
              '      rgb[base] = 255;\n'
              '      rgb[base + 1] = 255;\n'
              '      rgb[base + 2] = 255;\n'
              '      i++;\n'
              '      if (i >= n) break;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _engineDraftCloud = CoverageCloudPacked(xyz, rgb);\n'
              '    _scheduleCoveragePush();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  // ─── "拍摄角度不足"实时横幅(补强1,真值口径)───────────────────────\n'
              '  // starvedTrue(观测达标但真实三角化角低于 parallaxMinDeg=5° 的体素数,\n'
              '  // 2026-07-11 阈值校准 8°→5°)持续 ≥20 → 顶部\n'
              '  // 非阻塞横幅提示绕行补拍;回落 <10(滞回)自动隐藏。去抖/滞回状态机在\n'
              '  // parallax_banner_gate.dart(纯 Dart,tool/parallax_banner_check.dart\n'
              '  // 断言);采样**不加新计时器**,挂在既有的 markCapture 回调\n'
              '  // (_onCoverageKeyframe)与 SfmLiveTrueParallax 事件上。\n'
              '  final StarvedParallaxBannerGate _starvedBannerGate =\n'
              '      StarvedParallaxBannerGate();\n'
              '  bool _starvedBannerVisible = false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  // ─── 拥塞遥测标签(07-12 签决:快门彻底不限流)──────────────────────\n'
              '  // 快门永不因队列深度/thermal 被阻挡——积压走 sfm_live_recon 的磁盘 spool\n'
              '  // 队列(不丢帧、不爆内存),热保护由 native 热调速器透明承担。这里只保留\n'
              '  // 一个**纯观测**标签(shutter_backpressure_gate.shutterPaceNext:队列 +\n'
              '  // thermal → normal/soft/hard 三级),转换时记一行 `shutter_pace` 遥测便于\n'
              '  // 事后画积压曲线;绝不 gate 快门、不置灰、不弹横幅。\n'
              '  ShutterPace _shutterPace = ShutterPace.normal;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 最近一次采到的 thermal 桶(0 nominal · 1 fair · 2 serious · 3 critical;\n'
              '  /// **-1 = 未知**,与 `_recomputeShutterPace` 里的降级口径同源)。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// 只喂**自动拍**的 tick 间隔(spec §7「热态 critical 只拉长间隔、不停止」,\n'
              '  /// 兑现在 `autoCaptureTickIntervalSec`)。手动快门一个字节不受影响 ——\n'
              '  /// 07-12 签决的「快门彻底不限流」不变。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// 为什么缓存而不是每个 pose 现采:`PwTelemetry.sample()` 是一次 FFI +\n'
              '  /// 三个 native 指针的分配/释放,而 pose 流是 20–60 Hz;\n'
              '  /// [_recomputeShutterPace] 本来就在每次高清照落地与每次 SfM 队列事件上\n'
              '  /// 采一次(自动拍跑起来至少 1 Hz),而机温是分钟级的量,这个刷新率足够。\n'
              '  int _lastThermalState = -1;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 遥测【WAIT-BUDGET 2026-07-29】上一次快门的 epoch ms。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// 用户签决「可忍受发热,不可忍受等待变长」后,**快门间隔是整笔账的分母**:\n'
              '  /// 流式 SfM 的逐帧成本(host 实测 A=760ms/帧、候选 K30 臂=1144ms/帧)只有\n'
              '  /// 低于用户实际的按快门间隔时才对用户隐形。这个分母我们**从来没量过**,\n'
              '  /// 没有它就无法判断 K30 的 +384ms/帧 是被完全吸收还是变成欠债。\n'
              '  /// `shutter` 事件本身带 `t`,理论上可事后差分,但失败/被拒的快门不写事件,\n'
              '  /// 差分会把它们静默算成"用户拍得慢",故记成一等字段。\n'
              '  int _lastShutterMs = 0;\n'
              '\n'
              '  // ─── AR 照片卡片四态边框(黑/白/红/黄,判定全在 Dart)──────────────\n'
              '  // 状态机在 photo_card_state.dart;native(AetherARKitPlugin 的\n'
              '  // setPhotoCardStates)只收 jpegPath→channelValue 做哑渲染。事件驱动:\n'
              '  // SfmLiveConnectivity(拍摄期合成连通性)/ finalize 快照(真值)/\n'
              '  // markCapture(视差中位数变化)三处触发差量刷新,不轮询。\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 最新一份 posesPacked。拍摄期 = worker 的合成连通性(只有\n'
              '  /// [frameId, registered] 有效);finalize 快照到达后 = COLMAP 真值。\n'
              '  Float64List _sfmLatestPoses = Float64List(0);\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Route B(真实三角化角):worker 的 SfmLiveTrueParallax 事件带来的\n'
              '  /// "frameId → 该帧观测点真实三角化角中位数(度)"。判黄**只用真值**\n'
              '  /// (photo_card_state.frameLowParallaxTrue):真值未到达的已注册帧保持\n'
              '  /// 黑(处理中),视锥近似已彻底退出卡片判定(白→黄反序修复);4°/6°\n'
              '  /// 滞回消黄白抖动。合并式 upsert:本次没被采样到的帧保留旧值。\n'
              '  final Map<int, double> _trueFrameParallaxDeg = <int, double>{};\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 白态粘性(防动态污染):frameId → 连续低于白→黄 enter 阈(4°)的\n'
              '  /// 真值**采样**次数(photo_card_state.frameBelowEnterStreak 维护,只在\n'
              '  /// SfmLiveTrueParallax 采样到达时更新 —— 状态机刷新不计数,否则同一份\n'
              '  /// 陈旧采样会被重复计数)。已白帧需连续 2 次采样跌破 enter 阈才转黄,\n'
              '  /// 消"点云长大时一批新低视差点瞬间拉低中位"的单次抖动。\n'
              '  final Map<int, int> _frameBelowEnterStreak = <int, int>{};\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Route B:最近一次 worker 真实视差聚合耗时(ms;-1=尚未到达)。\n'
              '  /// 遥测【guidance】行随行携带,便于真机对 SLA 直接对数。\n'
              '  int _trueParallaxComputeMs = -1;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 已推送给 native 的每卡状态(jpegPath → channelValue),差量推送用。\n'
              '  final Map<String, int> _photoCardStateSent = <String, int>{};\n'
              '\n'
              '  // ─── 遥测(真机验收显微镜,telemetry_official_dart.jsonl)───────────\n'
              '  /// 【tracking】上一次见到的 ARKit trackingStateName —— 变化才记一行。\n'
              '  String? _telemLastTrackingState;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 【card】jpegPath → 拍摄时刻(epoch ms):卡片状态变化行回填\n'
              '  /// "距拍摄延迟"。_onCoverageKeyframe(每次快门的既有回调)顺手记。\n'
              '  final Map<String, int> _photoCaptureEpochMs = <String, int>{};\n'
              '  final Set<String> _failedEvidenceJpegPaths = <String>{};\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 【guidance】5s 节流采样定时器(拍摄中运行,完成/退出即停)。\n'
              '  Timer? _guidanceTelemetryTimer;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 覆盖云推送合并节流(65k 提额,2026-07-11):满载 packed+编码\n'
              '  /// ≈975KB/次,快门与真值注入同秒到达时 leading edge 立即推、400ms\n'
              '  /// 窗口内的后续变更合并成一次 trailing 推。\n'
              '  Timer? _coveragePushTimer;\n'
              '  bool _coveragePushPending = false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 3-state UX: idle → aim → recording.\n'
              '  /// idle:      user has not started anything; tap → enter aim.\n'
              '  /// aim:       crosshair shown center, tap → trigger lockOrigin AT\n'
              '  ///            user\'s current aim direction. If lock succeeds, enter\n'
              '  ///            recording. If fails, stay in aim with hint text.\n'
              '  /// recording: video + dome live; tap → stop + upload.\n'
              '  /// Replaces the legacy auto-lock UX where tapping record kicked off\n'
              '  /// `_lockOriginWhenReady` retry loop in the background. User\n'
              '  /// feedback: should be a deliberate "I\'m aiming at the subject NOW"\n'
              '  /// gesture, not a magic auto-lock.\n'
              '  bool _isAiming = false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// ARKit warm-up gate. False only while the native AR session is still\n'
              '  /// proving that it can deliver frames. Once the pose stream is alive we\n'
              '  /// let the user enter aim mode; if ARKit is temporarily `.limited`,\n'
              '  /// lockOrigin will show the actionable retry hint instead of trapping the\n'
              '  /// user behind "Initializing AR..." forever.\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// Why this exists: on a thermally pressured device (e.g. user came\n'
              '  /// from a home page that rendered spz models for a few minutes), if\n'
              '  /// the user taps lock-subject the moment they hit the capture page,\n'
              '  /// ARKit\'s visual SLAM is still warming up + may immediately drop to\n'
              '  /// .notAvailable / .limited(initializing) for 1-2 s under\n'
              '  /// `ARWorldTrackingTechnique resource constraints [33]`. The dome\n'
              '  /// then freezes that whole time, which reads as "卡了几秒灰色". By\n'
              '  /// The old version required 1.5 s of perfectly continuous\n'
              '  /// `trackingState == .normal`. In real rooms, especially close-up desk\n'
              '  /// shots with blur / low texture, ARKit can flicker normal↔limited for\n'
              '  /// many seconds even though the camera preview and pose stream are usable.\n'
              '  /// That read as a hard freeze. We now open the gate on first healthy pose\n'
              '  /// or after a short bounded fallback once the session is attached.\n'
              '  bool _arWarmupComplete = false;\n'
              '  Timer? _warmupFallbackTimer;\n'
              '  int _warmupPoseEvents = 0;\n'
              '  static const Duration _warmupFallbackDuration = Duration(milliseconds: 1800);\n'
              '\n'
              '  // Pose-stream diagnostic — verifies events arrive at expected rate and\n'
              '  // quality reports come at the throttled 6 Hz from the Swift side. Flip\n'
              '  // `_kDiagLog` to false once the dome is debugged.\n'
              '  static const bool _kDiagLog = true;\n'
              '  final Stopwatch _diagPoseClock = Stopwatch()..start();\n'
              '  int _diagPoseEvents = 0;\n'
              '  int _diagQualityEvents = 0;\n'
              '\n'
              '  DateTime? _lastArSessionResumeAt;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void initState() {\n'
              '    super.initState();\n'
              '    _shutterQueue = ManualCaptureQueue(\n'
              '      maxTickets: kOfficialMaximumCaptureFrames,\n'
              '      execute: _executeShutterTicket,\n'
              '      onError: _onShutterTicketError,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);\n'
              '    // Capture reconstruction runs on-device via streaming SfM (see\n'
              '    // _startSfmLiveRecon) plus server-side recon on upload — no local model\n'
              '    // download gate. The App Store install bundle stays small (~80 MB).\n'
              '    _initCamera();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _initCamera() async {\n'
              '    // ARKit takes exclusive control of the back camera while the AR\n'
              '    // session is running, so we DON\'T initialize a Flutter `camera`\n'
              '    // plugin CameraController in parallel — that produces\n'
              '    // FigCaptureSourceRemote err=-17281 (camera service not\n'
              '    // responding) and breaks both paths. The capture page operates\n'
              '    // off the AR pose stream alone; native side reads pixel buffers\n'
              '    // from `ARFrame.capturedImage` for the Laplacian / signature\n'
              '    // pipeline.\n'
              '    //\n'
              '    // We call `session.attach()` here to start the ARSession as soon\n'
              '    // as the page mounts. lockOrigin needs `tracking == .normal`,\n'
              '    // which can take ~1-2 s after ARKit cold-start; by warming up\n'
              '    // before the user taps Record, the lock fires against a stable\n'
              '    // baseline pose instead of whatever angle ARKit happens to have\n'
              '    // mid-warm-up while the user is still moving the phone.\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final session = CaptureSession(targetPoints: _targetPoints);\n'
              '      _poseSub = session.poseStream.listen((p) {\n'
              '        if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '        _diagPoseEvents++;\n'
              '        if (p.quality != null) _diagQualityEvents++;\n'
              '        if (_diagPoseClock.elapsedMilliseconds >= 5000) {\n'
              '          final secs = _diagPoseClock.elapsedMilliseconds / 1000;\n'
              '          if (_kDiagLog) {\n'
              '            // ignore: avoid_print\n'
              '            print(\n'
              '              \'[CapturePage] 5s pose stream: \'\n'
              '              \'$_diagPoseEvents events \'\n'
              '              \'(${(_diagPoseEvents / secs).toStringAsFixed(1)} Hz), \'\n'
              '              \'$_diagQualityEvents quality \'\n'
              '              \'(${(_diagQualityEvents / secs).toStringAsFixed(1)} Hz), \'\n'
              '              \'hasOrigin=${p.hasOrigin}\',\n'
              '            );\n'
              '          }\n'
              '          _diagPoseEvents = 0;\n'
              '          _diagQualityEvents = 0;\n'
              '          _diagPoseClock.reset();\n'
              '          _diagPoseClock.start();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        _previewModel.updateFromPose(p, photoCount: _projectPhotos.count);\n'
              '        // [AF-SELFHEAL] 失焦死锁自愈判定(取景与拍摄全程生效)。\n'
              '        _afSelfHealCheck(p);\n'
              '        // [ENGINE-DRAFT] 第 1 张后、SfM 云到达前的引擎草稿推送(内部自带\n'
              '        // 节流与让位判断,SfM 云接管后是纯 no-op)。\n'
              '        _maybePushEngineDraft();\n'
              '        // Coverage-cloud position upkeep — never lights points up by itself\n'
              '        // (only markCapture at each shutter does).\n'
              '        _coverageCloud.ingestPose(p);\n'
              '        // 遥测【tracking】:ARKit tracking state 变化事件(既有 pose 流顺手\n'
              '        // 记,只在字符串变化时写一行 —— 正常拍摄整场 <10 行)。\n'
              '        final tsName = p.trackingStateName;\n'
              '        if (tsName != null && tsName != _telemLastTrackingState) {\n'
              '          TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'tracking\', {\n'
              '            \'state\': tsName,\n'
              '            \'prev\': _telemLastTrackingState,\n'
              '          });\n'
              '          _telemLastTrackingState = tsName;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        _checkArWarmup(p);\n'
              '        // ─── 自动采集(spec §5.4)。**唯一**的驱动源就是这条 pose 流。\n'
              '        // 不新起 Timer:elapsedSec / sinceLastTickSec 都是 ARPose.timestamp\n'
              '        // 的差(ARFrame 时间轴 = CACurrentMediaTime,自开机秒数),而本页\n'
              '        // 别处用的是 DateTime.now().microsecondsSinceEpoch —— 两个纪元混用\n'
              '        // 什么都不会抛,只会把 tick 与时间上限的时钟静默算错。\n'
              '        _driveAutoCapture(p);\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      await session.attach();\n'
              '      // [ADAPTIVE-FPS] 策略时钟(5s 轮询,页面生命周期内)。\n'
              '      _adaptiveFpsTimer ??= Timer.periodic(\n'
              '        const Duration(seconds: 5),\n'
              '        (_) => _adaptiveFpsTick(),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      // 遥测【resource】:拍摄页进入 → 通知 Swift 起 10s 资源采样\n'
              '      // (thermal/footprint/电池/CPU/SceneKit FPS →\n'
              '      // telemetry_official_native.jsonl)。\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<void>(\'telemetryCaptureBegin\');\n'
              '      } catch (_) {}\n'
              '      if (!mounted) {\n'
              '        await session.dispose();\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _session = session;\n'
              '        _initializing = false;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      // The pose stream is subscribed before attach completes. A healthy pose\n'
              '      // can therefore win the race, mark warmup complete, and attempt manual\n'
              '      // startup while `_session` is still null. Retry from the other side of\n'
              '      // the rendezvous once the attached session has been published; otherwise\n'
              '      // the fallback sees warmup=true and the shutter stays disabled forever.\n'
              '      if (_arWarmupComplete) {\n'
              '        unawaited(_startManualCapture());\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        _armArWarmupFallback();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } catch (e) {\n'
              '      if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _initError = AppL10n.of(context).captureInitFailed(\'$e\');\n'
              '        _initializing = false;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Open the idle gate once AR has demonstrably started. We prefer a real\n'
              '  /// `.normal` pose, but after a few pose events we also allow limited\n'
              '  /// tracking through so the user can aim and get a concrete lock failure\n'
              '  /// hint rather than a permanent initializer.\n'
              '  void _checkArWarmup(ARPose pose) {\n'
              '    if (_arWarmupComplete) return;\n'
              '    _warmupPoseEvents += 1;\n'
              '    if (pose.isTracking) {\n'
              '      _markArWarmupComplete(\'tracking=normal\');\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (_warmupPoseEvents >= 6) {\n'
              '      _markArWarmupComplete(\n'
              '        \'pose stream active, tracking=${pose.trackingStateName ?? \'unknown\'}\',\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _armArWarmupFallback() {\n'
              '    _warmupFallbackTimer?.cancel();\n'
              '    _warmupFallbackTimer = Timer(_warmupFallbackDuration, () {\n'
              '      if (!mounted || _arWarmupComplete || _session == null) return;\n'
              '      _markArWarmupComplete(\'attached timeout fallback\');\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _markArWarmupComplete(String reason) {\n'
              '    if (_arWarmupComplete) return;\n'
              '    _warmupFallbackTimer?.cancel();\n'
              '    _warmupFallbackTimer = null;\n'
              '    if (_kDiagLog) {\n'
              '      // ignore: avoid_print\n'
              '      print(\'[CapturePage] AR warmup complete: $reason; enabling aim\');\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (mounted) {\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _arWarmupComplete = true;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      // RealityScan-style manual capture: as soon as ARKit is warm, silently\n'
              '      // start the session (auto-lock origin in the background, no aim UI).\n'
              '      unawaited(_startManualCapture());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {\n'
              '    // BGContinuedProcessingTask\'s system card foregrounds the app but does not\n'
              '    // expose a distinct Dart tap callback. While this route owns an active job,\n'
              '    // any foreground return restores its waiting page. The in-app draft card\n'
              '    // below provides the exact same transition without backgrounding.\n'
              '    if (state == AppLifecycleState.resumed &&\n'
              '        _sfmPhase != null &&\n'
              '        _showDraftsWhileReconstructing &&\n'
              '        mounted) {\n'
              '      setState(() => _showDraftsWhileReconstructing = false);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (_session == null) return;\n'
              '    if (state == AppLifecycleState.paused ||\n'
              '        state == AppLifecycleState.hidden) {\n'
              '      // TRUE background — release the camera so the OS doesn\'t kill us, but do\n'
              '      // NOT finalize: keep the in-progress capture (session stays `_started`,\n'
              '      // photos intact) so the album survives a background round-trip.\n'
              '      // `inactive` is TRANSIENT (screenshot, control center, notification) and\n'
              '      // must NOT release/finalize, or the album resets on a screenshot.\n'
              '      //\n'
              '      // 自动拍在这里**停止,而不是暂停**(spec §7)。下面 resumed 分支走的\n'
              '      // _restartArSessionAfterResume → native startSession{\'resume\': true} →\n'
              '      // session.run(configuration),ARKit **可能重定位世界原点**;一个活过\n'
              '      // 后台的基准帧此后指向的是一个不再存在的坐标系,回来第一帧就会拿垃圾\n'
              '      // 开火。resumed 分支**刻意不自动重开** —— 由用户再点一次录制键。\n'
              '      _stopAutoCapture();\n'
              '      _pauseArForBackground();\n'
              '    } else if (state == AppLifecycleState.resumed) {\n'
              '      // Only re-open the camera if a capture is still ACTIVE. Once the finish\n'
              '      // flow stopped the camera for the SfM preview/finalize (camera off to\n'
              '      // free GPU/memory for the solve), a background round-trip must NOT\n'
              '      // reopen it — the preview overlay has no use for the camera.\n'
              '      if (_recording && _sfmPhase == null) {\n'
              '        _restartArSessionAfterResume();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _pauseArForBackground() async {\n'
              '    // Release only the camera; leave the Dart CaptureSession started and its\n'
              '    // retained photos untouched so resume continues the same capture.\n'
              '    final session = _session;\n'
              '    session?.suspendManualCaptureTransactions();\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<void>(\'stopSession\');\n'
              '    } catch (_) {\n'
              '      session?.resumeManualCaptureTransactions();\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _restartArSessionAfterResume() async {\n'
              '    final now = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    final last = _lastArSessionResumeAt;\n'
              '    if (last != null &&\n'
              '        now.difference(last).inMilliseconds < 1200 &&\n'
              '        !(_session?.manualCaptureTransactionsSuspended ?? false)) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _lastArSessionResumeAt = now;\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      // resume:true → native keeps the world map + photo-card anchors (no\n'
              '      // resetTracking / removeExistingAnchors) so the AR cards survive.\n'
              '      await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<void>(\'startSession\', {\'resume\': true});\n'
              '      _cameraResumeFailed = false;\n'
              '      _session?.resumeManualCaptureTransactions();\n'
              '      if (_recording &&\n'
              '          !_finishTapInProgress &&\n'
              '          !_closeTapInProgress &&\n'
              '          !_shutterQueue.accepting) {\n'
              '        _shutterQueue.resume();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '      if (_recording) {\n'
              '        // Continue the SAME capture (session still _started, photos intact).\n'
              '        // Just re-lock the origin in the resumed world frame so new taps keep\n'
              '        // saving. Do NOT reset preview/warmup — that path wipes the album.\n'
              '        final s = _session;\n'
              '        if (s != null) unawaited(s.lockOrigin(distanceMeters: 1.0));\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _arWarmupComplete = false;\n'
              '        _warmupPoseEvents = 0;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      _armArWarmupFallback();\n'
              '      if (_kDiagLog) {\n'
              '        // ignore: avoid_print\n'
              '        print(\'[CapturePage] ARSession restarted after app resume\');\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } catch (e) {\n'
              '      _shutterQueue.cancelPending();\n'
              '      _session?.failSuspendedManualCaptureTransactions(e);\n'
              '      _cameraResumeFailed = true;\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _captureQueueFailureText = \'相机恢复失败；已停止等待中的拍摄，请重试或退出。\';\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (_kDiagLog) {\n'
              '        // ignore: avoid_print\n'
              '        print(\'[CapturePage] ARSession resume restart skipped: $e\');\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _stopRecordingIfRunning() async {\n'
              '    if (!_recording) return;\n'
              '    await _finalizeRecording(navigateToDrafts: false, showSparseHint: false);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _onCloseTap() async {\n'
              '    if (_finalizingRecording ||\n'
              '        _lockInProgress ||\n'
              '        _closeTapInProgress ||\n'
              '        _discardingCapture) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!_recording) {\n'
              '      if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).maybePop(false);\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    if (_finishTapInProgress) _finishCancellationRequested = true;\n'
              '    _closeTapInProgress = true;\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      // [2026-08-22 用户签决] 黑白弹窗 + 开关:"是否保存照片,方便下次补拍"\n'
              '      // 的小开关(默认开=绿=保存,关=红=不保存)+ "确定"/"取消" 两颗按钮。\n'
              '      // (取代 2026-08-09 那版滑轴 —— 见 capture_exit_dialog.dart 文件头。)\n'
              '      // 返回值语义不变:saveExit / discardExit / null=回拍摄。\n'
              '      // 弹窗是模态的:不先停,自动拍会在弹窗背后继续落帧。\n'
              '      _stopAutoCapture();\n'
              '      final choice = await showCaptureExitDialog(context);\n'
              '      if (!mounted || choice == null) return;\n'
              '\n'
              '      if (choice == CaptureExitChoice.saveExit) {\n'
              '        // 退出并保存:与"完成"同一条落草稿链路,但**不启动重建** ——\n'
              '        // 照片与增量 db 原样留在盘上,草稿显示"未完成",点卡片可断点续跑。\n'
              '        // 无损:不 cancelPending,先把在途快门全部落地。\n'
              '        _discardingCapture = true;\n'
              '        final session = _session;\n'
              '        await _shutterQueue.freezeAndDrain();\n'
              '        if (session != null) {\n'
              '          await session.stop();\n'
              '          await session.waitForPendingPhotoSaves();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        final recon = _sfmRecon;\n'
              '        if (recon != null) {\n'
              '          _sfmRecon = null;\n'
              '          await _sfmFeedSub?.cancel();\n'
              '          _sfmFeedSub = null;\n'
              '          await _sfmEventSub?.cancel();\n'
              '          _sfmEventSub = null;\n'
              '          unawaited(recon.dispose());\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        await _persistDraft(showSnackBar: false);\n'
              '        if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _recording = false;\n'
              '          _isAiming = false;\n'
              '          _lockInProgress = false;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '        _previewModel.reset();\n'
              '        // pop(true) = 提示外壳切到"我的草稿"(与完成路径同语义)。\n'
              '        Navigator.of(context).pop(true);\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '\n'
              '      _discardingCapture = true;\n'
              '      _shutterQueue.cancelPending();\n'
              '      final session = _session;\n'
              '      if (session != null) await session.stop();\n'
              '      await _shutterQueue.freezeAndDrain();\n'
              '      if (session != null) {\n'
              '        await session.discardCurrentCapture();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _recording = false;\n'
              '        _isAiming = false;\n'
              '        _lockInProgress = false;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      _previewModel.reset();\n'
              '      Navigator.of(context).pop(false);\n'
              '    } finally {\n'
              '      _discardingCapture = false;\n'
              '      _closeTapInProgress = false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _onCenterTap() async {\n'
              '    final session = _session;\n'
              '    if (session == null) return;\n'
              '\n'
              '    if (_recording) {\n'
              '      await _onFinishTap();\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    if (_isAiming) {\n'
              '      if (_lockInProgress) return;\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _lockInProgress = true;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      // AIM → try LOCK at user\'s current aim direction.\n'
              '      // Single-shot (no retry loop). Failure leaves user in aim with\n'
              '      // a hint snackbar so they can re-aim and retry.\n'
              '      // 1.0 m: matches the typical "stand 1-1.5 m from a chair / bag /\n'
              '      // small object" capture distance. iOS Aether3D\'s original 0.5 m\n'
              '      // assumed close-up handheld figurines; PocketWorld users tend to\n'
              '      // shoot floor-level objects at arm\'s length+, so the smaller\n'
              '      // value put the world origin in the air in front of (rather than\n'
              '      // ON) the subject, shrinking the dome\'s azimuth span.\n'
              '      final result = await session.lockOrigin(distanceMeters: 1.0);\n'
              '      if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '      if (result == null) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _lockInProgress = false;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '          SnackBar(\n'
              '            content: Text(AppL10n.of(context).captureLockFailedHint),\n'
              '            behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // Lock succeeded; proceed to recording (skip auto-retry loop).\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        await session.start(autoLock: false);\n'
              '        if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '        _previewModel.reset();\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _isAiming = false;\n'
              '          _recording = true;\n'
              '          _lockInProgress = false;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      } catch (e) {\n'
              '        if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _lockInProgress = false;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '          SnackBar(\n'
              '            content: Text(\n'
              '              AppL10n.of(context).captureRecordingStartFailed(\'$e\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    // IDLE → enter AIM. Show crosshair, wait for the user to actively\n'
              '    // aim at the subject and tap again to lock. No auto-anchor.\n'
              '    // Gated by [_arWarmupComplete] — onTap on the parent button is\n'
              '    // already null when warmup hasn\'t completed, but defensively\n'
              '    // double-check so a programmatic tap can\'t slip past the gate.\n'
              '    if (!_arWarmupComplete) return;\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _isAiming = true;\n'
              '      _lockInProgress = false;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// RealityScan-style manual capture: silently start the session once ARKit\n'
              '  /// is warm (auto-lock the world origin in the background, no aim crosshair),\n'
              '  /// then each shutter tap takes exactly one photo. Idempotent.\n'
              '  Future<void> _startManualCapture() async {\n'
              '    final session = _session;\n'
              '    if (session == null || _recording || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await session.start(autoLock: true, manualCapture: true);\n'
              '      if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '      // Fresh take → clear any anchored AR cards left from a previous session.\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<void>(\'clearPhotoCards\');\n'
              '      } catch (_) {}\n'
              '      // T6: turn on the live sparse coverage cloud for this take (RS-style —\n'
              '      // ARKit feature points, world-anchored, coloured by coverage)。尊重\n'
              '      // 用户显示开关:开关关着就保持隐藏(数据照常计算)。\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<void>(\n'
              '          \'setFeaturePointsVisible\',\n'
              '          <String, dynamic>{\'visible\': _coverageDotsVisible},\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      } catch (_) {}\n'
              '      _previewModel.reset();\n'
              '      _projectPhotos.clear();\n'
              '      _captureQueueFailureText = null;\n'
              '      _cameraResumeFailed = false;\n'
              '      // Fresh take → empty coverage cloud (0 photos ⇒ 0 dots on screen).\n'
              '      _coverageCloud.reset();\n'
              '      _officialSfmArCloud = null;\n'
              '      _officialSfmDiagnosticTag = null;\n'
              '      _engineDraftCloud = null;\n'
              '      _engineDraftLastBuildMs = 0;\n'
              '      // Fresh take → 卡片边框状态机归零(native 卡片已由 clearPhotoCards\n'
              '      // 清掉,这里清 Dart 侧差量缓存与连通性数据)。\n'
              '      _photoCardStateSent.clear();\n'
              '      _photoCaptureEpochMs.clear();\n'
              '      _failedEvidenceJpegPaths.clear();\n'
              '      _sfmLatestPoses = Float64List(0);\n'
              '      _trueFrameParallaxDeg.clear();\n'
              '      _frameBelowEnterStreak.clear();\n'
              '      _trueParallaxComputeMs = -1;\n'
              '      // 补强1:starved 横幅门与覆盖云同时机归零(下方 setState 会重建)。\n'
              '      _starvedBannerGate.reset();\n'
              '      _starvedBannerVisible = false;\n'
              '      TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'live_cloud_diag_build_v1\', {\n'
              '        \'contract\': liveCloudDiagnosticContractId,\n'
              '        \'dart_build_id\': liveCloudDiagnosticDartBuildId,\n'
              '        \'product_manifest_source\': liveCloudDiagnosticProductManifestSource,\n'
              '        \'observation_only\': true,\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      // force:新一轮拍摄的归零推送必须落到 native,不能被去重门挡掉。\n'
              '      unawaited(_pushCoverageCloud(force: true));\n'
              '      _coverageFeedSub ??= session.sfmFrameStream.listen(_onCoverageKeyframe);\n'
              '      // [SPRINT-FIX + YIELD-FPS-LINK] 新一轮拍摄:框架内匹配器旗复位。\n'
              '      AetherMatchFlags.setCaptureActive(true);\n'
              '      AetherMatchFlags.setPreviewFps30(_adaptiveFpsCurrent <= 30);\n'
              '      setState(() {\n'
              '        _recording = true;\n'
              '        _sfmStarting = true;\n'
              '        _sfmStartFailureText = null;\n'
              '        _isAiming = false;\n'
              '        _lockInProgress = false;\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      // [2026-07-27 UI 签决] 开拍即弹的"20 张"入场提示已删除 —— 每次进\n'
              '      // 拍摄都挡一次取景框、说的又是用户还没到的事。同一句提示改在真正\n'
              '      // 相关的时刻出现:不足 20 张点完成时的 _onFinishTap 对话框。\n'
              '      _startGuidanceTelemetry();\n'
              '      // Capture-time streaming SfM is required for a valid product take.\n'
              '      // Await only worker startup (not reconstruction); controls remain\n'
              '      // disabled while the native lease/session is being acquired.\n'
              '      await _startSfmLiveRecon(session);\n'
              '    } catch (e) {\n'
              '      // ignore: avoid_print\n'
              '      print(\'[OfficialARCapturePage] manual capture start failed: $e\');\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Per committed shutter: frustum-mark the coverage cloud with the\n'
              '  /// frame-exact pose+intrinsics (the same SfmFrameFeed that drives\n'
              '  /// streaming SfM — but fully independent of the SfM worker, so the\n'
              '  /// coverage UX works even where on-device SfM is unavailable).\n'
              '  void _onCoverageKeyframe(OfficialHighResReconstructionInput input) {\n'
              '    final committed = _projectPhotos.commitVerified(\n'
              '      jpegPath: input.jpegPath,\n'
              '      captureTimestamp: input.captureTimestamp,\n'
              '      imageWidth: input.imageWidth,\n'
              '      imageHeight: input.imageHeight,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!committed) {\n'
              '      DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '        \'OfficialARCapturePage\',\n'
              '        \'verified project photo was not committed: ${input.jpegPath}\',\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final feed = SfmFrameFeed(\n'
              '      gray: Uint8List(0),\n'
              '      grayW: input.imageWidth,\n'
              '      grayH: input.imageHeight,\n'
              '      imageW: input.imageWidth,\n'
              '      imageH: input.imageHeight,\n'
              '      intrinsicFxFyCxCy: input.intrinsics,\n'
              '      extrinsic4x4: input.cameraTransform,\n'
              '      timestamp: input.captureTimestamp,\n'
              '      jpegPath: input.jpegPath,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    // 遥测【card】:记下每张照片的拍摄时刻(epoch ms),卡片状态变化行\n'
              '    // 用它算"距拍摄延迟"。既有回调顺手记,零额外调用。\n'
              '    final jp = feed.jpegPath;\n'
              '    if (jp != null) {\n'
              '      _photoCaptureEpochMs[jp] = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // 只在覆盖真的变化时才重打包+推送(markCapture 返回 false = 视锥\n'
              '    // 没罩住任何体素,payload 没变);推送走 400ms 合并节流。\n'
              '    if (_coverageCloud.markCapture(feed)) {\n'
              '      _scheduleCoveragePush();\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // 新照片刷新了体素 maxParallaxDeg → 已注册帧的黄/白可能翻转\n'
              '    // (黄=低视差,补拍换角度后转白)。差量推送,无变化零开销。\n'
              '    _refreshPhotoCardStates();\n'
              '    _sampleStarvedBanner();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 补强1:starved 横幅采样。挂在既有回调上(markCapture 后 +\n'
              '  /// SfmLiveTrueParallax 到达后),不新增计时器。只在可见性真的翻转时\n'
              '  /// setState —— SfmLiveTrueParallax 处理路径刻意不 rebuild,这里保持\n'
              '  /// 同样的克制(翻转是稀有事件)。\n'
              '  void _sampleStarvedBanner() {\n'
              '    if (!_recording) return;\n'
              '    // 真值口径:coverageStats().starvedTrue(观测达标 + 真实三角化角\n'
              '    // < parallaxMinDeg=5°),与【guidance】遥测的 starved_true 字段同源同义。\n'
              '    final visible = _starvedBannerGate.onSample(\n'
              '      _coverageCloud.coverageStats().starvedTrue,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (visible != _starvedBannerVisible && mounted) {\n'
              '      setState(() => _starvedBannerVisible = visible);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 拥塞遥测标签采样(**纯观测,不阻挡快门**):队列深度(SfM facade 的\n'
              '  /// remainingCount,与 `_sfmQueued` 同源)+ thermal 桶(pw_telemetry FFI,\n'
              '  /// 微秒级)→ shutterPaceNext 得出新标签。挂在既有 SfM 队列事件与快门点按\n'
              '  /// 上,零新增计时器;只在标签真的翻转时记一行 `shutter_pace` 遥测(翻转\n'
              '  /// 是稀有事件)。[inSetState] = 调用点已在 setState 回调内,此时只改字段,\n'
              '  /// rebuild 由外层 setState 完成,不嵌套(标签本身不改变任何可见 UI)。\n'
              '  void _recomputeShutterPace({bool inSetState = false}) {\n'
              '    final queue = _sfmRecon?.remainingCount ?? 0;\n'
              '    final thermal = PwTelemetry.sample()?.thermalState ?? -1;\n'
              '    // ⚠️ 记在**早退之前**:档位没翻转时这个函数就 return 了,而热态自己\n'
              '    // 是会变的 —— 记在后面等于只在档位翻转的那几帧更新机温。\n'
              '    // 这一行对手动快门是纯 no-op(没有任何手动路径读它)。\n'
              '    _lastThermalState = thermal;\n'
              '    final next = shutterPaceNext(\n'
              '      previous: _shutterPace,\n'
              '      queueDepth: queue,\n'
              '      thermalState: thermal,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (next == _shutterPace) return;\n'
              '    final prev = _shutterPace;\n'
              '    _shutterPace = next;\n'
              '    TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'shutter_pace\', {\n'
              '      \'from\': prev.name,\n'
              '      \'to\': next.name,\n'
              '      \'queue\': queue,\n'
              '      \'thermal\': thermal,\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '      \'OfficialARCapturePage\',\n'
              '      \'shutter pace ${prev.name}→${next.name} (queue=$queue thermal=$thermal)\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!inSetState && mounted) setState(() {});\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  // ─── RS 复刻:快门上方两个独立显示开关(2026-07-19 用户规格)──────────\n'
              '  // 都是 display-only:关闭只隐藏,不删照片/AR 锚点/拍摄记录/SfM 数据;\n'
              '  // 拍照、覆盖率计算、点云更新、质量判断、重建全部照常;两开关相互独立。\n'
              '  bool _photoCardsVisible = true; // 左:AR 照片卡片(照片图标)\n'
              '  bool _coverageDotsVisible = true; // 右:彩色覆盖点(3×3 九点图标,恒黄)\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _togglePhotoCards() async {\n'
              '    setState(() => _photoCardsVisible = !_photoCardsVisible);\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<void>(\n'
              '        \'setPhotoCardsVisible\',\n'
              '        <String, dynamic>{\'visible\': _photoCardsVisible},\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    } catch (_) {\n'
              '      // Display-only channel — never let it disturb capture.\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _toggleCoverageDots() async {\n'
              '    setState(() => _coverageDotsVisible = !_coverageDotsVisible);\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<void>(\n'
              '        \'setFeaturePointsVisible\',\n'
              '        <String, dynamic>{\'visible\': _coverageDotsVisible},\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    } catch (_) {}\n'
              '    if (_coverageDotsVisible) {\n'
              '      // 隐藏期 native 丢弃推送防旧云;重开必须立刻补推当前全量状态——\n'
              '      // 用户规格:显示最新计算结果,不能等用户再拍一张才恢复。\n'
              '      // force:native 侧已丢弃过,内容没变也必须重推。\n'
              '      await _pushCoverageCloud(force: true);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Ships the latest stable official SfM version to the native dumb renderer.\n'
              '  /// Before the first successful 20-frame global BA this intentionally sends\n'
              '  /// an empty cloud, even though private capture-guidance voxels already exist.\n'
              '  /// The exact payload last handed to native, so an unchanged cloud is never\n'
              '  /// re-marshalled. [AR-PERF 2026-07-26] eaf8706 repointed this from the\n'
              '  /// capped coverage-voxel cloud (~188 pts/photo, 65k ceiling) to the uncapped\n'
              '  /// official SfM cloud (~1.3k pts/frame): at 126 frames that is ~170k points,\n'
              '  /// and every display-toggle / coverage-dot tap re-sent the identical buffer\n'
              '  /// across the method channel. Copying that per push is what made the capture\n'
              '  /// UI feel laggy late in a long take. The cloud object is replaced wholesale\n'
              '  /// on each publish, so identity is a sound change test.\n'
              '  CoverageCloudPacked? _pushedArCloud;\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _pushCoverageCloud({\n'
              '    bool force = false,\n'
              '    LiveCloudTelemetryTag? diagnosticTag,\n'
              '  }) async {\n'
              '    // [ENGINE-DRAFT] 优先级:SfM 云(配对草稿/正式)> 引擎草稿 > 空。\n'
              '    final packed =\n'
              '        _officialSfmArCloud ??\n'
              '        _engineDraftCloud ??\n'
              '        CoverageCloudPacked(Float32List(0), Uint8List(0));\n'
              '    if (!force && identical(packed, _pushedArCloud)) return;\n'
              '    _pushedArCloud = packed;\n'
              '    var tag = diagnosticTag;\n'
              '    if (tag == null &&\n'
              '        _officialSfmArCloud != null &&\n'
              '        identical(packed, _officialSfmArCloud)) {\n'
              '      tag = _officialSfmDiagnosticTag;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (tag == null) {\n'
              '      final now = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;\n'
              '      tag = _liveCloudTelemetry\n'
              '          .receive(\n'
              '            source:\n'
              '                _engineDraftCloud != null &&\n'
              '                    identical(packed, _engineDraftCloud)\n'
              '                ? \'engine_draft\'\n'
              '                : \'empty\',\n'
              '            publishVersion: 0,\n'
              '            pointCount: packed.xyz.length ~/ 3,\n'
              '            receiveEpochMs: now,\n'
              '          )\n'
              '          .withComputeDone(now);\n'
              '      TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'live_cloud_receive_v1\', tag.baseFields);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final channel = _liveCloudTelemetry.channelSend(\n'
              '      tag,\n'
              '      channelSendEpochMs: DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\n'
              '      \'live_cloud_channel_send_v1\',\n'
              '      channel.fields,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await _arKitChannel\n'
              '          .invokeMethod<void>(\'setCoveragePointCloud\', <String, dynamic>{\n'
              '            \'xyz\': packed.xyz,\n'
              '            \'rgb\': packed.rgb,\n'
              '            ...tag.channelArguments(channelPushSequence: channel.pushSequence),\n'
              '          });\n'
              '      TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'live_cloud_channel_ack_v1\', {\n'
              '        ...channel.fields,\n'
              '        \'channel_ack_t\': DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch,\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    } catch (_) {\n'
              '      TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'live_cloud_channel_error_v1\', {\n'
              '        ...channel.fields,\n'
              '        \'channel_error_t\': DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch,\n'
              '      });\n'
              '      // Display-only channel — never let it disturb capture. Forget the\n'
              '      // payload so the next push retries instead of de-duplicating against a\n'
              '      // send that never landed.\n'
              '      _pushedArCloud = null;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Atomically replaces the AR overlay with a globally refined official SfM\n'
              '  /// snapshot. This is display-only: no point is removed or rewritten in the\n'
              '  /// reconstruction or final PLY.\n'
              '  Future<void> _publishOfficialSfmCloudToAr(\n'
              '    SfmLiveSnapshot snapshot,\n'
              '    LiveCloudTelemetryTag receiveTag,\n'
              '  ) async {\n'
              '    // [AR-EVERY-FRAME 2026-08-04] 接受两种拍摄期流式 source:检查点的\n'
              '    // \'streaming_global_ba\'(既有),以及每帧的 \'streaming_local_ba_live\'(实验臂\n'
              '    // OFFICIAL_AETHER_AR_EVERY_FRAME=1 时才由生产端发出)。env 关时后者永不\n'
              '    // 到达,故此处放开对现状零影响(逐字节复现)。注意用 \'_live\' 后缀,\n'
              '    // 与 finish-time 终态云的 \'streaming_local_ba\' 严格区分。两种都是\n'
              '    // display-only,不删改重建或最终 PLY。\n'
              '    final source = snapshot.summary[\'source\'];\n'
              '    if (!_recording ||\n'
              '        (source != \'streaming_global_ba\' &&\n'
              '            source != \'streaming_local_ba_live\')) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (snapshot.pointCount <= 0) {\n'
              '      // [ENGINE-DRAFT 2026-08-09] 删照片把 live 模型删空(3→2→1)时,worker\n'
              '      // 的 frame_removed 推送会带 0 点 —— 此前这里直接 return,屏幕会留着\n'
              '      // 删除前的旧云。现在:清掉 SfM 云占位,让 _pushCoverageCloud 落回\n'
              '      // 引擎草稿(还有照片时)或空(全删光)。\n'
              '      if (snapshot.summary[\'frame_removed\'] != null) {\n'
              '        _officialSfmArCloud = null;\n'
              '        _officialSfmDiagnosticTag = null;\n'
              '        if (_projectPhotos.count < 1) _engineDraftCloud = null;\n'
              '        _engineDraftLastBuildMs = 0;\n'
              '        await _pushCoverageCloud(force: true);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [2026-08-09 用户签决] 拍摄期 AR live 云**全白**,不再按质量分绿/黄/红。\n'
              '    // 原先每个点按 track 长度过 kCaptureQualityRamp 上色(2=红 3=橙 4=黄绿\n'
              '    // ≥5=绿),用户裁掉:"不用根据颜色区分状态"。ramp 本体与它的引导横幅\n'
              '    // ("对黄色区域…",挂的是覆盖体素统计,另一套系统)不在本刀范围。\n'
              '    final rgb = Uint8List(snapshot.pointCount * 3);\n'
              '    rgb.fillRange(0, rgb.length, 255);\n'
              '    // Potree-style hierarchy ordering is computed on a worker isolate. This is\n'
              '    // display-only: the source SfM snapshot and final PLY stay intact.\n'
              '    final displayCloud = await compute(buildProgressivePointCloud, (\n'
              '      xyz: snapshot.xyz,\n'
              '      rgb: rgb,\n'
              '    ), debugLabel: \'capture_progressive_octree_order\');\n'
              '    final computeDoneAt = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;\n'
              '    TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\n'
              '      \'live_cloud_compute_done_v1\',\n'
              '      _liveCloudTelemetry.computeDoneFields(\n'
              '        receiveTag,\n'
              '        computeDoneEpochMs: computeDoneAt,\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!_recording) return;\n'
              '    final completedTag = receiveTag.withComputeDone(computeDoneAt);\n'
              '    _officialSfmArCloud = CoverageCloudPacked(\n'
              '      displayCloud.xyz,\n'
              '      displayCloud.rgb,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    _officialSfmDiagnosticTag = completedTag;\n'
              '    await _pushCoverageCloud(diagnosticTag: completedTag);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 覆盖云推送合并节流:首次调用立即推(引导反馈不加延迟),400ms 窗口\n'
              '  /// 内的后续变更合并为窗口结束时的一次 trailing 推(推的永远是当时的\n'
              '  /// 最新状态,不会丢末尾更新)。65k 满载单次 payload ≈975KB —— 快门\n'
              '  /// (~0.4Hz)与真值注入(~0.3Hz)撞在同一秒时从两次推缩成一次。\n'
              '  void _scheduleCoveragePush() {\n'
              '    if (_coveragePushTimer != null) {\n'
              '      _coveragePushPending = true;\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    unawaited(_pushCoverageCloud());\n'
              '    _coveragePushTimer = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 400), () {\n'
              '      _coveragePushTimer = null;\n'
              '      if (_coveragePushPending) {\n'
              '        _coveragePushPending = false;\n'
              '        _scheduleCoveragePush();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 四态边框状态机刷新(判定纯 Dart,见 photo_card_state.dart):对每个\n'
              '  /// 已喂 SfM 的帧算 黑(未处理)/白(已注册)/黄(已注册但低视差)/\n'
              '  /// 红(断联),与上次推送差量对比,只把变化的 jpegPath→state 推给\n'
              '  /// native 哑渲染器。没喂过 SfM 的照片不推——native 默认黑,语义一致。\n'
              '  void _refreshPhotoCardStates() {\n'
              '    final recon = _sfmRecon;\n'
              '    if (recon == null) return;\n'
              '    final diff = <String, int>{};\n'
              '    recon.fedFrameMeta.forEach((frameId, meta) {\n'
              '      // 白→黄反序修复:判黄只用真值(SfmLiveTrueParallax)。真值未到达\n'
              '      // → frameLowParallaxTrue 返回 null → 已注册帧保持黑(处理中),\n'
              '      // 视锥近似不再参与卡片判定(覆盖云体素级的真值优先+近似兜底不受\n'
              '      // 影响)。滞回 4°/6°:上次白/黄裁决从已推送状态恢复,消 1↔3 抖动;\n'
              '      // 白→黄另加粘性(连续 2 次采样 <4°,计数在 SfmLiveTrueParallax\n'
              '      // 到达处维护,这里只读)。\n'
              '      final prev = _photoCardStateSent[meta.jpegPath];\n'
              '      final state = photoCardSfmState(\n'
              '        frameId: frameId,\n'
              '        posesPacked: _sfmLatestPoses,\n'
              '        lowParallax: frameLowParallaxTrue(\n'
              '          trueMedianDeg: _trueFrameParallaxDeg[frameId],\n'
              '          wasLowParallax: prev == PhotoCardSfmState.lowParallax.channelValue\n'
              '              ? true\n'
              '              : prev == PhotoCardSfmState.registered.channelValue\n'
              '              ? false\n'
              '              : null,\n'
              '          belowEnterStreak: _frameBelowEnterStreak[frameId] ?? 0,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      _projectPhotos.updateAnalysisState(meta.jpegPath, state);\n'
              '      final st = state.channelValue;\n'
              '      if (_photoCardStateSent[meta.jpegPath] != st) {\n'
              '        diff[meta.jpegPath] = st;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    if (diff.isEmpty) return;\n'
              '    // 遥测【card】:每次状态变化一行(旧态→新态 + 距拍摄延迟)。差量处\n'
              '    // 数据都在手上;必须在 addAll 覆盖前读旧态。\n'
              '    final nowMs = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;\n'
              '    diff.forEach((jpeg, next) {\n'
              '      final capturedAt = _photoCaptureEpochMs[jpeg];\n'
              '      TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'card\', {\n'
              '        \'jpeg\': jpeg.split(\'/\').last,\n'
              '        \'old\': _photoCardStateSent[jpeg],\n'
              '        \'new\': next,\n'
              '        if (capturedAt != null) \'since_capture_ms\': nowMs - capturedAt,\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    _photoCardStateSent.addAll(diff);\n'
              '    unawaited(_pushPhotoCardStates(diff));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Consecutive native add_frame faults. A single throw is noise (one lost\n'
              '  /// frame, the next one usually lands); a RUN of them means the worker is\n'
              '  /// broken and every further shutter tap is wasted, which is the state one\n'
              '  /// device take spent 25 frames in while the UI blamed the user\'s coverage.\n'
              '  int _sfmInternalFailureStreak = 0;\n'
              '  static const int _kSfmInternalFailureWarnStreak = 3;\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _noteSfmInternalFailure(String reason) {\n'
              '    _sfmInternalFailureStreak++;\n'
              '    DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '      \'OfficialARCapturePage\',\n'
              '      \'sfm internal fault #$_sfmInternalFailureStreak ($reason) — \'\n'
              '          \'frame not fed; NOT a coverage problem\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (_sfmInternalFailureStreak < _kSfmInternalFailureWarnStreak) return;\n'
              '    const text =\n'
              '        \'点云重建服务出错，最近的照片没有进入重建。\'\n'
              '        \'照片已保留，但继续拍摄不会改善——请结束本次拍摄后重试。\';\n'
              '    if (_sfmStartFailureText == text || !mounted) return;\n'
              '    // ⚠️ 这一句翻的是 `_sfmCaptureReady`,而它一假,`_admitShutterCapture`\n'
              '    // 就**恒**被拒 —— 自动拍从此每次开火都入队失败。基准帧按设计不动,\n'
              '    // 于是它会一路空转到 5 分钟上限才停,期间一张都拍不出来。\n'
              '    // 与其余四条会让入队失效的路径(退后台 / 退出弹窗 / 完成 / finalize)\n'
              '    // 同源:让入队失效的人负责停自动拍。\n'
              '    // (幂等:_stopAutoCapture 开头就 `if (!isRunning) return;`。)\n'
              '    _stopAutoCapture();\n'
              '    setState(() => _sfmStartFailureText = text);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _markPhotoDisconnected(String jpegPath, String reason) {\n'
              '    const state = PhotoCardSfmState.disconnected;\n'
              '    _projectPhotos.updateAnalysisState(jpegPath, state);\n'
              '    if (_photoCardStateSent[jpegPath] == state.channelValue) return;\n'
              '    _photoCardStateSent[jpegPath] = state.channelValue;\n'
              '    DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '      \'OfficialARCapturePage\',\n'
              '      \'photo retained but marked disconnected: \'\n'
              '          \'${jpegPath.split(\'/\').last} ($reason)\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    unawaited(\n'
              '      _pushPhotoCardStates(<String, int>{jpegPath: state.channelValue}),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 遥测【guidance】:拍摄期 5s 节流采样 —— 断连区段摘要(合成连通性\n'
              '  /// posesPacked)+ 覆盖云红黄绿体素计数 + 视差饥饿体素数。全部只读\n'
              '  /// 现有状态(coverageStats/disconnectedSegmentsFromPoses),不碰引导逻辑。\n'
              '  void _startGuidanceTelemetry() {\n'
              '    _guidanceTelemetryTimer?.cancel();\n'
              '    _guidanceTelemetryTimer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 5), (_) {\n'
              '      if (!_recording) return;\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        final cov = _coverageCloud.coverageStats();\n'
              '        final segs = disconnectedSegmentsFromPoses(_sfmLatestPoses);\n'
              '        var disconnectedFrames = 0;\n'
              '        for (final s in segs) {\n'
              '          disconnectedFrames += s.count;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'guidance\', {\n'
              '          \'covered\': cov.covered,\n'
              '          \'red\': cov.red,\n'
              '          \'yellow\': cov.yellow,\n'
              '          \'green\': cov.green,\n'
              '          \'parallax_capped\': cov.parallaxCapped,\n'
              '          \'parallax_starved\': _coverageCloud.parallaxStarvedVoxelCount,\n'
              '          // Route B 真值对数字段:starved_true = 观测达标但真实三角化角\n'
              '          // < parallaxMinDeg(5°,2026-07-11 校准)的体素(route A 同口径\n'
              '          // 上次只报 1/5997);true_lt8/true_vox 字段名沿革自旧锚 8°,现\n'
              '          // 口径 = <5°(历史对数:最终云点级 <8° 占比实锤 40.3%);\n'
              '          // true_frame_lp = 真值判黄的帧数;true_ms = worker 聚合耗时。\n'
              '          \'starved_true\': cov.starvedTrue,\n'
              '          \'true_vox\': cov.trueVoxels,\n'
              '          \'true_lt8\': cov.trueLt8,\n'
              '          \'true_frame_lp\': _trueFrameParallaxDeg.values\n'
              '              .where((d) => d < _coverageCloud.parallaxMinDeg)\n'
              '              .length,\n'
              '          \'true_ms\': _trueParallaxComputeMs,\n'
              '          // 案②修复对数:容量淘汰累计(>0 = 摸到 16000 安全阀;淘汰序已\n'
              '          // 保证新区必胜,这里只留观测量)。\n'
              '          \'evicted\': _coverageCloud.evictedTotal,\n'
              '          \'captures\': _coverageCloud.capturesMarked,\n'
              '          \'fed\': _sfmFed,\n'
              '          \'queued\': _sfmQueued,\n'
              '          \'n_disconnected_frames\': disconnectedFrames,\n'
              '          \'n_segments\': segs.length,\n'
              '          // 区段摘要(封顶 8 段防刷行):[firstId,lastId,count]。\n'
              '          \'segments\': [\n'
              '            for (final s in segs.take(8)) [s.firstId, s.lastId, s.count],\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      } catch (_) {\n'
              '        // 遥测绝不伤害拍摄。\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _stopGuidanceTelemetry() {\n'
              '    _guidanceTelemetryTimer?.cancel();\n'
              '    _guidanceTelemetryTimer = null;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 把状态差量交给 native(AetherARKitPlugin `setPhotoCardStates`)。\n'
              '  Future<void> _pushPhotoCardStates(Map<String, int> diff) async {\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<void>(\n'
              '        \'setPhotoCardStates\',\n'
              '        <String, dynamic>{\'states\': diff},\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    } catch (_) {\n'
              '      // Display-only channel — never let it disturb capture.\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _markSfmStartFailure(String detail) {\n'
              '    DeviceLog.log(\'OfficialARCapturePage\', \'sfm: startup blocked: $detail\');\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    // 与 _noteSfmInternalFailure 同源:凡是翻 _sfmCaptureReady 的地方都要停\n'
              '    // 自动拍,否则它会对着一扇永远关着的门每 tick 撞一次。\n'
              '    _stopAutoCapture();\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _sfmStarting = false;\n'
              '      _sfmStartFailureText = \'点云重建未能启动（$detail）。请退出后重试；此次拍摄不会保存。\';\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Spawns the required streaming-SfM worker for this take and wires the\n'
              '  /// keyframe feed. Startup is fail-closed: unsupported devices, missing\n'
              '  /// capture storage, lease contention (reported as a null worker), and thrown\n'
              '  /// errors all leave a persistent page error with capture/save disabled.\n'
              '  Future<void> _startSfmLiveRecon(CaptureSession session) async {\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      if (_sfmRecon != null) {\n'
              '        if (mounted) setState(() => _sfmStarting = false);\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!SfmLiveRecon.isSupported) {\n'
              '        _markSfmStartFailure(\'当前设备不支持本地点云重建\');\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final captureDir = session.captureDir;\n'
              '      if (captureDir == null) {\n'
              '        _markSfmStartFailure(\'拍摄目录不可用\');\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final recon = await SfmLiveRecon.start(\n'
              '        dbPath: \'$captureDir/official_sfm_live.db\',\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (recon == null) {\n'
              '        _markSfmStartFailure(\'重建资源正被占用或启动失败\');\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (!mounted || !_recording) {\n'
              '        DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '          \'OfficialARCapturePage\',\n'
              '          \'sfm: page gone before worker up\',\n'
              '        );\n'
              '        unawaited(recon.dispose());\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      _sfmRecon = recon;\n'
              '      _sfmFeedSub = session.sfmFrameStream.listen(recon.offerFrame);\n'
              '      _sfmEventSub = recon.events.listen(_onSfmEvent);\n'
              '      _highResFailureSub ??= session.highResFailureStream.listen(\n'
              '        _onHighResCaptureFailure,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() => _sfmStarting = false); // enable controls + feed chip\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      DeviceLog.log(\'OfficialARCapturePage\', \'sfm: live recon wired\');\n'
              '    } catch (e, st) {\n'
              '      DeviceLog.log(\'OfficialARCapturePage\', \'sfm: start FAILED: $e\\n$st\');\n'
              '      _markSfmStartFailure(\'重建服务启动异常\');\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _onHighResCaptureFailure(OfficialHighResCaptureFailureEvent event) {\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    final message = switch (event.failure) {\n'
              '      OfficialHighResInputFailure.unexpectedDimensions =>\n'
              '        \'高分辨率照片不是 4032×3024，本张未进入重建，请重拍\',\n'
              '      OfficialHighResInputFailure.outOfSync => \'高分辨率照片与点击时刻不同步，本张未进入重建，请重拍\',\n'
              '      OfficialHighResInputFailure.missingPose ||\n'
              '      OfficialHighResInputFailure.missingIntrinsics =>\n'
              '        \'本张 ARKit 相机数据不完整，未进入重建，请重拍\',\n'
              '      OfficialHighResInputFailure.captureFailed ||\n'
              '      OfficialHighResInputFailure.missingJpeg => \'高分辨率照片拍摄失败，本张未进入重建，请重拍\',\n'
              '    };\n'
              '    _markPhotoCardFailed(event.evidenceJpegPath, message);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _markPhotoCardFailed(String evidenceJpegPath, String message) {\n'
              '    _failedEvidenceJpegPaths.add(evidenceJpegPath);\n'
              '    unawaited(\n'
              '      _arKitChannel\n'
              '          .invokeMethod<void>(\'removePhotoCard\', <String, dynamic>{\n'
              '            \'evidenceJpegPath\': evidenceJpegPath,\n'
              '          })\n'
              '          .catchError((Object _) {}),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '      ..hideCurrentSnackBar()\n'
              '      ..showSnackBar(\n'
              '        SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(message),\n'
              '          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,\n'
              '          duration: const Duration(seconds: 3),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 修1:推进等待页阶段(单调递增,重复/回退调用被忽略),重置该阶段的\n'
              '  /// 计秒起点。事件驱动,不轮询 worker。\n'
              '  void _advanceSfmStage(int stage) {\n'
              '    if (stage <= _sfmFinalizeStage) return;\n'
              '    _sfmFinalizeStage = stage;\n'
              '    _sfmStageStartMs = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;\n'
              '    if (mounted && _sfmPhase != null) setState(() {});\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 修1:等待页计秒 ticker。generating 期间每秒 setState 刷新"已 Xs";\n'
              '  /// 离开 generating 自停(refined/error/完成都会停)。\n'
              '  void _startSfmStageTicker() {\n'
              '    _sfmStageTicker?.cancel();\n'
              '    _sfmStageTicker = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (t) {\n'
              '      if (!mounted || _sfmPhase != SfmPreviewPhase.generating) {\n'
              '        t.cancel();\n'
              '        if (identical(_sfmStageTicker, t)) _sfmStageTicker = null;\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (_sfmFinalizeStage > 0 && _sfmQueued == 0) setState(() {});\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _stopSfmStageTicker() {\n'
              '    _sfmStageTicker?.cancel();\n'
              '    _sfmStageTicker = null;\n'
              '    _sfmFinalizeStage = 0;\n'
              '    _sfmStageStartMs = 0;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 修1:队列清空后的阶段文案(带该阶段已耗时)。阶段事件尚未到达时\n'
              '  /// 保底沿用旧文案,绝不显示空白。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// 阶段 2 细分(2026-07-11 案③顺带,46 号 155s 无子进度):native 的\n'
              '  /// finalize_segments 只在结束后落盘、worker 阶段 2 内无中途事件,所以\n'
              '  /// 这里做文案层轮换 —— enrich 补匹配与 stage1 全局 BA 本来就是并行跑\n'
              '  /// (finalize 三重优化定案),12s 轮换两句都是真话;真实子进度事件\n'
              '  /// 以后有了再接,不过度工程。已用时显示保留。\n'
              '  String _sfmStageProgressText(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final l = AppL10n.of(context);\n'
              '    if (_sfmFinalizeStage <= 0) return l.sfmQueueDrainedFinal;\n'
              '    final secs = _sfmStageStartMs > 0\n'
              '        ? ((DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch - _sfmStageStartMs) / 1000)\n'
              '              .floor()\n'
              '        : 0;\n'
              '    final elapsed = secs < 60\n'
              '        ? l.sfmElapsedSec(secs)\n'
              '        : l.sfmElapsedMinSec(secs ~/ 60, secs % 60);\n'
              '    return switch (_sfmFinalizeStage) {\n'
              '      1 => l.sfmStage1(elapsed),\n'
              '      2 => (secs ~/ 12).isEven ? l.sfmStage2a(elapsed) : l.sfmStage2b(elapsed),\n'
              '      3 => l.sfmStage3(elapsed),\n'
              '      _ => l.sfmStage4(elapsed),\n'
              '    };\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _onSfmEvent(SfmLiveEvent event) {\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
