              '    if (event is SfmLivePreview &&\n'
              '        (event.snapshot.summary[\'source\'] == \'streaming_global_ba\' ||\n'
              '            event.snapshot.summary[\'source\'] == \'streaming_local_ba_live\')) {\n'
              '      // [AR-EVERY-FRAME 2026-08-04] 两种拍摄期流式 source 都路由到 AR overlay\n'
              '      // 并 return:检查点的 \'streaming_global_ba\'(既有,~8次),以及每帧的\n'
              '      // \'streaming_local_ba_live\'(实验臂,默认关时永不发出)。**必须在此 return**,\n'
              '      // 否则会落进下方 colorize 路径,而那里 \'streaming_local_ba\'(注意无 _live)\n'
              '      // 被当作拍完的终态云会提前弹浮层。\'_live\' 后缀正是为避开该撞名。\n'
              '      final snapshot = event.snapshot;\n'
              '      final source = snapshot.summary[\'source\'] as String;\n'
              '      final receiveTag = _liveCloudTelemetry.receive(\n'
              '        source: source,\n'
              '        publishVersion:\n'
              '            (snapshot.summary[\'publish_version\'] as num?)?.toInt() ?? 0,\n'
              '        pointCount: snapshot.pointCount,\n'
              '        receiveEpochMs: DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch,\n'
              '        sourceReceiveSequence:\n'
              '            (snapshot.summary[\'diag_source_receive_seq\'] as num?)?.toInt(),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\n'
              '        \'live_cloud_receive_v1\',\n'
              '        receiveTag.baseFields,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (snapshot.posesPacked.isNotEmpty) {\n'
              '        _sfmLatestPoses = snapshot.posesPacked;\n'
              '        _refreshPhotoCardStates();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      unawaited(_publishOfficialSfmCloudToAr(snapshot, receiveTag));\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // 卡片边框连通性(黑→白/红):native 渲染,Flutter 无需 rebuild —\n'
              '    // 不进 setState,处理完直接返回。\n'
              '    if (event is SfmLiveConnectivity) {\n'
              '      _sfmLatestPoses = event.posesPacked;\n'
              '      _refreshPhotoCardStates();\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // Route B 真实三角化角到达:①帧级中位数合并进判黄真值表 ②体素级\n'
              '    // 真值注入覆盖云(压黄逻辑改用真值)→ 卡片与覆盖云都可能变色。\n'
              '    // 全部 native 哑渲染,无需 rebuild —— 不进 setState,处理完直接返回。\n'
              '    if (event is SfmLiveTrueParallax) {\n'
              '      final fp = event.framesPacked;\n'
              '      for (var i = 0; i + 1 < fp.length; i += 2) {\n'
              '        final fid = fp[i].toInt();\n'
              '        final deg = fp[i + 1];\n'
              '        _trueFrameParallaxDeg[fid] = deg;\n'
              '        // 白态粘性计数:只在真值采样到达处更新(连续 <4° 采样次数;\n'
              '        // ≥4° 清零)。_refreshPhotoCardStates 只读,不重复计数。\n'
              '        _frameBelowEnterStreak[fid] = frameBelowEnterStreak(\n'
              '          sampleDeg: deg,\n'
              '          prevStreak: _frameBelowEnterStreak[fid] ?? 0,\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      _coverageCloud.applyTrueParallax(event.voxelKeys, event.voxelDeg);\n'
              '      _trueParallaxComputeMs = event.computeMs;\n'
              '      _refreshPhotoCardStates();\n'
              '      _scheduleCoveragePush(); // 真值可能翻体素颜色 → 合并节流推送\n'
              '\n'
              '      // 补强1:真值刚注入 → starved 计数可能变化,顺路采样(去抖/滞回\n'
              '      // 在 gate 内;只在横幅翻转时才 setState,不破坏本分支"不 rebuild"\n'
              '      // 的克制)。\n'
              '      _sampleStarvedBanner();\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (event is SfmLiveFrameFed && event.result == \'ok\') {\n'
              '      // A frame landed: the worker is healthy again, so a past isolated throw\n'
              '      // must not accumulate toward the fault banner.\n'
              '      _sfmInternalFailureStreak = 0;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (event is SfmLiveFrameFed &&\n'
              '        event.result != \'ok\' &&\n'
              '        event.jpegPath != null) {\n'
              '      // [MONITORING 2026-07-26] Not every failure is a coverage problem.\n'
              '      //   errNotRegistered = COLMAP looked at the frame and declined to\n'
              '      //     register it. Upstream treats an unregistered image as a normal\n'
              '      //     outcome, and "shoot again near the red cards" is genuinely the\n'
              '      //     fix — keep the red disconnected state.\n'
              '      //   errInternal      = the native call THREW; the frame never entered\n'
              '      //     the reconstruction at all (frameId == -1). Reshooting cannot help\n'
              '      //     — it hits the same fault. Painting it red told the user to do\n'
              '      //     useless work AND disguised a real bug as a capture problem: one\n'
              '      //     device take lost 25 consecutive frames this way and it read as\n'
              '      //     "you didn\'t shoot well enough". Leave the card in its pending\n'
              '      //     state (black = SfM has not processed it, which is exactly true)\n'
              '      //     and surface the fault as a fault.\n'
              '      if (event.result == \'errInternal\') {\n'
              '        _noteSfmInternalFailure(event.result);\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        _markPhotoDisconnected(event.jpegPath!, event.result);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      switch (event) {\n'
              '        case SfmLiveConnectivity():\n'
              '        case SfmLiveTrueParallax():\n'
              '          break; // 已在上方早退处理(不触发 rebuild)\n'
              '        case SfmLiveFrameFed():\n'
              '          _sfmFed = _sfmRecon?.fedCount ?? _sfmFed;\n'
              '          _sfmQueued = _sfmRecon?.remainingCount ?? _sfmQueued;\n'
              '          // 拥塞遥测标签:队列深度刚变,重估标签(纯观测,已在 setState 内)。\n'
              '          _recomputeShutterPace(inSetState: true);\n'
              '          // 修1:等待页上队列刚排空 → finalize 即将/已经下发,进入\n'
              '          // 阶段 1(整理帧数据/phase1)。已在 setState 内,直接改字段。\n'
              '          if (_sfmPhase == SfmPreviewPhase.generating &&\n'
              '              _sfmQueued == 0 &&\n'
              '              _sfmFinalizeStage == 0) {\n'
              '            _sfmFinalizeStage = 1;\n'
              '            _sfmStageStartMs = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        case SfmLiveFrameQueued():\n'
              '          _sfmQueued = _sfmRecon?.remainingCount ?? _sfmQueued;\n'
              '          // 拥塞遥测标签:入队即重估(队列上行沿是标签的主要触发,纯观测)。\n'
              '          _recomputeShutterPace(inSetState: true);\n'
              '        case SfmLiveFinalizePhase1Done():\n'
              '          // 修1:phase1 完成 → 阶段 2(后台全局 BA,分钟级)。\n'
              '          if (_sfmPhase == SfmPreviewPhase.generating &&\n'
              '              _sfmFinalizeStage < 2) {\n'
              '            _sfmFinalizeStage = 2;\n'
              '            _sfmStageStartMs = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;\n'
              '            // 案④:灵动岛真实进度锚点 1 —— phase1 完成 = 10%。\n'
              '            unawaited(_pushReconProgress(0.10, \'全局优化中\'));\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        case SfmLivePreview():\n'
              '        case SfmLiveLocalReady():\n'
              '        case SfmLiveRefined():\n'
              '          // Display + phase advance are DEFERRED to the colorize pass below:\n'
              '          // the cloud is only shown once it\'s fully colored (geometry + true\n'
              '          // color together, no gray flash). The overlay keeps showing the\n'
              '          // generating spinner / the previous colored cloud until then. The\n'
              '          // streaming-preview cloud is now track-annotated, so it colorizes on\n'
              '          // the SAME path — it is the ONLY cloud shown (global BA deferred).\n'
              '          break;\n'
              '        case SfmLiveFailed(:final stage, :final message):\n'
              '          // During capture (overlay hidden) a per-frame failure is log-only;\n'
              '          // once the preview is up, a finalize/refine failure surfaces the\n'
              '          // non-blocking "已保留素材" state. But a REFINE failure after\n'
              '          // LOCAL_READY should reveal the perfectly usable colored LOCAL cloud\n'
              '          // we held back (deferred display), NOT an error.\n'
              '          if (_sfmPhase == SfmPreviewPhase.generating) {\n'
              '            final localFallback = _pendingLocalColored;\n'
              '            if (localFallback != null) {\n'
              '              _sfmSnapshot = localFallback;\n'
              '              _sfmPhase = SfmPreviewPhase.refined; // show the done chip + cloud\n'
              '              _pendingLocalColored = null;\n'
              '            } else {\n'
              '              _sfmPhase = SfmPreviewPhase.error;\n'
              '              _sfmErrorText = \'$stage: $message\';\n'
              '            }\n'
              '          }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    // A failure is terminal immediately. On success the umbrella stays alive\n'
              '    // through final colorization + PLY persistence and ends in\n'
              '    // [_colorizeSnapshot], just before the completion button appears.\n'
              '    switch (event) {\n'
              '      case SfmLiveFailed():\n'
              '        unawaited(_endReconUmbrella());\n'
              '      default:\n'
              '        break;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // Real-color pass: on-device extract_colors is off, so snapshots arrive\n'
              '    // colorless — sample the registered keyframes\' JPEGs. The COLORED result is\n'
              '    // what gets shown (see _colorizeSnapshot\'s tail) so geometry + color appear\n'
              '    // together. `_colorizeTarget` marks the newest pass so a stale one bails.\n'
              '    switch (event) {\n'
              '      case SfmLivePreview(:final snapshot):\n'
              '      case SfmLiveLocalReady(:final snapshot):\n'
              '      case SfmLiveRefined(:final snapshot):\n'
              '        // 卡片边框终态刷新:快照的 posesPacked 是注册真值(finalize 为\n'
              '        // COLMAP registered 位;流式 preview 为合成连通性),覆盖拍摄期\n'
              '        // 的实时判定。空 poses(异常路径)不回退已有状态。\n'
              '        if (snapshot.posesPacked.isNotEmpty) {\n'
              '          _sfmLatestPoses = snapshot.posesPacked;\n'
              '          _refreshPhotoCardStates();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        // 修1:finalize 快照到达 → 阶段 3(提取色彩)。拍摄期的流式\n'
              '        // preview(_sfmPhase == null)不进阶段流。\n'
              '        if (_sfmPhase == SfmPreviewPhase.generating) {\n'
              '          _advanceSfmStage(3);\n'
              '          // 案④:灵动岛真实进度锚点 2 —— RefineGlobalBA 全段完 = 75%\n'
              '          // (46 号 segments 实测:该段占总等待 96%,合成爬行在段内兜底)。\n'
              '          unawaited(_pushReconProgress(0.75, \'提取色彩中\'));\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        _colorizeTarget = snapshot;\n'
              '        unawaited(_colorizeSnapshot(snapshot));\n'
              '      default:\n'
              '        break;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  // [增量D 2026-07-28] 此处原挂着一段 BIT5/L1 仲裁重算的孤儿注释(所述\n'
              '  // 函数早已随 E25 停用删除)——注释一并清理,勿被其误导。\n'
              '  Future<void> _colorizeSnapshot(SfmLiveSnapshot snap) async {\n'
              '    final recon = _sfmRecon;\n'
              '    if (recon == null || snap.pointCount == 0) return;\n'
              '    final n = snap.pointCount;\n'
              '    final offs = snap.obsOffsets;\n'
              '    final fids = snap.obsFrameIds;\n'
              '    final oxy = snap.obsXY;\n'
              '    if (fids.isEmpty || offs.length != n + 1) return; // no track data\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Group observations by frame so every JPEG decodes exactly once.\n'
              '    // byFrame[frameId] = flat [pointIndex, kpX, kpY, ...] triples.\n'
              '    // obsCap 顺带统计每点有效观测数,作为样本池的预分配容量。\n'
              '    final byFrame = <int, List<double>>{};\n'
              '    final obsCap = Int32List(n);\n'
              '    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n'
              '      for (var j = offs[i]; j < offs[i + 1]; j++) {\n'
              '        final f = fids[j];\n'
              '        if (!recon.fedFrameMeta.containsKey(f)) continue;\n'
              '        obsCap[i]++;\n'
              '        (byFrame[f] ??= <double>[])\n'
              '          ..add(i.toDouble())\n'
              '          ..add(oxy[j * 2])\n'
              '          ..add(oxy[j * 2 + 1]);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (byFrame.isEmpty) return;\n'
              '\n'
              '    final sw = Stopwatch()..start();\n'
              '    // 代表色样本池:收集每点全部双线性观测样本,归约时选亮度中位的真实样本\n'
              '    // (不再算术平均——白床单混入个别红观测会被平均成粉,见\n'
              '    // representative_color.dart)。\n'
              '    final samples = RepresentativeColorSamples(obsCap);\n'
              '    // 取色解码 pipeline(colorize_pipeline.dart,07-12 提速两刀,输出逐位\n'
              '    // 一致,tool/colorize_parallel_check.dart 有串行对拍断言):\n'
              '    //   ① 按 jpegPath 去重共享解码(槽位重拍历史同文件只解一次);\n'
              '    //   ② 有界并行 3 + 单 consumer 严格按 byFrame 插入序采样(与旧逐帧\n'
              '    //      串行同序)。cap47 遥测:6.8s ≈ 121×56ms 串行解码受限 → 预期 ~2s。\n'
              '    // native 侧配套:colorizeQueue 已改并发队列(Dart 窗口=唯一 in-flight\n'
              '    // 上限,3×1280px RGB ≈ 11MB)。每帧解码仍走 native ImageIO downscale\n'
              '    // (1280px 长边,30-80ms;纯 Dart 全分辨率解码 1.5-4s 是"白点云"旧根因)。\n'
              '    // 取消哨兵与旧逐帧检查同语义:被新快照取代立刻停。\n'
              '    // NOTE: no !mounted bail here — even if the user tapped 完成 and the page\n'
              '    // popped, we finish + PERSIST so the draft PLY carries color.\n'
              '    final jobs = <ColorizeFrameJob>[];\n'
              '    for (final entry in byFrame.entries) {\n'
              '      final meta = recon.fedFrameMeta[entry.key]!;\n'
              '      jobs.add(\n'
              '        ColorizeFrameJob(\n'
              '          jpegPath: meta.jpegPath,\n'
              '          grayW: meta.grayW,\n'
              '          grayH: meta.grayH,\n'
              '          tri: entry.value,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [COLORIZE-PAR 2026-07-26, signed] 6→3 回退:par=6 在\n'
              '    // cap_1785078141726265 实测 14.4s,反而慢于 par=3 的 12.6s\n'
              '    // (cap_1785070530166049)——瓶颈在硬件解码器吞吐/热降频,3→6 无收益。\n'
              '    // par=3 与串行的逐位一致对拍见 tool/colorize_parallel_check.dart。\n'
              '    const colorizePar = 3;\n'
              '    final dstats = await sampleColorsPipelined(\n'
              '      jobs: jobs,\n'
              '      samples: samples,\n'
              '      decode: _decodeJpegNative,\n'
              '      maxInFlight: colorizePar,\n'
              '      isCancelled: () => !identical(_colorizeTarget, snap),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final decoded = dstats.framesSampled;\n'
              '    final decodeFail = dstats.decodeFail;\n'
              '    // 遥测【colorize】:每次真实 native 解码耗时(去重后 unique 次数;\n'
              '    // 中位数定位 ImageIO 慢帧/热降频)。\n'
              '    final decodeMsList = dstats.decodeMs;\n'
              '    if (!identical(_colorizeTarget, snap)) {\n'
              '      return; // superseded during decode/sampling\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final rgb = Uint8List(n * 3);\n'
              '    var colored = 0;\n'
              '    var gainMs = 0;\n'
              '    // [RS-CORRECT-COLORS 2026-08-14] 复刻 RealityScan 的 `Correct colors`:\n'
              '    // 先估每帧三通道增益(用已采到的样本,零额外解码),再在**校正后**的\n'
              '    // 亮度上选代表样本。不校正时"两个观测取更暗的那个"有 81% 的概率取到的\n'
              '    // 是"自动曝光收得更紧的那一帧"而非"没吃到高光的角度"(s4 实测),\n'
              '    // 相邻点因此各挑各的帧 ⇒ 整片云出斑块。\n'
              '    // 档位(official_env.json,Dart 直读;Platform.environment 读不到 setenv):\n'
              '    //   0/缺省 = 关,逐位等同旧实现;1 = 只用于选择;2 = 同时应用到输出(RS 忠实形态)\n'
              '    final ccMode = AetherEnvFile.intOf(\'OFFICIAL_AETHER_COLOR_CORRECT\', 0);\n'
              '    FrameColorGains? gains;\n'
              '    if (ccMode > 0 && jobs.isNotEmpty) {\n'
              '      final gsw = Stopwatch()..start();\n'
              '      gains = samples.estimateFrameGains(jobs.length);\n'
              '      gsw.stop();\n'
              '      gainMs = gsw.elapsedMilliseconds;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // [RS-MULTIBAND 2026-08-14] 复刻 RS 的 Multi-band 顶点上色:低频(颜色/\n'
              '    // 亮度)在邻域内线性融合 → 协调;高频(细节)仍来自单一真实观测 → 不糊。\n'
              '    // 只改 RGB,不增删/修补任何点(遵守"Dart 阶段不得 delete/repair/enrich"\n'
              '    // 的既有铁律)。默认关。\n'
              '    final mbMode = AetherEnvFile.intOf(\'OFFICIAL_AETHER_COLOR_MULTIBAND\', 0);\n'
              '    var mbMs = 0;\n'
              '    if (mbMode > 0) {\n'
              '      final msw = Stopwatch()..start();\n'
              '      final selF = Float32List(n * 3);\n'
              '      final linF = Float32List(n * 3);\n'
              '      for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n'
              '        if (!samples.selectIntoFloat(\n'
              '          i,\n'
              '          selF,\n'
              '          gains: gains,\n'
              '          applyToOutput: ccMode >= 2,\n'
              '        )) {\n'
              '          selF[i * 3] = 185;\n'
              '          selF[i * 3 + 1] = 185;\n'
              '          selF[i * 3 + 2] = 190;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (!samples.meanIntoFloat(i, linF, gains: gains)) {\n'
              '          linF[i * 3] = selF[i * 3];\n'
              '          linF[i * 3 + 1] = selF[i * 3 + 1];\n'
              '          linF[i * 3 + 2] = selF[i * 3 + 2];\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final blended = multiBandBlend(\n'
              '        xyz: snap.xyz,\n'
              '        selected: selF,\n'
              '        linear: linF,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      rgb.setAll(0, blended);\n'
              '      msw.stop();\n'
              '      mbMs = msw.elapsedMilliseconds;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final obsHist = List<int>.filled(5, 0);\n'
              '    final rmsList = <double>[];\n'
              '    var rmsGt40 = 0;\n'
              '    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {\n'
              '      // 代表色归约:选亮度中位的真实观测样本,不合成新颜色。\n'
              '      // multi-band 已写好 rgb 时,这里只补统计,不覆盖颜色。\n'
              '      if (mbMode > 0\n'
              '          ? samples.hitCount(i) > 0\n'
              '          : samples.selectInto(\n'
              '              i,\n'
              '              rgb,\n'
              '              gains: gains,\n'
              '              applyToOutput: ccMode >= 2,\n'
              '            )) {\n'
              '        colored++;\n'
              '        final hc = samples.hitCount(i);\n'
              '        obsHist[obsHistBucket(hc)]++;\n'
              '        if (hc >= 2) {\n'
              '          final rms = samples.rmsDeviation(\n'
              '            i,\n'
              '            rgb[i * 3],\n'
              '            rgb[i * 3 + 1],\n'
              '            rgb[i * 3 + 2],\n'
              '          );\n'
              '          rmsList.add(rms);\n'
              '          if (rms > 40) rmsGt40++;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        // Track frames unavailable (decode failed) — readable light gray.\n'
              '        rgb[i * 3] = 185;\n'
              '        rgb[i * 3 + 1] = 185;\n'
              '        rgb[i * 3 + 2] = 190;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    sw.stop();\n'
              '    DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '      \'Colorize\',\n'
              '      \'${snap.refined ? "refined" : "local"} done in ${sw.elapsedMilliseconds}ms: \'\n'
              '          \'colored $colored/$n (${(100 * colored / n).round()}%) | \'\n'
              '          \'frames decoded=$decoded fail=$decodeFail \'\n'
              '          \'unique=${dstats.uniqueDecodes} par=$colorizePar\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    // 遥测【colorize】一行汇总(排序两个小数组,~几 ms,等待页后台)。\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      decodeMsList.sort();\n'
              '      rmsList.sort();\n'
              '      double r1(double? v) => v == null ? 0 : (v * 10).round() / 10;\n'
              '      TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'colorize\', {\n'
              '        \'refined\': snap.refined,\n'
              '        \'total_ms\': sw.elapsedMilliseconds,\n'
              '        \'frames\': byFrame.length,\n'
              '        \'decoded\': decoded,\n'
              '        \'decode_fail\': decodeFail,\n'
              '        // [RS-CORRECT-COLORS] 档位与实测增益跨度;cc_mode=0 时后三项恒为\n'
              '        // 默认值,可据此确认"这一场没开校正"。\n'
              '        \'cc_mode\': ccMode,\n'
              '        \'cc_ms\': gainMs,\n'
              '        \'cc_gain_lo\': gains?.span[0],\n'
              '        \'cc_gain_hi\': gains?.span[1],\n'
              '        \'cc_banned\': gains?.bannedCount,\n'
              '        \'mb_mode\': mbMode,\n'
              '        \'mb_ms\': mbMs,\n'
              '        // 07-12 并行化新增:真实 native 解码次数(按 jpegPath 去重)与\n'
              '        // 并行窗口;frames-decode_unique = 去重省下的解码次数。\n'
              '        \'decode_unique\': dstats.uniqueDecodes,\n'
              '        \'decode_par\': 3,\n'
              '        \'decode_ms_p50\': r1(percentileSorted(decodeMsList, 0.50)),\n'
              '        \'decode_ms_p90\': r1(percentileSorted(decodeMsList, 0.90)),\n'
              '        \'decode_ms_max\': decodeMsList.isEmpty ? 0 : decodeMsList.last.round(),\n'
              '        // [E25] 实际解码尺寸 + 配置上限 —— 证明"全分辨率取色"是否真生效。\n'
              '        \'decode_w\': _colorizeDecodeW,\n'
              '        \'decode_h\': _colorizeDecodeH,\n'
              '        \'decode_max_px_cfg\': kColorizeDecodeMaxPx,\n'
              '        \'points\': n,\n'
              '        \'colored\': colored,\n'
              '        \'no_obs\': n - colored, // 无可用观测(解码失败/track 帧缺失)→ 灰点\n'
              '        \'obs_hist\': obsHist, // [1, 2, 3-4, 5-8, 9+]\n'
              '        \'var_n\': rmsList.length,\n'
              '        \'var_p50\': r1(percentileSorted(rmsList, 0.50)),\n'
              '        \'var_p90\': r1(percentileSorted(rmsList, 0.90)),\n'
              '        \'var_gt40\': rmsGt40, // 混色嫌疑点数(均方差 > 40 灰阶)\n'
              '      });\n'
              '    } catch (_) {}\n'
              '    // [E25-D 2026-07-20] L2 渲染门已删除 —— 交付即显示,不再计算/落盘\n'
              '    // ghost_view_mask.bin。原块(97 行)在此计算 band15∧¬rescued 可见性、\n'
              '    // 把 native ghost_mask.bin 重排到交付点序、并发 ghost_view_filter 遥测。\n'
              '    // The official endpoint snapshot is reused unchanged for BOTH persistence\n'
              '    // and display. Colorization may add RGB, but no Dart stage may delete,\n'
              '    // repair, or enrich a point after COLMAP\'s final BA/filtering.\n'
              '    final fsnap = SfmLiveSnapshot(\n'
              '      xyz: snap.xyz,\n'
              '      rgb: rgb,\n'
              '      posesPacked: snap.posesPacked,\n'
              '      summary: snap.summary,\n'
              '      refined: snap.refined,\n'
              '      obsOffsets: Int32List(0),\n'
              '      obsFrameIds: Int32List(0),\n'
              '      obsXY: Float32List(0),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    // PERSIST FIRST: the completion button must mean that the final colored PLY\n'
              '    // is actually on disk, not merely queued for a later asynchronous write.\n'
              '    // 修1:进入阶段 4(保存点云)。\n'
              '    if (_sfmPhase == SfmPreviewPhase.generating &&\n'
              '        identical(_colorizeTarget, snap)) {\n'
              '      _advanceSfmStage(4);\n'
              '      // 案④:灵动岛真实进度锚点 3 —— 取色完成、开始落盘 = 85%。\n'
              '      unawaited(_pushReconProgress(0.85, \'保存点云中\'));\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final captureDir = _session?.captureDir;\n'
              '    if (captureDir != null && identical(_colorizeTarget, snap)) {\n'
              '      final psw = Stopwatch()..start();\n'
              '      var persistOk = false;\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        await persistSparseSnapshot(\n'
              '          captureDir: captureDir,\n'
              '          snapshot: fsnap,\n'
              '          rgb: rgb,\n'
              '        );\n'
              '        persistOk = true;\n'
              '        // [2026-08-08 用户实机指认] "点云诞生出来的那一刻就删除封面照片然后立刻\n'
              '        // 替换成点云截图" —— 草稿卡片的封面在这里就画好,而不是等回到草稿页轮询\n'
              '        // 补图(那会让卡片先显示照片、几秒后肉眼跳变一下)。\n'
              '        //\n'
              '        // unawaited 是刻意的:上面那句注释说明了 persist 必须先完成才让"完成"\n'
              '        // 按钮出现,画封面不能再往这条路上加延迟。用户此刻还在预览页看结果,\n'
              '        // 等他点"完成"再走到草稿页,封面早就在盘上了。万一没赶上(立刻点完成),\n'
              '        // 草稿页的懒补图仍是兜底。\n'
              '        unawaited(\n'
              '          writeSparseThumbFrom(\n'
              '            captureDir: captureDir,\n'
              '            xyz: fsnap.xyz,\n'
              '            rgb: rgb,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '        // [E25-D 2026-07-20] 原在此把交付点序的 ghost_view_mask.bin 与 PLY\n'
              '        // 一起落盘(供草稿查看页对齐渲染门)。L2 已删,不再产该 sidecar。\n'
              '      } catch (e) {\n'
              '        DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '          \'OfficialARCapturePage\',\n'
              '          \'final sparse persist failed: $e\',\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      psw.stop();\n'
              '      // 遥测【persist】:PLY+meta 落盘耗时与字节数("完成"按钮的前置)。\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        int fileLen(String p) {\n'
              '          try {\n'
              '            return File(p).lengthSync();\n'
              '          } catch (_) {\n'
              '            return -1;\n'
              '          }\n'
              '        }\n'
              '\n'
              '        TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'persist\', {\n'
              '          \'ok\': persistOk,\n'
              '          \'ms\': psw.elapsedMilliseconds,\n'
              '          \'n_pts\': fsnap.pointCount,\n'
              '          \'refined\': fsnap.refined,\n'
              '          \'ply_bytes\': fileLen(\'$captureDir/official_sfm_sparse.ply\'),\n'
              '          \'meta_bytes\': fileLen(\'$captureDir/official_sfm_sparse_meta.json\'),\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      } catch (_) {}\n'
              '      // 案④:灵动岛真实进度锚点 4 —— PLY 已在盘上 = 95%\n'
              '      // (100% 仍只由 endReconUmbrella 置,语义 = "完成"按钮可见)。\n'
              '      if (persistOk) {\n'
              '        unawaited(_pushReconProgress(0.95, \'即将完成\'));\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // Display only if still mounted + current. THIS is where the cloud first\n'
              '    // becomes visible — fully colored — and the phase advances in lock-step, so\n'
              '    // the overlay shows the spinner until the colored cloud is ready (no gray).\n'
              '    if (mounted && identical(_colorizeTarget, snap)) {\n'
              '      // Show the orphan-FILTERED cloud (same buffers persisted above); it only\n'
              '      // reads xyz+rgb and the obs arrays were already dropped in fsnap. (mem audit)\n'
              '      final display = fsnap;\n'
              '      // Streaming local-BA is now the terminal finish-time cloud: it matches the\n'
              '      // older good captures\' delivery path and avoids a post-finish pure global\n'
              '      // BA wait/overwrite. `streaming_global_ba` remains accepted for old builds\n'
              '      // or explicit experiments. The resume/cold-finalize path still emits a\n'
              '      // noisier phase-1 `local_ready` that we defer for its `refined` follow-up.\n'
              '      final src = snap.summary[\'source\'];\n'
              '      final isStreamingPreview =\n'
              '          src == \'streaming_local_ba\' || src == \'streaming_global_ba\';\n'
              '      if (snap.refined || isStreamingPreview) {\n'
              '        // Reveal the clean terminal cloud (streaming preview, or REFINED phase-2).\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _sfmSnapshot = display;\n'
              '          _sfmPhase = SfmPreviewPhase.refined;\n'
              '          _pendingLocalColored = null;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        // Defer: do NOT show the noisier phase-1 (local) cloud — wait for the\n'
              '        // refined one. Hold it as the refine-failure fallback; the generating\n'
              '        // spinner ("实时重建") stays up as the finalize loading state.\n'
              '        _pendingLocalColored = display;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final isTerminalColorize =\n'
              '        snap.summary[\'terminal\'] == true ||\n'
              '        snap.summary[\'source\'] == \'streaming_global_ba\' ||\n'
              '        snap.refined;\n'
              '    if (isTerminalColorize && identical(_colorizeTarget, snap)) {\n'
              '      if (snap.refined) unawaited(_endReconUmbrella());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Fast native JPEG decode for colorization — ImageIO decode at\n'
              '  /// [kColorizeDecodeMaxPx] (= 全分辨率,见该常量的出处注释), raw sensor\n'
              '  /// orientation (no EXIF transform), 3 B/px top-down.\n'
              '  /// Returns null on any failure.\n'
              '  int _colorizeDecodeW = 0;\n'
              '  int _colorizeDecodeH = 0;\n'
              '  int _colorizeDecodeMaxArea = 0;\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<({Uint8List rgb, int w, int h})?> _decodeJpegNative(\n'
              '    String jpegPath,\n'
              '  ) async {\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final res = await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<Map<Object?, Object?>>(\n'
              '        \'decodeJpegForColor\',\n'
              '        {\'jpegPath\': jpegPath, \'maxPx\': kColorizeDecodeMaxPx},\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (res == null) return null;\n'
              '      final w = res[\'w\'] as int?, h = res[\'h\'] as int?;\n'
              '      // [E25 遥测] 记住实际解码尺寸 —— 上一轮改成全分辨率后拿不出任何证据\n'
              '      // 证明它生效(耗时几乎没变,因为 1280 本就不落 DCT 整除档、旧版也是\n'
              '      // 全解再缩)。把尺寸打进 colorize 事件,下次一读便知。\n'
              '      if (w != null && h != null && w * h > _colorizeDecodeMaxArea) {\n'
              '        _colorizeDecodeMaxArea = w * h;\n'
              '        _colorizeDecodeW = w;\n'
              '        _colorizeDecodeH = h;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final rgb = res[\'rgb\'] as Uint8List?;\n'
              '      if (w == null || h == null || rgb == null || w <= 0 || h <= 0) {\n'
              '        return null;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (rgb.length < w * h * 3) {\n'
              '        _noteColorizeDecodeFail(\'short_buffer\');\n'
              '        return null;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return (rgb: rgb, w: w, h: h);\n'
              '    } catch (e) {\n'
              '      // [E25 2026-07-20] 原本是 `catch (_) { return null; }` —— 把 native 抛的\n'
              '      // 错误码整个吞掉,结果 colorize 遥测只能报 decode_fail=N,**永远查不出\n'
              '      // 为什么**(2026-07-20 未命名7 出现 decode_fail=1,死因不可知)。\n'
              '      // native 侧的码是有意义的:ar_decode_failed=文件打不开(被删/未写完)、\n'
              '      // ar_decode_empty=尺寸为 0、ar_decode_bad_args=缺参数。记下来。\n'
              '      _noteColorizeDecodeFail(e is PlatformException ? e.code : \'exception\');\n'
              '      return null;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 取色解码失败原因计数,随 colorize 事件一起落遥测。\n'
              '  final Map<String, int> _colorizeDecodeFailReasons = <String, int>{};\n'
              '  void _noteColorizeDecodeFail(String code) {\n'
              '    _colorizeDecodeFailReasons[code] =\n'
              '        (_colorizeDecodeFailReasons[code] ?? 0) + 1;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Arm the iOS-26 continuation task only for an actual user-triggered finish.\n'
              '  /// The capture directory is also the native idempotency key, so rebuilds or\n'
              '  /// duplicate callbacks cannot create another Dynamic Island task.\n'
              '  Future<void> _beginReconUmbrella(String captureDir) async {\n'
              '    if (_reconUmbrellaJobID == captureDir) return;\n'
              '    _reconUmbrellaJobID = captureDir;\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<void>(\n'
              '        \'beginReconUmbrella\',\n'
              '        <String, Object?>{\'jobId\': captureDir},\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    } catch (_) {\n'
              '      if (_reconUmbrellaJobID == captureDir) {\n'
              '        _reconUmbrellaJobID = null;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Idempotent teardown after the final colored point cloud (or an error).\n'
              '  Future<void> _endReconUmbrella() async {\n'
              '    final jobID = _reconUmbrellaJobID;\n'
              '    if (jobID == null) return;\n'
              '    _reconUmbrellaJobID = null;\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<void>(\n'
              '        \'endReconUmbrella\',\n'
              '        <String, Object?>{\'jobId\': jobID},\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    } catch (_) {}\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 案④【灵动岛真实进度】:finalize 阶段边界把真实进度推给\n'
              '  /// ReconUmbrella(Swift 侧与合成爬行曲线取 max,严格单调不回退;\n'
              '  /// 100% 仍只由 endReconUmbrella 置)。锚点(46 号 segments 实测比例):\n'
              '  /// phase1 done→0.10 / refined(RefineGlobalBA 全段完)→0.75 /\n'
              '  /// colorize 完→0.85 / persist 落盘→0.95。阶段之间由 Swift 合成爬行\n'
              '  /// 兜底递增(iOS 30s 看门狗保险)。伞未武装时静默 no-op。\n'
              '  /// 遥测【island】:每次推送记 {p, stage}(t 由 TelemetryWriter 自加),\n'
              '  /// 下次对账"岛显示 vs 真实进度"不再靠代码+时间轴反推。\n'
              '  Future<void> _pushReconProgress(double fraction, String subtitle) async {\n'
              '    if (_reconUmbrellaJobID == null) return;\n'
              '    TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'island\', {\n'
              '      \'p\': fraction,\n'
              '      \'stage\': subtitle,\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<void>(\n'
              '        \'setReconProgress\',\n'
              '        <String, Object?>{\'fraction\': fraction, \'subtitle\': subtitle},\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    } catch (_) {\n'
              '      // Display-only channel — never let it disturb the finalize.\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// "完成" on the preview overlay: tear the worker down (frees the native\n'
              '  /// session + sqlite db) and run the exit the finish flow deferred.\n'
              '  Future<void> _releaseLiveReconstructionResources() async {\n'
              '    // The root FAB must not become enabled until dispose releases the\n'
              '    // process-wide reconstruction lease.\n'
              '    await _endReconUmbrella();\n'
              '    _stopSfmStageTicker();\n'
              '    final recon = _sfmRecon;\n'
              '    final feedSub = _sfmFeedSub;\n'
              '    final eventSub = _sfmEventSub;\n'
              '    final failureSub = _highResFailureSub;\n'
              '    _sfmRecon = null;\n'
              '    _sfmFeedSub = null;\n'
              '    _sfmEventSub = null;\n'
              '    _highResFailureSub = null;\n'
              '    await feedSub?.cancel();\n'
              '    await eventSub?.cancel();\n'
              '    await failureSub?.cancel();\n'
              '    if (recon != null) await recon.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _onSfmPreviewDone() async {\n'
              '    if (_sfmPhase != SfmPreviewPhase.refined &&\n'
              '        _sfmPhase != SfmPreviewPhase.error) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    await _routeReleaseGate.release(\n'
              '      releaseResources: _releaseLiveReconstructionResources,\n'
              '      revealRoot: () {\n'
              '        if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _sfmPhase = null;\n'
              '          _showDraftsWhileReconstructing = false;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '        if (_sfmPendingPop) {\n'
              '          _sfmPendingPop = false;\n'
              '          Navigator.of(context).pop(true);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      },\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// [选区 2026-07-27] 等待页"下一步"→ 选区页。返回 \'save_draft\'(签决:\n'
              '  /// 选区页返回不回等待页)→ 走与"保存草稿"完全同一的退出链路。\n'
              '  /// 预览相机(骰子经 ValueListenable 跟随,不触发整页重建)。\n'
              '  final ValueNotifier<CloudViewCamera?> _sfmPreviewCamera = ValueNotifier(null);\n'
              '  final CloudViewController _sfmCloudController = CloudViewController();\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// [2026-07-28 用户签决] "预览跟编辑就是一个页面":不再 push 选区页,\n'
              '  /// 点"下一步"只把工具层叠到同一个预览视图上,相机原地不动。\n'
              '  bool _sfmEditing = false;\n'
              '  SelectionBox? _sfmBox;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// [SEL-DISCARD 2026-07-30 用户签决] 退到草稿页时问"编辑记录是否保存"。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// [2026-08-03 修正] 拖框只更新内存预览；正式选区记录只在用户点"完成"\n'
              '  /// 后提交。取消或关闭放弃动作单都不能提前保存。基线回写仍保留，用于清理\n'
              '  /// 旧版本可能已经提前落盘的记录。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// 基线在**第一次修改之前**抓取(而不是进编辑态时),因为用户可能进出编辑态\n'
              '  /// 多次而一次都没动过框 —— 那种情况不该弹窗。null = 本次会话从未改过。\n'
              '  SelectionBox? _sfmBoxBaseline;\n'
              '  bool _sfmBoxBaselineWasAbsent = false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 底部"下一步":把这一份点云交给后续处理。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// 选区只在用户**真的**选过时才带上 —— 没选区就传 null 表示"处理整朵云"。\n'
              '  Future<void> _startDenseStage() async {\n'
              '    final dir = _session?.captureDir;\n'
              '    final snap = _sfmSnapshot;\n'
              '    if (dir == null || snap == null) return;\n'
              '    final r = await denseStageLauncher.start(\n'
              '      DenseStageRequest(\n'
              '        captureDir: dir,\n'
              '        sparsePlyPath: \'$dir/official_sfm_sparse.ply\',\n'
              '        pointCount: snap.pointCount,\n'
              '        selection: _sfmSelectionApplied ? _sfmBox : null,\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!mounted || r.status == DenseStageStatus.started) return;\n'
              '    final l = AppL10n.of(context);\n'
              '    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '      SnackBar(content: Text(r.message ?? l.denseStageUnavailable)),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _enterSfmEditing() async {\n'
              '    if (_sfmPhase != SfmPreviewPhase.refined) return;\n'
              '    final snap = _sfmSnapshot;\n'
              '    final dir = _session?.captureDir;\n'
              '    if (snap == null || dir == null || snap.pointCount == 0) return;\n'
              '    final fit = SparseCloudPainter.fitOf(snap.xyz);\n'
              '    final loaded = await SelectionBox.loadFrom(dir);\n'
              '    final sane =\n'
              '        loaded != null &&\n'
              '        loaded.isSaneFor(\n'
              '          fitCx: fit.cx,\n'
              '          fitCy: fit.cy,\n'
              '          fitCz: fit.cz,\n'
              '          fitRadius: fit.radius,\n'
              '        );\n'
              '    final frame = editingFrameOf(snap.xyz);\n'
              '    final box = sane\n'
              '        ? loaded\n'
              '        : SelectionBox.initialSquareFace(\n'
              '            cx: frame.center[0],\n'
              '            cy: frame.center[1],\n'
              '            cz: frame.center[2],\n'
              '            halfExtent: math.max(frame.hx, math.max(frame.hy, frame.hz)),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _sfmEditEntryBox = box;\n'
              '      _sfmEditEntryApplied = _sfmSelectionApplied;\n'
              '      _sfmSelectionApplied = sane;\n'
              '      _sfmBox = box;\n'
              '      _sfmEditing = true;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _onSfmBoxChanged(SelectionBox b) {\n'
              '    final previousApplied = _sfmSelectionApplied;\n'
              '    // [SEL-DISCARD] 第一次修改时抓基线(见 _sfmBoxBaseline 的注释:必须是\n'
              '    // "改之前"而不是"进编辑态时",否则进出而未改也会被判成脏)。\n'
              '    if (_sfmBoxBaseline == null && !_sfmBoxBaselineWasAbsent) {\n'
              '      final prev = _sfmBox;\n'
              '      if (prev != null && !prev.sameAs(b)) {\n'
              '        _sfmBoxBaseline = prev;\n'
              '        // 初次打开时用于显示的兜底框不是正式选区。必须把“盘上原本没有\n'
              '        // 选区”与框几何一起冻结，否则退页点“不保存”会反而写入兜底框。\n'
              '        _sfmBoxBaselineWasAbsent = !previousApplied;\n'
              '      } else if (prev == null) {\n'
              '        _sfmBoxBaselineWasAbsent = true;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // 用户动手改了框 ⇒ 从此这就是"他的选区",浏览态开始按它裁剪。\n'
              '    _sfmSelectionApplied = true;\n'
              '    setState(() => _sfmBox = b);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// "恢复原始框大小":按当前点云重算初始框。\n'
              '  void _resetSfmBoxSize() {\n'
              '    final snap = _sfmSnapshot;\n'
              '    if (snap == null) return;\n'
              '    final frame = editingFrameOf(snap.xyz);\n'
              '    _onSfmBoxChanged(\n'
              '      SelectionBox.initialSquareFace(\n'
              '        cx: frame.center[0],\n'
              '        cy: frame.center[1],\n'
              '        cz: frame.center[2],\n'
              '        halfExtent: math.max(frame.hx, math.max(frame.hy, frame.hz)),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 用户**真的**选过区吗 —— 没选过时预览呈现原始点云,不能拿按 AABB 算出来的\n'
              '  /// 兜底框去裁(initialFor 留边距,会悄悄切掉外圈的点)。\n'
              '  bool _sfmSelectionApplied = false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 进编辑态那一刻的框 —— "不保存"回滚到这里。\n'
              '  SelectionBox? _sfmEditEntryBox;\n'
              '  bool _sfmEditEntryApplied = false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 右上"完成":提交本次编辑,不问。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// [2026-07-30 用户签决"直接学苹果的相册"] 确认的负担只压在破坏性的那一侧。\n'
              '  Future<void> _exitSfmEditing() async {\n'
              '    final dir = _session?.captureDir;\n'
              '    final b = _sfmBox;\n'
              '    if (dir == null || b == null) return;\n'
              '    await _persistSfmBox(b, dir, applied: _sfmSelectionApplied);\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _sfmEditing = false;\n'
              '      _sfmBoxBaseline = null; // 已表态,下次修改重新抓基线\n'
              '      _sfmBoxBaselineWasAbsent = false;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 左上"取消":放弃本次编辑。没改过直接回浏览态;改过则弹苹果那张动作单,\n'
              '  /// 点其它地方消失并留在编辑页。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// "放弃"是**真回滚**；编辑期只改内存，入口状态写回同时兼容清理旧版本\n'
              '  /// 可能遗留的提前落盘记录。\n'
              '  Future<void> _cancelSfmEditing() async {\n'
              '    final dir = _session?.captureDir;\n'
              '    final entry = _sfmEditEntryBox;\n'
              '    final b = _sfmBox;\n'
              '    if (dir == null) return;\n'
              '    final dirty = entry != null && b != null && !b.sameAs(entry);\n'
              '\n'
              '    if (dirty) {\n'
              '      final confirmed = await showCupertinoModalPopup<bool>(\n'
              '        context: context,\n'
              '        builder: (ctx) => CupertinoActionSheet(\n'
              '          title: Text(AppL10n.of(ctx).selectionDiscardTitle),\n'
              '          actions: [\n'
              '            CupertinoActionSheetAction(\n'
              '              isDestructiveAction: true,\n'
              '              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(true),\n'
              '              child: Text(AppL10n.of(ctx).selectionDiscardConfirm),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (confirmed != true) return; // 点了动作单以外的地方 ⇒ 留在编辑页\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final finalBox = entry ?? b;\n'
              '    final finalApplied = entry != null\n'
              '        ? _sfmEditEntryApplied\n'
              '        : _sfmSelectionApplied;\n'
              '    if (finalBox != null) {\n'
              '      await _persistSfmBox(finalBox, dir, applied: finalApplied);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _sfmEditing = false;\n'
              '      if (finalBox != null) _sfmBox = finalBox;\n'
              '      _sfmSelectionApplied = finalApplied;\n'
              '      _sfmBoxBaseline = null;\n'
              '      _sfmBoxBaselineWasAbsent = false;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 落盘;[applied] 为假表示"用户没有选区",此时删掉文件而不是留一个兜底的\n'
              '  /// 全域框冒充选区。\n'
              '  Future<void> _persistSfmBox(\n'
              '    SelectionBox box,\n'
              '    String dir, {\n'
              '    required bool applied,\n'
              '  }) async {\n'
              '    if (applied) {\n'
              '      await box.saveTo(dir);\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final f = File(\'$dir/$kSelectionBoxFileName\');\n'
              '      if (f.existsSync()) await f.delete();\n'
              '    } catch (_) {}\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// [SEL-DISCARD 2026-07-30 用户签决] 退到草稿页前问"编辑记录是否保存"。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// 只在本次会话真的改过框时才问 —— 进出编辑态而没动过框不该被打断。\n'
              '  /// "不保存"回滚到基线并写盘；显式回写兼容旧版本可能留下的提前落盘记录。\n'
              '  /// 返回 true = 可以离开。\n'
              '  Future<bool> _confirmLeaveWithSelectionEdits() async {\n'
              '    final baseline = _sfmBoxBaseline;\n'
              '    if (baseline == null) return true; // 没改过 → 直接走\n'
              '    final l = AppL10n.of(context);\n'
              '    final choice = await showDialog<String>(\n'
              '      context: context,\n'
              '      barrierDismissible: false,\n'
              '      builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(\n'
              '        title: Text(l.sfmSelectionSaveTitle),\n'
              '        content: Text(l.sfmSelectionSaveBody),\n'
              '        actions: [\n'
              '          TextButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(\'cancel\'),\n'
              '            child: Text(l.sfmSelectionSaveCancel),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          TextButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(\'discard\'),\n'
              '            child: Text(l.sfmSelectionSaveDiscard),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          TextButton(\n'
              '            onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(\'keep\'),\n'
              '            child: Text(l.sfmSelectionSaveKeep),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (choice == null || choice == \'cancel\') return false;\n'
              '    final dir = _session?.captureDir;\n'
              '    if (choice == \'discard\') {\n'
              '      // 回滚到编辑前的正式状态。若当时盘上没有选区，删除旧版本可能提前\n'
              '      // 写入的记录；不能把仅用于显示的兜底框冒充成用户保存的选区。\n'
              '      if (dir != null) {\n'
              '        await _persistSfmBox(baseline, dir, applied: !_sfmBoxBaselineWasAbsent);\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _sfmBox = baseline;\n'
              '          _sfmSelectionApplied = !_sfmBoxBaselineWasAbsent;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } else if (dir != null && _sfmBox != null) {\n'
              '      await _persistSfmBox(_sfmBox!, dir, applied: _sfmSelectionApplied);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _sfmBoxBaseline = null; // 本次编辑已裁决,下次修改重新抓基线\n'
              '    _sfmBoxBaselineWasAbsent = false;\n'
              '    TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'selection_leave\', {\n'
              '      \'choice\': choice,\n'
              '      \'was_editing\': _sfmEditing,\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    return true;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 返回草稿页的统一入口 —— 先过选区保存裁决,再让草稿层显现。\n'
              '  Future<void> _onSfmPreviewBack() async {\n'
              '    if (!await _confirmLeaveWithSelectionEdits()) return;\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    _showDraftsDuringReconstruction();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _permanentlyDeleteActiveReconstruction(ScanRecord record) async {\n'
              '    if (!recordOwnsActiveReconstruction(\n'
              '      recordCaptureDir: record.captureDir,\n'
              '      recordPipelineKind: record.pipelineKind,\n'
              '      activeCaptureDir: _session?.captureDir,\n'
              '      activePipelineKind: CapturePipelineKind.official,\n'
              '    )) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final released = await _routeReleaseGate.release(\n'
              '      releaseResources: () async {\n'
              '        await _releaseLiveReconstructionResources();\n'
              '        await _coverageFeedSub?.cancel();\n'
              '        _coverageFeedSub = null;\n'
              '        final session = _session;\n'
              '        _session = null;\n'
              '        await session?.dispose();\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      revealRoot: () {},\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (!released) return;\n'
              '\n'
              '    await ScanRecordStore.instance.delete(record.id);\n'
              '    if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop(true);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Reveal Drafts without disposing the capture route or touching SfM.\n'
              '  void _showDraftsDuringReconstruction() {\n'
              '    if (_sfmPhase == null || _showDraftsWhileReconstructing) return;\n'
              '    setState(() => _showDraftsWhileReconstructing = true);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Called only by the matching active draft card.\n'
              '  void _showReconstructionProgress() {\n'
              '    if (_sfmPhase == null || !_showDraftsWhileReconstructing) return;\n'
              '    setState(() => _showDraftsWhileReconstructing = false);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _scheduleDraftTerminalExitIfNeeded() {\n'
              '    final terminal =\n'
              '        _sfmPhase == SfmPreviewPhase.refined ||\n'
              '        _sfmPhase == SfmPreviewPhase.error;\n'
              '    if (_draftTerminalExitScheduled ||\n'
              '        !shouldAutoExitReconstructionDrafts(\n'
              '          showingDrafts: _showDraftsWhileReconstructing,\n'
              '          reconstructionTerminal: terminal,\n'
              '        )) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _draftTerminalExitScheduled = true;\n'
              '    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {\n'
              '      _draftTerminalExitScheduled = false;\n'
              '      if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '      final stillTerminal =\n'
              '          _sfmPhase == SfmPreviewPhase.refined ||\n'
              '          _sfmPhase == SfmPreviewPhase.error;\n'
              '      if (!shouldAutoExitReconstructionDrafts(\n'
              '        showingDrafts: _showDraftsWhileReconstructing,\n'
              '        reconstructionTerminal: stillTerminal,\n'
              '      )) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      _sfmPendingPop = true;\n'
              '      unawaited(_onSfmPreviewDone());\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  // ─── 自动采集接线 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
              '  // 只有接线。任何"要不要拍"的判断都在 auto_capture_governor.dart。\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 自动拍看到的"已拍张数"。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// 必须与队列的 admission 口径**逐字一致**:ManualCaptureQueue 收人的条件\n'
              '  /// 是 `verifiedCount + outstandingCount < 300`(manual_capture_queue.dart),\n'
              '  /// 而 governor 停在 `capturedCount >= 300`。只报 `_projectPhotos.count`\n'
              '  /// 会少算在途票(一秒一张时常有 1–3 张),两边就永远对不上 —— controller\n'
              '  /// 等不到那个该让它停的数,只会每帧撞一次已经关上的门。\n'
              '  /// 与 [_ManualCaptureBar] 里 officialCaptureCanShoot 用的是同一个表达式。\n'
              '  int _autoCaptureAcceptedFrameCount() =>\n'
              '      _projectPhotos.count + _shutterQueue.outstandingCount;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 能不能起跑。前三条与手动快门的置灰判据同源(自动拍走的就是同一条入队\n'
              '  /// 路径,它不该在手动快门已被判死时还能开火);第四条是"AR 会话在,pose\n'
              '  /// 确实在流"—— 退后台时我们已经把自动拍停掉了,所以这一条足够。\n'
              '  bool get _autoCaptureCanStart => autoCaptureCanStart(\n'
              '    captureReady: _sfmCaptureReady,\n'
              '    queueAccepting: _shutterQueue.accepting,\n'
              '    withinFrameBudget: officialCaptureCanShoot(\n'
              '      acceptedFrameCount: _autoCaptureAcceptedFrameCount(),\n'
              '    ),\n'
              '    posesFlowing: _session != null,\n'
              '  );\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 把一帧 pose 喂给 controller,并把结果映射成 UI 状态。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// **判定一行都不在这里** —— 全在 AutoCaptureController / Governor。\n'
              '  void _driveAutoCapture(ARPose pose) {\n'
              '    if (_captureMode != OfficialCaptureMode.auto) {\n'
              '      // 模式已经切走,挂起的起跑作废 —— 否则切回自动时会莫名其妙自己开拍。\n'
              '      _autoStartPending = false;\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (_autoStartPending) {\n'
              '      _autoStartPending = false;\n'
              '      // 用**这一帧**起跑,不用缓存的"最近一帧":见 _autoStartPending 的注释。\n'
              '      _startAutoCapture(pose);\n'
              '      return; // 起跑帧只播种,不判定。\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!_autoCapture.isRunning) return;\n'
              '    // 开火钩子在 onPose **内部**同步跑完,并且只在**真的入队成功**时把这个\n'
              '    // 令牌 +1(见 _onAutoCaptureFire)。所以前后一比就知道这一帧到底落没落。\n'
              '    final pulseBefore = _autoFirePulseToken;\n'
              '    final decision = _autoCapture.onPose(pose);\n'
              '    // 遥测【auto_capture】:**每个**判定都记(spec §11)。\n'
              '    //\n'
              '    // ⚠️ 位置必须在下面那条提前 return **之前**:稳定态判定\n'
              '    // (skipPaced / skipNotMoved)占绝大多数,而它们正好全都走那条 return\n'
              '    // ——记在后面等于一条都采不到,偏偏 skipNotMoved 的占比正是这套遥测\n'
              '    // 存在的理由(spec §9 差异1:视差下限到底有没有用)。\n'
              '    //\n'
              '    // 时钟用 pose.timestamp(ARFrame 时间轴,与 controller 同一条);\n'
              '    // 档位用 _shutterPace —— 与 controller 的 paceProvider **同一个字段**,\n'
              '    // 换个来源就会与 governor 实际用的 tick 间隔对不上。\n'
              '    _autoTelemetry.recordDecision(\n'
              '      decision,\n'
              '      tSec: pose.timestamp,\n'
              '      pace: _shutterPace,\n'
              '      // 与 controller 的 thermalStateProvider **同一个字段** —— fire_before_tick\n'
              '      // 用的间隔必须与 governor 实际用的逐位相同,否则热机时会算漏。\n'
              '      thermalState: _lastThermalState,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    // isRunning 由 true 翻 false = controller 自停(撞 300 张或 5 分钟)。\n'
              '    // 这里读的是 isRunning 而不是 decision:停机后 onPose 恒返回\n'
              '    // skipNotMoved,与"你还没动够"逐字相同(见 autoCaptureIndicatorFor)。\n'
              '    final running = _autoCapture.isRunning;\n'
              '    if (running) {\n'
              '      _emitAutoTelemetry(_autoTelemetry.snapshotIfDue(pose.timestamp));\n'
              '    } else {\n'
              '      // 自停这条路**不经过** _stopAutoCapture(它开头就 `if (!isRunning)\n'
              '      // return;`)。不在这里收口,恰恰是最该被记下来的那两种收场\n'
              '      //(撞 300 张 / 撞 5 分钟上限)一行都写不出来。\n'
              '      _emitAutoTelemetry(_autoTelemetry.recordSessionEnd());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // ⚠️ 判据是「令牌变了 = **真的落了一帧**」,不是 `decision == fire`\n'
              '    // 〔2026-08-19 评审改正〕。两处理由:\n'
              '    //   ① 开火 ≠ 拍成(spec §7,遥测层正是为此把 fire_enqueued /\n'
              '    //      fire_enqueue_failed 分开记);拿 fire 当"落帧"会在入队失败时\n'
              '    //      给用户一个**假的正反馈** —— 红键脉冲一下、N/300 一动不动,\n'
              '    //      而自动模式下那颗红键的脉冲是"到底拍上没有"的唯一反馈。\n'
              '    //   ② `fired == true` 会跳过这条短路。入队持续失败时(SfM 内部故障)\n'
              '    //      判定会连着好几帧是 fire,于是这个 4800 行的页面被每帧重建一次\n'
              '    //      —— 正是这段注释自己要避免的那个热源。\n'
              '    final landed = _autoFirePulseToken != pulseBefore;\n'
              '    if (!landed &&\n'
              '        decision == _lastAutoDecision &&\n'
              '        running == _autoRunningLastSeen) {\n'
              '      // pose 流是 20–60 Hz。没有任何变化时不重建整页 —— 每帧 setState\n'
              '      // 会把这个 4800 行的页面变成一个热源。\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _lastAutoDecision = decision;\n'
              '    _autoRunningLastSeen = running;\n'
              '    if (mounted) setState(() {});\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 自动采集遥测的**唯一**落盘出口:走既有的 TelemetryWriter →\n'
              '  /// App 容器 `Documents/telemetry_official_dart.jsonl`,与 frame /\n'
              '  /// queue_drain / shutter_pace 同一个文件,真机拔线跑完 `devicectl copy`\n'
              '  /// 一次拉走。**不另起遥测通道**(多一条出口就多一处会漏采的地方),\n'
              '  /// 也不 print —— print 只到 stdout,拔线测试后根本取不回来。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// [snap] 为 null 意为"这一刻没有该写的行"(没到 5 秒节流点,或会话\n'
              '  /// 根本没开着)—— 节流与幂等都收在 AutoCaptureTelemetry 里,这里只负责写。\n'
              '  void _emitAutoTelemetry(Map<String, Object>? snap) {\n'
              '    if (snap == null) return;\n'
              '    TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'auto_capture\', snap);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _startAutoCapture(ARPose seed) {\n'
              '    if (_autoCapture.isRunning) return;\n'
              '    // 遥测起点取**起跑那一帧**的 ARFrame 时间戳(与 controller.start(seed)\n'
              '    // 收到的是同一个 pose)。本页别处用的 DateTime.now() 是另一个纪元,\n'
              '    // 混进来什么都不会抛,只会把时长与节流一起静默算错。\n'
              '    _autoTelemetry.recordSessionStart(seed.timestamp);\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _lastAutoDecision = AutoCaptureDecision.skipNotMoved;\n'
              '      _autoCapture.start(seed);\n'
              '      _autoRunningLastSeen = _autoCapture.isRunning;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _stopAutoCapture() {\n'
              '    _autoStartPending = false;\n'
              '    if (!_autoCapture.isRunning) return;\n'
              '    _autoCapture.stop();\n'
              '    // 用户停 / 切模式 / 退后台 / 完成 —— 这一轮到此为止,写终态行。\n'
              '    _emitAutoTelemetry(_autoTelemetry.recordSessionEnd());\n'
              '    _autoRunningLastSeen = false;\n'
              '    if (mounted) setState(() {});\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _setCaptureMode(OfficialCaptureMode mode) {\n'
              '    if (_captureMode == mode) return;\n'
              '    // 切走自动 ⇒ 自动拍立即停。**已拍帧全部保留**、队列继续消化\n'
              '    // (spec §7 第一条:切模式是 UI 行为,不该动数据)。\n'
              '    if (mode != OfficialCaptureMode.auto) _stopAutoCapture();\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _captureMode = mode;\n'
              '      // 切到自动**不开拍**(spec §7 / §8.1),只浮一条提示说明模式变了。\n'
              '      if (mode == OfficialCaptureMode.auto) _autoModeToastToken++;\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _toggleAutoRun() {\n'
              '    if (_autoStartPending) {\n'
              '      // 起跑还没落到帧上,再点一下就是取消。\n'
              '      setState(() => _autoStartPending = false);\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (_autoCapture.isRunning) {\n'
              '      _stopAutoCapture();\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!_autoCaptureCanStart) return;\n'
              '    // 这里**不**直接 start():起跑帧必须是 pose 回调里的那一帧本身,\n'
              '    // 见 _autoStartPending 的注释。代价至多一帧(17–50 ms)。\n'
              '    setState(() => _autoStartPending = true);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 自动拍的触发口。**返回 true = 真的入队成功** —— controller 据此决定\n'
              '  /// 要不要把基准帧推到这一帧上。报假的 true 会把基准帧钉在一个**根本没有\n'
              '  /// 照片**的位置上,此后位移闸系统性欠触发,正是 T3 要防的那件事。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// 与 [_onShutterTap] 的唯一区别:到 300 张时**不弹对话框** —— 自动模式\n'
              '  /// 每个 tick 撞一次,弹窗会刷屏。到顶由 controller 自停(它的\n'
              '  /// capturedCountProvider 与队列同口径,见 [_autoCaptureAcceptedFrameCount])。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// **契约:绝不抛。** 异常穿出去会打断整条 pose 回调(覆盖云、预警横幅、\n'
              '  /// 暖机判定都挂在上面)。入队路径里有平台通道与磁盘工作,不能假设它永远\n'
              '  /// 干净,所以一律按"没入队"处理 —— 基准帧因此不动,下一 tick 自然重试。\n'
              '  bool _onAutoCaptureFire() {\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final enqueued = _enqueueShutterCapture();\n'
              '      // spec §7「入队失败 ⇒ 基准帧不更新 + **记遥测**」。这里是全链路唯一\n'
              '      // 拿得到真实入队结果的地方 —— 判定层只知道"开了一枪"。\n'
              '      _autoTelemetry.recordFireOutcome(enqueued: enqueued);\n'
              '      // spec §8「**落帧**时 → 指示器脉冲一次」。脉冲与 fire_enqueued 在\n'
              '      // **同一处**记账,屏幕与遥测因此不可能说两套话\n'
              '      //〔2026-08-19 评审改正:此前脉冲挂在 `decision == fire` 上,\n'
              '      // 入队失败也照样脉冲〕。\n'
              '      if (enqueued) _autoFirePulseToken++;\n'
              '      return enqueued;\n'
              '    } catch (e) {\n'
              '      _autoTelemetry.recordFireOutcome(enqueued: false);\n'
              '      DeviceLog.log(\'OfficialARCapturePage\', \'auto capture enqueue failed: $e\');\n'
              '      return false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// O(1) UI admission only. Camera, JPEG, disk, and SfM work are serialized\n'
              '  /// by [_shutterQueue] after this callback has already returned.\n'
              '  void _onShutterTap() {\n'
              '    if (_admitShutterCapture() == _ShutterAdmission.budgetExhausted) {\n'
              '      unawaited(_showMaximumPhotosDialog());\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 快门的**唯一**入队路径:手动 tap 与自动拍都走这里。\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// 三道守卫与 300 张上限判据因此只有一份。给自动拍抄第二份守卫迟早会漏掉\n'
              '  /// 其中一条 —— 尤其是 `_shutterQueue.accepting`,它只在收尾流程\n'
              '  /// (freezeAndDrain / cancelPending)期间为 false,平时测不出来。\n'
              '  _ShutterAdmission _admitShutterCapture() {\n'
              '    if (_session == null || !_sfmCaptureReady || !_shutterQueue.accepting) {\n'
              '      return _ShutterAdmission.blocked;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return _shutterQueue.enqueue(verifiedCount: _projectPhotos.count) == null\n'
              '        ? _ShutterAdmission.budgetExhausted\n'
              '        : _ShutterAdmission.admitted;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// [_admitShutterCapture] 的布尔视图,给 [AutoCaptureController.onFire]。\n'
              '  bool _enqueueShutterCapture() =>\n'
              '      _admitShutterCapture() == _ShutterAdmission.admitted;\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _showMaximumPhotosDialog() async {\n'
              '    if (!mounted || _maximumPhotosDialogOpen) return;\n'
              '    _maximumPhotosDialogOpen = true;\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await showDialog<void>(\n'
              '        context: context,\n'
              '        builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(\n'
              '          key: const ValueKey<String>(\'official-maximum-photos-dialog\'),\n'
              '          title: const Text(\'已达 $kOfficialMaximumCaptureFrames 张上限\'),\n'
              '          content: const Text(\n'
              '            \'单次任务最多拍摄 $kOfficialMaximumCaptureFrames 张照片。\\n\'\n'
              '            \'点击右下角箭头结束拍摄并开始重建。\',\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          actions: [\n'
              '            FilledButton(\n'
              '              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),\n'
              '              child: const Text(\'好\'),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    } finally {\n'
              '      _maximumPhotosDialogOpen = false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Serial executor for one admitted shutter ticket. This preserves the\n'
              '  /// production native transaction and canonical 4032x3024 on-disk JPEG.\n'
              '  Future<void> _executeShutterTicket(ManualCaptureTicket ticket) async {\n'
              '    final session = _session;\n'
              '    if (session == null) {\n'
              '      throw StateError(\'accepted shutter ticket has no capture session\');\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final queueWaitMicros =\n'
              '        DateTime.now().microsecondsSinceEpoch - ticket.tapTimestampMicros;\n'
              '    final tapMs = ticket.tapTimestampMicros ~/ 1000;\n'
              '    final gapMs = _lastShutterMs > 0 ? tapMs - _lastShutterMs : -1;\n'
              '    _lastShutterMs = tapMs;\n'
              '    final shutterSw = Stopwatch()..start();\n'
              '    final capture = await session.captureSinglePhoto();\n'
              '    if (capture == null) {\n'
              '      throw StateError(\'accepted shutter ticket could not start\');\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '      \'OfficialARCapturePage\',\n'
              '      \'shutter ticket=${ticket.id} queue_wait_us=$queueWaitMicros \'\n'
              '          \'capture_start_wait_ms=${shutterSw.elapsedMilliseconds} \'\n'
              '          \'sfmPhase=$_sfmPhase\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'shutter_admit\', {\n'
              '      \'ticket_id\': ticket.id,\n'
              '      \'tap_timestamp_us\': ticket.tapTimestampMicros,\n'
              '      \'queue_wait_us\': queueWaitMicros,\n'
              '      \'verified_at_start\': _projectPhotos.count,\n'
              '      \'outstanding_at_start\': _shutterQueue.outstandingCount,\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    if (mounted &&\n'
              '        !_failedEvidenceJpegPaths.contains(capture.evidenceJpegPath)) {\n'
              '      unawaited(\n'
              '        _arKitChannel\n'
              '            .invokeMethod<void>(\'addPhotoCard\', <String, dynamic>{\n'
              '              \'textureJpegPath\': capture.previewJpegPath,\n'
              '              \'evidenceJpegPath\': capture.evidenceJpegPath,\n'
              '            })\n'
              '            .catchError((Object e) {\n'
              '              // ignore: avoid_print\n'
              '              print(\'[OfficialARCapturePage] addPhotoCard failed: $e\');\n'
              '            }),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final input = await capture.highResolutionCompletion;\n'
              '    _recomputeShutterPace();\n'
              '    TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'shutter\', {\n'
              '      \'ticket_id\': ticket.id,\n'
              '      \'tap_timestamp_us\': ticket.tapTimestampMicros,\n'
              '      \'queue_wait_us\': queueWaitMicros,\n'
              '      \'wait_ms\': shutterSw.elapsedMilliseconds,\n'
              '      \'gap_ms\': gapMs,\n'
              '      \'transaction_ms\': shutterSw.elapsedMilliseconds,\n'
              '      \'capture_timestamp\': input.captureTimestamp,\n'
              '      \'phase\': _sfmPhase?.name,\n'
              '      \'jpeg\': capture.evidenceJpegPath.split(\'/\').last,\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _onShutterTicketError(\n'
              '    ManualCaptureTicket ticket,\n'
              '    Object error,\n'
              '    StackTrace stackTrace,\n'
              '  ) {\n'
              '    DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '      \'OfficialARCapturePage\',\n'
              '      \'shutter ticket=${ticket.id} FAILED: $error\\n$stackTrace\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'shutter_error\', {\n'
              '      \'ticket_id\': ticket.id,\n'
              '      \'tap_timestamp_us\': ticket.tapTimestampMicros,\n'
              '      \'error\': \'$error\',\n'
              '    });\n'
              '    if (_finishTapInProgress) {\n'
              '      _finishDrainFailed = true;\n'
              '      _shutterQueue.cancelPending();\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (!mounted || _discardingCapture) return;\n'
              '    setState(() {\n'
              '      _captureQueueFailureText =\n'
              '          \'有一张高分辨率照片未完成（任务 ${ticket.id}）。\'\n'
              '          \'已继续处理后续拍摄；你可以继续拍摄或退出重试。\';\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Open the full-screen, time-ordered photo album.\n'
              '  void _openAlbum() {\n'
              '    Navigator.of(context).push(\n'
              '      MaterialPageRoute<void>(\n'
              '        builder: (_) => ARAlbumPage(\n'
              '          projectPhotos: _projectPhotos,\n'
              '          onDelete: _deleteProjectPhoto,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 补强2:完成按钮的前置把关(真值口径,与横幅同一 starved 计数)。\n'
              '  /// **占比口径**:starved_true / true_vox > [kParallaxStarvedFinishRatio]\n'
              '  /// (40%)才弹(starvedFinishGateShouldPrompt;绝对数 >20 已废——大\n'
              '  /// 场景体素基数大必超,每次完成必弹;true_vox==0 真值未到达不拦)。\n'
              '  /// 文案定性 + 教动作,不报体素数(抄 RS:数字吓人且不可执行)。\n'
              '  /// 非破坏性确认门:【继续拍摄】关弹窗回拍摄(什么都不发生),\n'
              '  /// 【仍要完成】走原 [_finalizeRecording] 流程 —— 原逻辑一个字不改,\n'
              '  /// 弹窗只是前置门。每次点完成都记一行 finish_gate 遥测\n'
              '  /// (starved/true_vox + 用户选择;未触发门 = pass)。\n'
              '  Future<void> _onFinishTap() async {\n'
              '    if (!_sfmCaptureReady ||\n'
              '        _finalizingRecording ||\n'
              '        _finishTapInProgress ||\n'
              '        _closeTapInProgress ||\n'
              '        _discardingCapture) {\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _finishCancellationRequested = false;\n'
              '    _finishDrainFailed = false;\n'
              '    // 收尾第一件事就是停自动拍:下面 freezeAndDrain 之后队列不再收人,\n'
              '    // 自动拍会每个 tick 撞一次关着的门(还撞不出任何反馈)。\n'
              '    _stopAutoCapture();\n'
              '    setState(() => _finishTapInProgress = true);\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await _shutterQueue.freezeAndDrain();\n'
              '      if (!mounted ||\n'
              '          _finishCancellationRequested ||\n'
              '          _finishDrainFailed ||\n'
              '          _discardingCapture ||\n'
              '          !_recording) {\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final acceptedFrameCount = _projectPhotos.count;\n'
              '      if (!officialCaptureCanFinish(acceptedFrameCount: acceptedFrameCount)) {\n'
              '        final remaining = kOfficialMinimumCaptureFrames - acceptedFrameCount;\n'
              '        await showDialog<void>(\n'
              '          context: context,\n'
              '          barrierDismissible: false,\n'
              '          builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(\n'
              '            key: const ValueKey<String>(\'official-minimum-photos-dialog\'),\n'
              '            title: const Text(\'至少拍摄20张照片\'),\n'
              '            content: Text(\n'
              '              \'要结束任务，必须至少拍摄20张照片。\\n\'\n'
              '              \'当前已完成 $acceptedFrameCount 张，还需要 $remaining 张。\\n\'\n'
              '              \'尽量从更多不同角度拍摄照片，\'\n'
              '              \'完成20张并分析后，点云会覆盖显示在物体上。\',\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            actions: [\n'
              '              FilledButton(\n'
              '                onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(),\n'
              '                child: const Text(\'继续拍摄\'),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final cov = _coverageCloud.coverageStats();\n'
              '      if (starvedFinishGateShouldPrompt(\n'
              '        starvedTrue: cov.starvedTrue,\n'
              '        trueVoxels: cov.trueVoxels,\n'
              '      )) {\n'
              '        final finishAnyway = await showDialog<bool>(\n'
              '          context: context,\n'
              '          barrierDismissible: false,\n'
              '          builder: (ctx) => AlertDialog(\n'
              '            title: const Text(\'拍摄角度可能不足\'),\n'
              '            content: const Text(\n'
              '              \'仍有较多区域拍摄角度不足，可能出现分层。\\n\'\n'
              '              \'对黄色区域：横移一大步，或走近一半再拍。\',\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            actions: [\n'
              '              FilledButton(\n'
              '                onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(false),\n'
              '                child: const Text(\'继续拍摄\'),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              TextButton(\n'
              '                onPressed: () => Navigator.of(ctx).pop(true),\n'
              '                child: const Text(\'仍要完成\'),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '        TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'finish_gate\', {\n'
              '          \'starved\': cov.starvedTrue,\n'
              '          \'true_vox\': cov.trueVoxels,\n'
              '          \'choice\': finishAnyway == true ? \'finish_anyway\' : \'continue_capture\',\n'
              '        });\n'
              '        if (finishAnyway != true || !mounted) return;\n'
              '      } else {\n'
              '        TelemetryWriter.instance.event(\'finish_gate\', {\n'
              '          \'starved\': cov.starvedTrue,\n'
              '          \'true_vox\': cov.trueVoxels,\n'
              '          \'choice\': \'pass\',\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      await _finalizeRecording(navigateToDrafts: true, showSparseHint: true);\n'
              '    } finally {\n'
              '      if (mounted &&\n'
              '          _recording &&\n'
              '          !_discardingCapture &&\n'
              '          !_cameraResumeFailed) {\n'
              '        _shutterQueue.resume();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      _finishCancellationRequested = false;\n'
              '      if (mounted) setState(() => _finishTapInProgress = false);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Persist the just-recorded capture as a DRAFT scan.\n'
              '  ///\n'
              '  /// The Drafts card is the user-facing handle for the raw capture bundle:\n'
              '  /// `scan_records.json` points back to `<captureDir>/photos_highres/`, and\n'
              '  /// `<captureDir>/official_photo_bundle.json` is the source-of-truth manifest for\n'
              '  /// local DA3 / preflight / texture derivation.\n'
              '  Future<void> _finalizeRecording({\n'
              '    required bool navigateToDrafts,\n'
              '    required bool showSparseHint,\n'
              '  }) async {\n'
              '    final session = _session;\n'
              '    if (session == null || !_sfmCaptureReady) return;\n'
