              '    if (_finalizingRecording) return;\n'
              '    _finalizingRecording = true;\n'
              '    _stopAutoCapture();\n'
              '    _stopGuidanceTelemetry(); // 拍摄结束,【guidance】采样停止\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      await _shutterQueue.freezeAndDrain();\n'
              '      // RECORDING → STOP. The high-res stills are written incrementally\n'
              '      // under `<captureDir>/photos_highres/`; stop freezes curation and\n'
              '      // writes the shared photo_bundle contract.\n'
              '      await session.stop();\n'
              '      // T6: tear down the live sparse cloud when the take ends.\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<void>(\n'
              '          \'setFeaturePointsVisible\',\n'
              '          <String, dynamic>{\'visible\': false},\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      } catch (_) {}\n'
              '      if (mounted) {\n'
              '        setState(() {\n'
              '          _recording = false;\n'
              '          _isAiming = false;\n'
              '          _lockInProgress = false;\n'
              '        });\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      await session.waitForPendingPhotoSaves();\n'
              '      await _highResFailureSub?.cancel();\n'
              '      _highResFailureSub = null;\n'
              '      // Capture is over — STOP THE CAMERA NOW, before the minutes-scale SfM\n'
              '      // finalize. All keyframes are fed and every high-res still is on disk\n'
              '      // (the barrier above guarantees it), so the ARSession (4K camera\n'
              '      // capture + VIO + buffered ARFrames + ARSCNView GPU work) is pure\n'
              '      // overhead from here — and it was competing with the finalize for\n'
              '      // memory/GPU/thermal (mem ~950 MB, thermal=serious during solve).\n'
              '      // pause() + clearing recentFrameSnapshots frees it all for CPU+GPU SfM.\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        await _arKitChannel.invokeMethod<void>(\'stopSession\');\n'
              '        DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '          \'OfficialARCapturePage\',\n'
              '          \'finish: ARSession stopped (camera off)\',\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      } catch (_) {}\n'
              '      final recon = _sfmRecon;\n'
              '      if (_projectPhotos.count == 0) {\n'
              '        if (recon != null) {\n'
              '          _sfmRecon = null;\n'
              '          await _sfmFeedSub?.cancel();\n'
              '          _sfmFeedSub = null;\n'
              '          await _sfmEventSub?.cancel();\n'
              '          _sfmEventSub = null;\n'
              '          unawaited(recon.dispose());\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (mounted && showSparseHint) {\n'
              '          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '            SnackBar(\n'
              '              content: Text(AppL10n.of(context).captureMaterialTooSparseHint),\n'
              '              behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (navigateToDrafts && mounted) {\n'
              '          _exitToDrafts();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '\n'
              '      // Keep the post-capture waiting page alive until the authoritative final\n'
              '      // sparse cloud lands. SfM remains asynchronous: the worker drains every\n'
              '      // offered frame while this page merely renders queue progress, then runs\n'
              '      // spatial loop matching + full finalize. We deliberately retain the event\n'
              '      // subscription and session ownership here; detaching them would make the\n'
              '      // page exit straight to Drafts and hide the queue/final result.\n'
              '      final sfmPreviewing = recon != null && recon.offeredCount >= 2;\n'
              '      final captureDirForSfm = session.captureDir;\n'
              '      DeviceLog.log(\n'
              '        \'OfficialARCapturePage\',\n'
              '        \'finish: sfm fed=${recon?.fedCount ?? -1} \'\n'
              '            \'remaining=${recon?.remainingCount ?? -1} preview=$sfmPreviewing\',\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (sfmPreviewing) {\n'
              '        await _sfmFeedSub?.cancel();\n'
              '        _sfmFeedSub = null;\n'
              '        if (mounted) {\n'
              '          setState(() {\n'
              '            _sfmFed = recon.fedCount;\n'
              '            _sfmQueued = recon.remainingCount;\n'
              '            _sfmSnapshot = null;\n'
              '            _colorizeTarget = null;\n'
              '            _pendingLocalColored = null;\n'
              '            _sfmErrorText = null;\n'
              '            _sfmPhase = SfmPreviewPhase.generating;\n'
              '            _showDraftsWhileReconstructing = false;\n'
              '            // 修1:队列已空则立即进入阶段 1;否则等 FrameFed 排空时进。\n'
              '            _sfmFinalizeStage = recon.remainingCount == 0 ? 1 : 0;\n'
              '            _sfmStageStartMs = DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch;\n'
              '          });\n'
              '          _startSfmStageTicker();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        if (captureDirForSfm != null) {\n'
              '          await _beginReconUmbrella(captureDirForSfm);\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        recon.finalize();\n'
              '      } else if (recon != null) {\n'
              '        _sfmRecon = null;\n'
              '        await _sfmFeedSub?.cancel();\n'
              '        _sfmFeedSub = null;\n'
              '        await _sfmEventSub?.cancel();\n'
              '        _sfmEventSub = null;\n'
              '        unawaited(recon.dispose());\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      // Every verified 12MP shutter is a project photo. Upload curation may\n'
              '      // choose a subset for a later stage, but it must never delete photos from\n'
              '      // the user-visible album or change its one authoritative count.\n'
              '      await _persistDraft(showSnackBar: mounted && showSparseHint);\n'
              '      // Pop with `true` as a signal to AetherAppShell that it should\n'
              '      // switch the active tab to Me Drafts (the user just created a\n'
              '      // scan and expects to see it sitting in their drafts list).\n'
              '      if (navigateToDrafts && mounted) {\n'
              '        _exitToDrafts();\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    } finally {\n'
              '      _finalizingRecording = false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Exit to Drafts — unless the live-reconstruction preview overlay is up,\n'
              '  /// in which case the user leaves via its "完成" button and the pop is\n'
              '  /// deferred to [_onSfmPreviewDone].\n'
              '  void _exitToDrafts() {\n'
              '    if (_sfmPhase != null) {\n'
              '      _sfmPendingPop = true;\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    Navigator.of(context).pop(true);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _persistDraft({required bool showSnackBar}) async {\n'
              '    final session = _session;\n'
              '    if (session == null) return;\n'
              '    final dir = session.photosHighresDir ?? session.photosDir;\n'
              '    final photoCount = _projectPhotos.count;\n'
              '    final captureDirPath = session.captureDir;\n'
              '    if (dir == null || captureDirPath == null || photoCount == 0) {\n'
              '      if (mounted && showSnackBar) {\n'
              '        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '          SnackBar(\n'
              '            content: Text(AppL10n.of(context).captureMaterialTooSparseHint),\n'
              '            behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final photosDir = Directory(dir);\n'
              '    final captureDir = Directory(captureDirPath);\n'
              '    final captureSegments = captureDir.uri.pathSegments\n'
              '        .where((s) => s.isNotEmpty)\n'
              '        .toList();\n'
              '    final captureId = captureSegments.isNotEmpty\n'
              '        ? captureSegments.last\n'
              '        : \'cap_${DateTime.now().microsecondsSinceEpoch}\';\n'
              '    final createdAt = DateTime.now();\n'
              '    final store = ScanRecordStore.instance;\n'
              '    await store.ensureLoaded();\n'
              '\n'
              '    String? thumbnailPath;\n'
              '    final firstPhoto =\n'
              '        _projectPhotos.paths\n'
              '            .where((p) => File(p).existsSync())\n'
              '            .toList(growable: false)\n'
              '          ..sort();\n'
              '    if (firstPhoto.isNotEmpty) {\n'
              '      final thumbnail = await store.thumbnailFileFor(\n'
              '        captureId,\n'
              '        pipelineKind: CapturePipelineKind.official,\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      final sourcePath = _cardThumbnailSourceFor(firstPhoto.first);\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        await thumbnail.parent.create(recursive: true);\n'
              '        final wroteThumbnail = await _writeCardThumbnail(\n'
              '          sourcePath: sourcePath,\n'
              '          destination: thumbnail,\n'
              '        );\n'
              '        if (wroteThumbnail) {\n'
              '          thumbnailPath = thumbnail.path;\n'
              '        } else {\n'
              '          await File(sourcePath).copy(thumbnail.path);\n'
              '          thumbnailPath = thumbnail.path;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      } on FileSystemException {\n'
              '        thumbnailPath = null;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '\n'
              '    final manifestFile = await session.writeProjectPhotoBundleManifest(\n'
              '      _projectPhotos.paths,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (manifestFile == null || !manifestFile.existsSync()) return;\n'
              '    final record = ScanRecord(\n'
              '      id: captureId,\n'
              '      name: nextUntitledScanName(store.records.map((r) => r.name)),\n'
              '      createdAt: createdAt,\n'
              '      pipelineKind: CapturePipelineKind.official,\n'
              '      preferredCaptureMode: CaptureMode.local,\n'
              '      thumbnailPath: thumbnailPath,\n'
              '      captureDir: captureDir.path,\n'
              '      photosDir: photosDir.path,\n'
              '      captureManifestPath: manifestFile.path,\n'
              '      photoCount: photoCount,\n'
              '      cloudUploadStatus: ScanCloudUploadStatus.localPending,\n'
              '      localRawRetainedForDebug: true,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    await store.addOrUpdate(record);\n'
              '    unawaited(removeTransientCapturePreviews(captureDir));\n'
              '    // Reconstruction happens two ways, both independent of any local mesh\n'
              '    // pipeline: the streaming SfM preview (already running) and server-side\n'
              '    // recon once the draft uploads. The draft stays at localPending for the\n'
              '    // uploader to pick up.\n'
              '    if (mounted && showSnackBar) {\n'
              '      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(\n'
              '        SnackBar(\n'
              '          content: Text(\'已保存本地素材：$photoCount 张有效照片\'),\n'
              '          behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  String _cardThumbnailSourceFor(String highresPath) {\n'
              '    final previewPath = highresPath.replaceFirst(\n'
              '      \'/photos_highres/\',\n'
              '      \'/previews/\',\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    if (previewPath != highresPath && File(previewPath).existsSync()) {\n'
              '      return previewPath;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return highresPath;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _deleteProjectPhoto(String path) async {\n'
              '    final recon = _sfmRecon;\n'
              '    int? removedFrameId;\n'
              '    if (recon != null) {\n'
              '      for (final entry in recon.fedFrameMeta.entries) {\n'
              '        if (entry.value.jpegPath == path) {\n'
              '          removedFrameId = entry.key;\n'
              '          break;\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      }\n'
              '      final removed = await recon.removePhoto(path);\n'
              '      if (!removed) {\n'
              '        if (mounted) {\n'
              '          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)\n'
              '            ..hideCurrentSnackBar()\n'
              '            ..showSnackBar(\n'
              '              const SnackBar(\n'
              '                content: Text(\'照片暂时无法从重建中撤回，请稍后重试\'),\n'
              '                behavior: SnackBarBehavior.floating,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            );\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        return;\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    _projectPhotos.remove(path);\n'
              '    _photoCardStateSent.remove(path);\n'
              '    _photoCaptureEpochMs.remove(path);\n'
              '    _failedEvidenceJpegPaths.remove(path);\n'
              '    if (removedFrameId != null) {\n'
              '      _trueFrameParallaxDeg.remove(removedFrameId);\n'
              '      _frameBelowEnterStreak.remove(removedFrameId);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    final keep = _targetPoints.retainedJpegPaths.toSet()..remove(path);\n'
              '    _targetPoints.retainOnlyJpegPaths(keep);\n'
              '    unawaited(\n'
              '      _arKitChannel\n'
              '          .invokeMethod<void>(\'removePhotoCard\', <String, dynamic>{\n'
              '            \'evidenceJpegPath\': path,\n'
              '          })\n'
              '          .catchError((Object _) {}),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final previewPath = path.replaceFirst(\'/photos_highres/\', \'/previews/\');\n'
              '    final sidecarPath = path.endsWith(\'.jpg\')\n'
              '        ? \'${path.substring(0, path.length - 4)}.json\'\n'
              '        : \'$path.json\';\n'
              '    // [E25 2026-07-20] 连带删掉 12MP 静照及其 sidecar。此前 `_hr` 反正会在\n'
              '    // 点"完成"时被策展清理全删,漏删无所谓;现在 `_hr` 要长期留存(它是纹理\n'
              '    // 素材源),不跟着删就会变成永久孤儿文件(每个约 4MB)。\n'
              '    final hrPath = path.replaceFirst(RegExp(r\'\\.jpg$\'), \'_hr.jpg\');\n'
              '    final hrSidecarPath = path.replaceFirst(RegExp(r\'\\.jpg$\'), \'_hr.json\');\n'
              '    for (final candidate in <String>{\n'
              '      path,\n'
              '      previewPath,\n'
              '      sidecarPath,\n'
              '      hrPath,\n'
              '      hrSidecarPath,\n'
              '    }) {\n'
              '      try {\n'
              '        final file = File(candidate);\n'
              '        if (await file.exists()) {\n'
              '          await file.delete();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '      } on FileSystemException {\n'
              '        // Best-effort UI deletion; the authoritative ledger has already been\n'
              '        // updated so the count cannot resurrect on a widget rebuild.\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (mounted) setState(() {});\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<bool> _writeCardThumbnail({\n'
              '    required String sourcePath,\n'
              '    required File destination,\n'
              '  }) async {\n'
              '    try {\n'
              '      final bytes = await compute(\n'
              '        _buildCaptureCardThumbnailBytes,\n'
              '        sourcePath,\n'
              '        debugLabel: \'capture-card-thumbnail\',\n'
              '      );\n'
              '      if (bytes == null) return false;\n'
              '      await destination.writeAsBytes(bytes, flush: true);\n'
              '      return true;\n'
              '    } catch (e) {\n'
              '      debugPrint(\'[CapturePage] card thumbnail bake failed: $e\');\n'
              '      return false;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Future<void> _disposeCaptureResourcesAfterQueueDrain(\n'
              '    ManualCaptureQueue shutterQueue,\n'
              '    CaptureSession? session,\n'
              '  ) async {\n'
              '    await session?.stop();\n'
              '    await shutterQueue.freezeAndDrain();\n'
              '    shutterQueue.dispose();\n'
              '    await session?.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void dispose() {\n'
              '    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);\n'
              '    unawaited(_endReconUmbrella());\n'
              '    _stopGuidanceTelemetry();\n'
              '    // 遥测【resource】:拍摄页退出 → 停 Swift 侧 10s 资源采样。\n'
              '    unawaited(\n'
              '      _arKitChannel\n'
              '          .invokeMethod<void>(\'telemetryCaptureEnd\')\n'
              '          .then<void>((_) {}, onError: (Object _) {}),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    _warmupFallbackTimer?.cancel();\n'
              '    _sfmStageTicker?.cancel();\n'
              '    _coveragePushTimer?.cancel();\n'
              '    _poseSub?.cancel();\n'
              '    // Streaming-SfM teardown: frees the native session (joins the background\n'
              '    // BA thread, drops the sqlite db) off this isolate — page dispose never\n'
              '    // blocks. Re-entering capture creates a fresh session + worker.\n'
              '    _coverageFeedSub?.cancel();\n'
              '    _sfmFeedSub?.cancel();\n'
              '    _sfmEventSub?.cancel();\n'
              '    _highResFailureSub?.cancel();\n'
              '    final shutterQueue = _shutterQueue;\n'
              '    final session = _session;\n'
              '    _session = null;\n'
              '    // 自动拍与快门队列同生共死:队列一停收,它就只剩空转。\n'
              '    // 关页面时把还开着的那一轮收口 —— 幂等,已经收过就什么都不写。\n'
              '    _emitAutoTelemetry(_autoTelemetry.recordSessionEnd());\n'
              '    _autoCapture.stop();\n'
              '    shutterQueue.cancelPending();\n'
              '    unawaited(_disposeCaptureResourcesAfterQueueDrain(shutterQueue, session));\n'
              '    final sfmRecon = _sfmRecon;\n'
              '    _sfmRecon = null;\n'
              '    if (sfmRecon != null) unawaited(sfmRecon.dispose());\n'
              '    _adaptiveFpsTimer?.cancel(); // [ADAPTIVE-FPS]\n'
              '    _previewModel.dispose();\n'
              '    _projectPhotos.dispose();\n'
              '    _targetPoints.dispose();\n'
              '    // Evict the full-res capture bitmaps decoded for the album/thumbnails so\n'
              '    // they don\'t linger in the global imageCache into the community/me tabs.\n'
              '    PaintingBinding.instance.imageCache\n'
              '      ..clear()\n'
              '      ..clearLiveImages();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  // ─── Layout ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    // 修2【任务卡重入回归根因】:本 route 是普通 MaterialPageRoute,iOS\n'
              '    // 边缘右滑(或任何 maybePop)可以在重建等待期把整个 capture route\n'
              '    // pop 掉 → State.dispose() → recon.dispose() 排队 → phase-1 一结束\n'
              '    // worker 就被销毁(真机日志 21:00:02 "local_ready withheld" 下一行\n'
              '    // 即 "dispose: freeing session")。这违反契约:返回草稿不得销毁\n'
              '    // capture route / SfM worker;同任务卡必须能回原等待页。\n'
              '    // 修法:重建进行中(_sfmPhase != null)禁止隐式 pop;把返回手势\n'
              '    // 折叠成"显示草稿"(与等待页左上角返回按钮同一语义)。显式的\n'
              '    // Navigator.pop(_exitToDrafts/_onSfmPreviewDone)不受 canPop 影响。\n'
              '    return PopScope(\n'
              '      canPop: _sfmPhase == null,\n'
              '      onPopInvokedWithResult: (bool didPop, Object? result) {\n'
              '        if (didPop || _sfmPhase == null) return;\n'
              '        if (!_showDraftsWhileReconstructing) _showDraftsDuringReconstruction();\n'
              '      },\n'
              '      child: _buildRouteBody(context),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _buildRouteBody(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    _scheduleDraftTerminalExitIfNeeded();\n'
              '    if (_showDraftsWhileReconstructing && _sfmPhase != null) {\n'

              '                  // [2026-07-27 UI 签决]"官方"路由徽章已删除:线上只剩这一条\n'

              '                  ? () => unawaited(_startDenseStage())\n'
              '                  : null,\n'
              '              // [SEL-ENTRY 2026-07-30] 右上角"选区编辑":选区是可选动作,不点\n'
              '              // 就直接保存草稿。与底部"下一步"共用同一个进入函数,所以两个\n'
              '              // 入口不会产生两种状态。编辑态的出口("保存"/"返回")归\n'
              '              // SelectionToolsLayer,这里不再出按钮。\n'
              '              onEnterEditing:\n'
              '                  _sfmSnapshot != null && _sfmSnapshot!.pointCount > 0\n'
              '                  ? _enterSfmEditing\n'
              '                  : null,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '        ],\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  Widget _buildPreviewLayer() {\n'
              '    if (_initializing) {\n'
              '      return const ColoredBox(\n'
              '        color: Color(0xFF111113),\n'
              '        child: Center(\n'
              '          child: SizedBox(\n'
              '            width: 28,\n'
              '            height: 28,\n'
              '            child: CircularProgressIndicator(\n'
              '              strokeWidth: 2,\n'
              '              valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(Colors.white70),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    if (_initError != null) {\n'
              '      return ColoredBox(\n'
              '        color: const Color(0xFF111113),\n'
              '        child: Center(\n'
              '          child: Padding(\n'
              '            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 32),\n'
              '            child: Text(\n'
              '              _initError!,\n'
              '              textAlign: TextAlign.center,\n'
              '              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 14),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // iOS: live ARKit camera feed via UiKitView wrapping ARSCNView\n'
              '    // attached to the same ARSession the plugin owns. Verbatim port of\n'
              '    // ObjectModeV2ARKitPreview.swift which uses the same ARSCNView\n'
              '    // strategy. Other platforms fall back to a dark backdrop until a\n'
              '    // platform-specific preview is wired (Android ARCore / HarmonyOS).\n'
              '    if (defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.iOS) {\n'
              '      // [WYSIWYG 2026-07-19] 预览 letterbox 成照片画幅(photo43=3:4),黑边\n'
              '      // 顶底,显示完整 4:3 画面 —— 所见即所得。native 卡片几何按同一 3:4\n'
              '      // 视口算(AetherARKitPlugin videoFormatMode==hires43 分支),两者对齐。\n'
              '      return const ColoredBox(\n'
              '        color: Color(0xFF000000),\n'
              '        child: CapturePreviewRect(\n'
              '          child: UiKitView(\n'
              '            viewType: \'pocketworld_official_arkit_preview\',\n'
              '            creationParams: <String, dynamic>{},\n'
              '            creationParamsCodec: StandardMessageCodec(),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      );\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    return const ColoredBox(color: Color(0xFF111113));\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ─── Top bar widgets ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
              '\n'
              '/// Plan G W2 P3: 3 s fading toast that surfaces GuidanceEngine HARD\n'
              '/// reject signals (blur / dark / bright) to the user mid-recording.\n'
              '/// Subscribes to [CaptureSession.guidanceStream] and re-arms its fade\n'
              '/// timer on every non-null `hardRejectKind` snapshot, so a continuous\n'
              '/// blur run keeps the toast pinned visible. Auto-fades 3 s after the\n'
              '/// last bad frame.\n'
              'class _HardRejectToast extends StatefulWidget {\n'
              '  final Stream<GuidanceSnapshot> stream;\n'
              '  const _HardRejectToast({required this.stream});\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  State<_HardRejectToast> createState() => _HardRejectToastState();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _HardRejectToastState extends State<_HardRejectToast> {\n'
              '  StreamSubscription<GuidanceSnapshot>? _sub;\n'
              '  Timer? _fadeTimer;\n'
              '  String? _shownKind;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void initState() {\n'
              '    super.initState();\n'
              '    _sub = widget.stream.listen(_onSnapshot);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _onSnapshot(GuidanceSnapshot snap) {\n'
              '    final kind = snap.hardRejectKind;\n'
              '    if (kind == null) return;\n'
              '    if (!mounted) return;\n'
              '    setState(() => _shownKind = kind);\n'
              '    _fadeTimer?.cancel();\n'
              '    _fadeTimer = Timer(const Duration(seconds: 3), () {\n'
              '      if (mounted) setState(() => _shownKind = null);\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void dispose() {\n'
              '    _sub?.cancel();\n'
              '    _fadeTimer?.cancel();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  ({String text, IconData icon}) _content(String kind) {\n'
              '    switch (kind) {\n'
              '      case \'blur\':\n'
              '        return (text: \'手抖了，稳一稳手\', icon: Icons.vibration);\n'
              '      case \'dark\':\n'
              '        return (text: \'光线太暗，找亮一些的地方\', icon: Icons.brightness_low);\n'
              '      case \'bright\':\n'
              '        return (text: \'光线太强，避开直射光\', icon: Icons.wb_sunny_outlined);\n'
              '      default:\n'
              '        return (text: \'\', icon: Icons.warning_amber_rounded);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final kind = _shownKind;\n'
              '    final visible = kind != null;\n'
              '    final pickedKind = kind ?? \'blur\'; // placeholder when fading out\n'
              '    final content = _content(pickedKind);\n'
              '    return IgnorePointer(\n'
              '      child: AnimatedOpacity(\n'
              '        opacity: visible ? 1.0 : 0.0,\n'
              '        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 250),\n'
              '        child: Container(\n'
              '          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),\n'
              '          decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.65),\n'
              '            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          child: Row(\n'
              '            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              Icon(content.icon, color: Colors.white, size: 18),\n'
              '              const SizedBox(width: 8),\n'
              '              Text(\n'
              '                content.text,\n'
              '                style: const TextStyle(\n'
              '                  color: Colors.white,\n'
              '                  fontSize: 14,\n'
              '                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '/// 补强1:"拍摄角度不足"实时横幅。样式与 [_HardRejectToast] 同款黑底\n'

              'class _MotionSpeedToast extends StatefulWidget {\n'

              '  State<_MotionSpeedToast> createState() => _MotionSpeedToastState();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _MotionSpeedToastState extends State<_MotionSpeedToast> {\n'

              '  void _onMotion(CaptureMotionSnapshot snap) {\n'

              '\n'
              'class _PhotoPositionOverlay extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  final RealtimeCapturePreviewModel model;\n'
              '  final DomeTargetPoints targetPoints;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const _PhotoPositionOverlay({\n'
              '    required this.model,\n'
              '    required this.targetPoints,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return AnimatedBuilder(\n'
              '      animation: model,\n'
              '      builder: (_, _) {\n'
              '        final pose = model.lastPose;\n'
              '        if (pose == null || model.cameraSamples.isEmpty) {\n'
              '          return const SizedBox.expand();\n'
              '        }\n'
              '        final photoPaths =\n'
              '            targetPoints.retainedJpegPaths\n'
              '                .where((p) => File(p).existsSync())\n'
              '                .toList(growable: false)\n'
              '              ..sort();\n'
              '        return LayoutBuilder(\n'
              '          builder: (context, constraints) {\n'
              '            final size = constraints.biggest;\n'
              '            final visibleCards = <_ProjectedPhotoCard>[];\n'
              '            for (final sample in model.cameraSamples.reversed.take(90)) {\n'
              '              final projected = _projectCameraSampleToScreen(\n'
              '                sample.position,\n'
              '                pose: pose,\n'
              '                size: size,\n'
              '              );\n'
              '              if (projected == null) continue;\n'
              '              final pathIndex = sample.photoCount - 1;\n'
              '              visibleCards.add(\n'
              '                _ProjectedPhotoCard(\n'
              '                  sample: sample,\n'
              '                  offset: projected.offset,\n'
              '                  depth: projected.depth,\n'
              '                  path: pathIndex >= 0 && pathIndex < photoPaths.length\n'
              '                      ? photoPaths[pathIndex]\n'
              '                      : null,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              );\n'
              '            }\n'
              '            visibleCards.sort((a, b) => b.depth.compareTo(a.depth));\n'
              '            return Stack(\n'
              '              children: [\n'
              '                for (final card in visibleCards)\n'
              '                  Positioned(\n'
              '                    left: card.offset.dx - card.width / 2,\n'
              '                    top: card.offset.dy - card.height / 2,\n'
              '                    child: _PhotoPositionCard(\n'
              '                      path: card.path,\n'
              '                      width: card.width,\n'
              '                      height: card.height,\n'
              '                      opacity: card.opacity,\n'
              '                      rotation: _cameraYawFromOrientation(\n'
              '                        card.sample.orientation,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                      sfmConfirmed: card.sample.sfmConfirmed,\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '              ],\n'
              '            );\n'
              '          },\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      },\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _ProjectedPhotoCard {\n'
              '  final CapturePreviewCameraSample sample;\n'
              '  final Offset offset;\n'
              '  final double depth;\n'
              '  final String? path;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const _ProjectedPhotoCard({\n'
              '    required this.sample,\n'
              '    required this.offset,\n'
              '    required this.depth,\n'
              '    required this.path,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  double get width => (34 - depth * 2.1).clamp(18.0, 32.0).toDouble();\n'
              '  double get height => width * 1.34;\n'
              '  double get opacity => (0.92 - depth * 0.045).clamp(0.46, 0.88).toDouble();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _ScreenProjection {\n'
              '  final Offset offset;\n'
              '  final double depth;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const _ScreenProjection({required this.offset, required this.depth});\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '_ScreenProjection? _projectCameraSampleToScreen(\n'
              '  Vector3 worldPosition, {\n'
              '  required ARPose pose,\n'
              '  required Size size,\n'
              '}) {\n'
              '  final invOrientation = pose.orientation.conjugated();\n'
              '  final rel = worldPosition - pose.position;\n'
              '  final cam = invOrientation.rotated(rel);\n'
              '  final depth = -cam.z;\n'
              '  if (depth <= 0.12 || depth > 12.0) return null;\n'
              '  final focal = size.shortestSide * 0.72;\n'
              '  final sx = size.width / 2 + (cam.x / depth) * focal;\n'
              '  final sy = size.height / 2 - (cam.y / depth) * focal;\n'
              '  if (sx < -60 || sx > size.width + 60 || sy < -80 || sy > size.height + 80) {\n'
              '    return null;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '  return _ScreenProjection(offset: Offset(sx, sy), depth: depth);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'double _cameraYawFromOrientation(Quaternion orientation) {\n'
              '  final forward = orientation.rotated(Vector3(0, 0, -1));\n'
              '  return math.atan2(forward.x, forward.z);\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _PhotoPositionCard extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  final String? path;\n'
              '  final double width;\n'
              '  final double height;\n'
              '  final double opacity;\n'
              '  final double rotation;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// Border color signal: false → BLACK (just captured, not yet\n'
              '  /// reconstructed), true → WHITE (backend SfM has confirmed it). Always\n'
              '  /// false for now — the SfM hookup is deferred.\n'
              '  final bool sfmConfirmed;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const _PhotoPositionCard({\n'
              '    required this.path,\n'
              '    required this.width,\n'
              '    required this.height,\n'
              '    required this.opacity,\n'
              '    required this.rotation,\n'
              '    required this.sfmConfirmed,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final imagePath = path;\n'
              '    return Opacity(\n'
              '      opacity: opacity,\n'
              '      child: Transform.rotate(\n'
              '        angle: rotation * 0.18,\n'
              '        child: Container(\n'
              '          width: width,\n'
              '          height: height,\n'
              '          decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '            color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.20),\n'
              '            border: Border.all(\n'
              '              color: sfmConfirmed ? Colors.white : Colors.black,\n'
              '              width: 1.6,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            boxShadow: [\n'
              '              BoxShadow(\n'
              '                color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.32),\n'
              '                blurRadius: 8,\n'
              '                offset: const Offset(0, 3),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,\n'
              '          child: imagePath == null\n'
              '              ? Icon(\n'
              '                  Icons.photo_outlined,\n'
              '                  size: width * 0.48,\n'
              '                  color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.75),\n'
              '                )\n'
              '              : Image.file(File(imagePath), fit: BoxFit.cover, cacheWidth: 120),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '/// RS 复刻显示开关的按钮壳:44×44 点击区,纯显示层,无任何业务副作用。\n'

              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '/// 3×3 九点图标(覆盖点显示开关)。规格(2026-07-19):图标恒定单色——\n'
              '/// 开=全黄、关=灰;绝不出现绿色圆点(不映射实时覆盖色)。\n'
              'class _NineDotIcon extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _NineDotIcon({required this.color});\n'
              '\n'
              '  final Color color;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return CustomPaint(\n'
              '      size: const Size(24, 24),\n'
              '      painter: _NineDotPainter(color),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _NineDotPainter extends CustomPainter {\n'
              '  const _NineDotPainter(this.color);\n'
              '\n'
              '  final Color color;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {\n'
              '    final paint = Paint()..color = color;\n'
              '    final step = size.width / 3;\n'
              '    final r = step * 0.30;\n'
              '    for (var row = 0; row < 3; row++) {\n'
              '      for (var col = 0; col < 3; col++) {\n'
              '        canvas.drawCircle(\n'
              '          Offset(step * (col + 0.5), step * (row + 0.5)),\n'
              '          r,\n'
              '          paint,\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      }\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  bool shouldRepaint(_NineDotPainter oldDelegate) => oldDelegate.color != color;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '/// RealityScan-style bottom capture bar: latest-photo album thumbnail (left),\n'
              '/// center shutter (one tap = one photo), and a blue finish arrow (right).\n'
              '/// Rebuilds on every [targetPoints] change so the count + thumbnail stay live.\n'
              'class _ManualCaptureBar extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _ManualCaptureBar({\n'
              '    required this.projectPhotos,\n'
              '    required this.shutterQueue,\n'
              '    required this.processedCount,\n'
              '    required this.ready,\n'
              '    required this.finishing,\n'
              '    required this.mode,\n'
              '    required this.autoRunning,\n'
              '    required this.autoIndicator,\n'
              '    required this.autoPulseToken,\n'
              '    required this.onShutter,\n'
              '    required this.onToggleMode,\n'
              '    required this.onToggleAutoRun,\n'
              '    required this.onOpenAlbum,\n'
              '    required this.onFinish,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final OfficialProjectPhotoAlbum projectPhotos;\n'
              '  final ManualCaptureQueue shutterQueue;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// [RS-RING 2026-08-06 用户签决] SfM 已处理完的帧数(页面 `_sfmFed`,每个\n'
              '  /// SfmLiveFrameFed 事件 setState 实时刷新)。相册缩略图外圈的白色进度环\n'
              '  /// = processedCount / count:拍新照分母涨环回退,处理跟上环前进,转满一圈\n'
              '  /// = 全部处理完成。复刻 RS 的相册进度环(RS 蓝,我们白)。\n'
              '  final int processedCount;\n'
              '  final bool ready;\n'
              '  final bool finishing;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// [spec §8.1] 手动 = 白快门;自动 = 红录制键。两态共用同一排,只换中间\n'
              '  /// 那一颗 —— **自动模式下没有第二颗手动快门**(RS 同款):要手动补拍就\n'
              '  /// 切回手动模式。\n'
              '  final OfficialCaptureMode mode;\n'
              '  final bool autoRunning;\n'
              '  final AutoCaptureIndicator autoIndicator;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 每落一帧 +1,驱动录制键脉冲一次。\n'
              '  final int autoPulseToken;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback onShutter;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback onToggleMode;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback onToggleAutoRun;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback onOpenAlbum;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback? onFinish;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return AnimatedBuilder(\n'
              '      animation: Listenable.merge(<Listenable>[projectPhotos, shutterQueue]),\n'
              '      builder: (context, _) {\n'
              '        // [SIGNED 2026-07-27] 300 张预算用尽 → 快门置灰(与相册徽章的\n'
              '        // 琥珀态、_onShutterTap 的兜底同源)。\n'
              '        final canShoot = officialCaptureCanShoot(\n'
              '          acceptedFrameCount:\n'
              '              projectPhotos.count + shutterQueue.outstandingCount,\n'
              '        );\n'
              '        final latest = projectPhotos.latestPath;\n'
              '        return Padding(\n'
              '          // [2026-07-27 UI-2 签决] 底部内边距 24→0:相册/快门/完成整排向下\n'
              '          // 平移 24pt,贴到 SafeArea 上沿 —— 刘海机由 SafeArea 让开的 34pt\n'
              '          // 兜着,按钮不进 Home 手势区。⚠️ Home 键机型(SE 2/3)的\n'
              '          // padding.bottom 是 0,SafeArea 让开的也是 0,所以那类机型要靠\n'
              '          // captureShutterRowBottomPadding 补一个最小外边距,否则快门圆会贴\n'
              '          // 死屏幕物理底边。\n'
              '          padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(\n'
              '            20,\n'
              '            0,\n'
              '            20,\n'
              '            captureShutterRowBottomPadding(\n'
              '              MediaQuery.paddingOf(context).bottom,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          child: Row(\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              // ⚠️ 槽是 SizedBox 给的**紧**宽度约束,徽章自己的 Container\n'
              '              // 逃不掉(BoxConstraints.enforce 会把 48 顶回槽宽)—— 少这层\n'
              '              // Align,徽章就会被拉成槽宽 × kCaptureAlbumThumbSize 的扁矩形。\n'
              '              // 右端的完成键一直有这层 Align,相册这边是漏的。\n'
              '              SizedBox(\n'
              '                width: kCaptureShutterRowSideSlot,\n'
              '                child: Align(\n'
              '                  alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,\n'
              '                  child: _AlbumThumbButton(\n'
              '                    latestPath: latest,\n'
              '                    count: projectPhotos.count,\n'
              '                    processed: processedCount,\n'
              '                    onTap: onOpenAlbum,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              // [spec §8.1] 模式 toggle 坐在相册与快门**之间**(RS 同款)。\n'
              '              // FittedBox 兜底:iPhone SE Display Zoom(320pt)这类声明支持\n'
              '              // 的窄机型上宁可整体缩一点,也不许 RenderFlex 溢出。\n'
              '              // ⚠️ 兜底只该在 320pt 那一档生效:胶囊加宽到\n'
              '              // kCaptureModeToggleWidth 后,14 Pro(393)剩 78.5、\n'
              '              // SE 2/3(375)剩 69.5,两台都装得下 68 —— 高度因此仍是实打实\n'
              '              // 的 44,没被 scaleDown 顺手压矮。这条余量是靠把两端槽位从写死\n'
              '              // 的 72 收到 kCaptureShutterRowSideSlot 让出来的。\n'
              '              Expanded(\n'
              '                child: Align(\n'
              '                  alignment: Alignment.centerRight,\n'
              '                  child: FittedBox(\n'
              '                    fit: BoxFit.scaleDown,\n'
              '                    alignment: Alignment.centerRight,\n'
              '                    child: Padding(\n'
              '                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 4),\n'
              '                      child: _CaptureModeToggle(\n'
              '                        mode: mode,\n'
              '                        // 采集中也允许切走(spec §7 第一条):切模式是 UI\n'
              '                        // 行为,已拍帧全保留、队列继续消化。只有收尾流程里\n'
              '                        // 才锁住 —— 那时整条采集已经在关门了。\n'
              '                        onTap: finishing ? null : onToggleMode,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              if (mode == OfficialCaptureMode.auto)\n'
              '                _AutoRecordButton(\n'
              '                  // 在跑时**恒可点**:哪怕预算刚好用尽、队列刚好停收,\n'
              '                  // 用户也必须能按停(autoCaptureRecordButtonEnabled)。\n'
              '                  enabled: autoCaptureRecordButtonEnabled(\n'
              '                    running: autoRunning,\n'
              '                    canStart: ready && shutterQueue.accepting && canShoot,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  running: autoRunning,\n'
              '                  indicator: autoIndicator,\n'
              '                  pulseToken: autoPulseToken,\n'
              '                  onTap: onToggleAutoRun,\n'
              '                )\n'
              '              else\n'
              '                _ShutterButton(\n'
              '                  // [SIGNED 2026-07-27] 300 张上限:唯一置灰理由(与\n'
              '                  // _onShutterTap 的兜底同源 officialCaptureCanShoot)。\n'
              '                  // 07-12 的"快门永不因队列/热态置灰"铁律不受影响 ——\n'
              '                  // 这不是限流,是任务预算用尽。\n'
              '                  enabled: ready && shutterQueue.accepting && canShoot,\n'
              '                  // 在途/排队期间继续接收点击；只有 admission 已冻结、会话\n'
              '                  // 未就绪或预算用尽才禁用。\n'
              '                  onTap: ready && shutterQueue.accepting && canShoot\n'
              '                      ? onShutter\n'
              '                      : null,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              // 与左侧 toggle 槽对称的留白 —— 两个等权 Expanded 才能让快门\n'
              '              // 停在整排的正中,而不是被 toggle 顶偏。\n'
              '              const Expanded(child: SizedBox.shrink()),\n'
              '              SizedBox(\n'
              '                width: kCaptureShutterRowSideSlot,\n'
              '                child: Align(\n'
              '                  alignment: Alignment.centerRight,\n'
              '                  child: _FinishArrowButton(\n'
              '                    // 2026-07-25 回退 eaf8706 的 capturing 门:「完成」必须随时\n'
              '                    // 可点。快门期间置灰+转圈是多余的 —— _finalizeRecording 本来\n'
              '                    // 就会等齐所有已点击快门、仍在队列/处理中的照片再收尾,\n'
              '                    // 按钮层再拦一道只会让 UX 出现本不该有的加载态。\n'
              '                    busy: finishing,\n'
              '                    onTap:\n'
              '                        projectPhotos.count + shutterQueue.outstandingCount == 0\n'
              '                        ? null\n'
              '                        : onFinish,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      },\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _AlbumThumbButton extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _AlbumThumbButton({\n'
              '    required this.latestPath,\n'
              '    required this.count,\n'
              '    required this.processed,\n'
              '    required this.onTap,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final String? latestPath;\n'
              '  final int count;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// SfM 已处理帧数;`processed/count` 驱动外圈白色进度环([RS-RING])。\n'
              '  final int processed;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback onTap;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    // [RS-RING 2026-08-06] 分母是"当前已拍",不是 300 上限 —— 拍新照环回退、\n'
              '    // 处理追上环闭合,与 RS 的语义一致(转满一圈 = 目前拍的全处理完)。\n'
              '    final double progress = count <= 0\n'
              '        ? 0.0\n'
              '        : (processed / count).clamp(0.0, 1.0);\n'
              '    return GestureDetector(\n'
              '      onTap: onTap,\n'
              '      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,\n'
              '      child: CustomPaint(\n'
              '        foregroundPainter: _AlbumRingPainter(progress: progress),\n'
              '        child: Container(\n'
              '          width: kCaptureAlbumThumbSize,\n'
              '          height: kCaptureAlbumThumbSize,\n'
              '          decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.44),\n'
              '            // [2026-08-21 等比缩小] 圆角/描边都乘 kCaptureAlbumThumbScale,\n'
              '            // 不是照抄原来的 14 / 1.5 —— 缩了外框不缩圆角,方框会变成药丸。\n'
              '            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(kCaptureAlbumThumbRadius),\n'
              '            // [RS-RING] 原 0.5α 静态白边即进度环的"轨道";实心白弧压其上。\n'
              '            border: Border.all(\n'
              '              color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.5),\n'
              '              width: 1.5 * kCaptureAlbumThumbScale,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,\n'
              '          // [2026-08-10 用户签决,附手绘] 相册缩略图照片撤下,徽章只显示计数:\n'
              '          // 左上**大**分子(已拍帧数)+ 斜杠 + 右下**小** 300。取代 07-27 的\n'
              '          // "照片上压竖排分数"。点击仍开相册,进度环照旧。\n'
              '          child: _AlbumCountFraction(count: count),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '/// [RS-RING 2026-08-06] 相册缩略图外圈进度环:沿圆角矩形边框路径顺时针扫过\n'
              '/// 的实心白弧,从顶边正中起笔。复刻 RS 的处理进度环呈现(RS 蓝我们白),\n'
              '/// 用 PathMetric 沿现有 14 圆角边框走线,不另起圆形以免与方形缩略图打架。\n'
              'class _AlbumRingPainter extends CustomPainter {\n'
              '  const _AlbumRingPainter({required this.progress});\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 0..1;1 = 当前已拍全部处理完成(环闭合)。\n'
              '  final double progress;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {\n'
              '    if (progress <= 0) return;\n'
              '    final rrect = RRect.fromRectAndRadius(\n'
              '      Offset.zero & size,\n'
              '      const Radius.circular(kCaptureAlbumThumbRadius),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final path = Path()..addRRect(rrect);\n'
              '    final metric = path.computeMetrics().first;\n'
              '    final total = metric.length;\n'
              '    final paint = Paint()\n'
              '      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke\n'
              '      ..strokeWidth = 3 * kCaptureAlbumThumbScale\n'
              '      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round\n'
              '      ..color = Colors.white;\n'
              '    if (progress >= 1) {\n'
              '      canvas.drawPath(path, paint);\n'
              '      return;\n'
              '    }\n'
              '    // addRRect 的路径起点在左上圆角后的顶边起点;把起笔挪到顶边正中,\n'
              '    // 环从 12 点方向顺时针生长(与 RS 一致)。\n'
              '    final start = (size.width / 2 - kCaptureAlbumThumbRadius).clamp(0.0, total);\n'
              '    final sweep = total * progress;\n'
              '    final end = start + sweep;\n'
              '    if (end <= total) {\n'
              '      canvas.drawPath(metric.extractPath(start, end), paint);\n'
              '    } else {\n'
              '      canvas.drawPath(metric.extractPath(start, total), paint);\n'
              '      canvas.drawPath(metric.extractPath(0, end - total), paint);\n'
              '    }\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  bool shouldRepaint(_AlbumRingPainter oldDelegate) =>\n'
              '      oldDelegate.progress != progress;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '/// RS 同款的堆叠分数:已拍张数 / 上限,压在缩略图正中,无底色。\n'
              'class _AlbumCountFraction extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _AlbumCountFraction({required this.count});\n'
              '\n'
              '  final int count;\n'
              '\n'
              '  static const List<Shadow> _shadows = <Shadow>[\n'
              '    Shadow(color: Color(0xCC000000), blurRadius: 4, offset: Offset(0, 1)),\n'
              '  ];\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    // 与快门置灰同源:拍满即转琥珀,不额外判断数字。\n'
              '    final tint = officialCaptureCanShoot(acceptedFrameCount: count)\n'
              '        ? Colors.white\n'
              '        : const Color(0xFFFFC24D);\n'
              '    final style = TextStyle(\n'
              '      color: tint,\n'
              '      fontSize: 15 * kCaptureAlbumThumbScale,\n'
              '      height: 1.05,\n'
              '      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,\n'
              '      shadows: _shadows,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    // [2026-08-10 用户签决,附手绘] 斜杠分数版式:左上大分子 + 45° 斜杠 +\n'
              '    // 右下小分母。分子随位数自适应字号(3 位数不撑破 60pt 徽章)。\n'
              '    //\n'
              '    // [2026-08-10 二稿] 斜杠恒 45°,且到两个数字的距离相等 —— 用 TextPainter\n'
              '    // 实测两段文字的包围盒,把斜杠中心放在"分子右下角 ↔ 分母左上角"连线的\n'
              '    // 中点上;位数变化(字宽变化)时自动保持等距,不靠写死坐标。\n'
              '    //\n'
              '    // [2026-08-21 等比缩小] 两级字号、斜杠、锚点内边距全部乘同一个\n'
              '    // kCaptureAlbumThumbScale。分子 22→17.6(三位数 19→15.2)、分母 10→8:\n'
              '    // 分子仍是徽章里最抢眼的那一段,分母作为次要信息在 Retina 上仍可读 ——\n'
              '    // 这是"缩到还看得清"的下限,再往下(0.7 ⇒ 分母 7)就糊了。\n'
              '    final bigSize = (count >= 100 ? 19.0 : 22.0) * kCaptureAlbumThumbScale;\n'
              '    final bigStyle = style.copyWith(fontSize: bigSize, height: 1.0);\n'
              '    final smallStyle = style.copyWith(\n'
              '      fontSize: 10 * kCaptureAlbumThumbScale,\n'
              '      height: 1.0,\n'
              '    );\n'
              '    final bigTp = TextPainter(\n'
              '      text: TextSpan(text: \'$count\', style: bigStyle),\n'
              '      textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,\n'
              '    )..layout();\n'
              '    final smallTp = TextPainter(\n'
              '      text: TextSpan(text: \'$kOfficialMaximumCaptureFrames\', style: smallStyle),\n'
              '      textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,\n'
              '    )..layout();\n'
              '    const slashLen = 24.0 * kCaptureAlbumThumbScale;\n'
              '    // 分子/分母的锚点内边距也随比例走,否则小徽章里两个数字会往中间挤。\n'
              '    const anchorBigL = 7.0 * kCaptureAlbumThumbScale;\n'
              '    const anchorBigT = 5.0 * kCaptureAlbumThumbScale;\n'
              '    const anchorSmallR = 6.0 * kCaptureAlbumThumbScale;\n'
              '    const anchorSmallB = 4.0 * kCaptureAlbumThumbScale;\n'
              '    return LayoutBuilder(\n'
              '      builder: (context, c) {\n'
              '        final w = c.maxWidth, h = c.maxHeight;\n'
              '        // 分子锚在 (anchorBigL, anchorBigT),分母锚在\n'
              '        // right:anchorSmallR / bottom:anchorSmallB(与 Positioned 一致)。\n'
              '        final bigBR = Offset(\n'
              '          anchorBigL + bigTp.width,\n'
              '          anchorBigT + bigTp.height,\n'
              '        );\n'
              '        final smallTL = Offset(\n'
              '          w - anchorSmallR - smallTp.width,\n'
              '          h - anchorSmallB - smallTp.height,\n'
              '        );\n'
              '        final mid = Offset(\n'
              '          (bigBR.dx + smallTL.dx) / 2,\n'
              '          (bigBR.dy + smallTL.dy) / 2,\n'
              '        );\n'
              '        return Stack(\n'
              '          children: [\n'
              '            Positioned(\n'
              '              left: anchorBigL,\n'
              '              top: anchorBigT,\n'
              '              child: Text(\'$count\', style: bigStyle),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            // 斜杠:竖线顺时针转 45° = "/",中心 = 两数字近角连线中点。\n'
              '            Positioned(\n'
              '              left: mid.dx - 0.75 * kCaptureAlbumThumbScale,\n'
              '              top: mid.dy - slashLen / 2,\n'
              '              child: Transform.rotate(\n'
              '                angle: math.pi / 4,\n'
              '                child: Container(\n'
              '                  width: 1.5 * kCaptureAlbumThumbScale,\n'
              '                  height: slashLen,\n'
              '                  decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '                    color: tint,\n'
              '                    boxShadow: const [\n'
              '                      BoxShadow(color: Color(0xCC000000), blurRadius: 4),\n'
              '                    ],\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            Positioned(\n'
              '              right: anchorSmallR,\n'
              '              bottom: anchorSmallB,\n'
              '              child: Text(\'$kOfficialMaximumCaptureFrames\', style: smallStyle),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ],\n'
              '        );\n'
              '      },\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _ShutterButton extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _ShutterButton({required this.onTap, this.enabled = true});\n'
              '\n'
              '  final bool enabled;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback? onTap;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return Opacity(\n'
              '      opacity: enabled ? 1.0 : 0.4,\n'
              '      child: GestureDetector(\n'
              '        onTap: onTap,\n'
              '        behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,\n'
              '        child: Container(\n'
              '          // 快门是快门行里最高的子项 —— 行高即由它决定,见\n'
              '          // kCaptureShutterRowHeight。\n'
              '          width: kCaptureShutterDiameter,\n'
              '          height: kCaptureShutterDiameter,\n'
              '          decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '            shape: BoxShape.circle,\n'
              '            border: Border.all(color: Colors.white, width: 4),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          child: Padding(\n'
              '            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(5),\n'
              '            // 队列忙碌不进入视觉状态：按钮只在会话未就绪、完成流程冻结\n'
              '            // admission 或 300 张预算用尽时变灰；在途/排队期间恒为纯白。\n'
              '            child: Container(\n'
              '              decoration: const BoxDecoration(\n'
              '                shape: BoxShape.circle,\n'
              '                color: Colors.white,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _FinishArrowButton extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _FinishArrowButton({required this.busy, required this.onTap});\n'
              '\n'
              '  final bool busy;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback? onTap;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final enabled = onTap != null && !busy;\n'
              '    return GestureDetector(\n'
              '      onTap: enabled ? onTap : null,\n'
              '      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,\n'
              '      child: Container(\n'
              '        width: kCaptureFinishButtonSize,\n'
              '        height: kCaptureFinishButtonSize,\n'
              '        decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '          shape: BoxShape.circle,\n'
              '          color: enabled\n'
              '              ? const Color(0xFF2F97FF)\n'
              '              : const Color(0xFF2F97FF).withValues(alpha: 0.4),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        child: busy\n'
              '            ? const Padding(\n'
              '                padding: EdgeInsets.all(16),\n'
              '                child: CircularProgressIndicator(\n'
              '                  strokeWidth: 2,\n'
              '                  valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation<Color>(Colors.white),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              )\n'
              '            : const Icon(\n'
              '                Icons.arrow_forward_rounded,\n'
              '                color: Colors.white,\n'
              '                size: 28,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _CloseButton extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  final VoidCallback onTap;\n'
              '  const _CloseButton({required this.onTap});\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return GestureDetector(\n'

              '          Icons.arrow_back_ios_new_rounded,\n'

              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ─── Aim mode overlay ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
              '//\n'
              '// Rendered while the user is in aim mode (between idle and recording).\n'
              '// White center crosshair (open circle, no fill) + small hint text.\n'
              '// IgnorePointer wrapper at the call site so the bottom record button\n'
              '// still receives taps; this overlay is purely visual.\n'
              'class _AimOverlay extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _AimOverlay();\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return Stack(\n'
              '      children: [\n'
              '        // Center aim guide. Deliberately not a filled ball or full ring:\n'
              '        // users read that as "subject already locked". Four brackets\n'
              '        // communicate "align here, then confirm with the bottom button".\n'
              '        Align(\n'
              '          alignment: const Alignment(0, -0.10),\n'
              '          child: SizedBox(\n'
              '            width: 78,\n'
              '            height: 78,\n'
              '            child: CustomPaint(\n'
              '              painter: _AimReticlePainter(\n'
              '                color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.9),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '        // Hint text below the crosshair.\n'
              '        Align(\n'
              '          alignment: const Alignment(0, 0.10),\n'
              '          child: Container(\n'
              '            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),\n'
              '            decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '              color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.55),\n'
              '              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '            child: Text(\n'
              '              AppL10n.of(context).captureAimHint,\n'
              '              style: TextStyle(\n'
              '                color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.9),\n'
              '                fontSize: 12,\n'
              '                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ],\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _AimReticlePainter extends CustomPainter {\n'
              '  final Color color;\n'
              '  const _AimReticlePainter({required this.color});\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {\n'
              '    final paint = Paint()\n'
              '      ..color = color\n'
              '      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke\n'
              '      ..strokeWidth = 2.6\n'
              '      ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round;\n'
              '    const inset = 4.0;\n'
              '    const len = 20.0;\n'
              '    final left = inset;\n'
              '    final top = inset;\n'
              '    final right = size.width - inset;\n'
              '    final bottom = size.height - inset;\n'
              '\n'
              '    canvas.drawLine(Offset(left, top), Offset(left + len, top), paint);\n'
              '    canvas.drawLine(Offset(left, top), Offset(left, top + len), paint);\n'
              '    canvas.drawLine(Offset(right, top), Offset(right - len, top), paint);\n'
              '    canvas.drawLine(Offset(right, top), Offset(right, top + len), paint);\n'
              '    canvas.drawLine(Offset(left, bottom), Offset(left + len, bottom), paint);\n'
              '    canvas.drawLine(Offset(left, bottom), Offset(left, bottom - len), paint);\n'
              '    canvas.drawLine(Offset(right, bottom), Offset(right - len, bottom), paint);\n'
              '    canvas.drawLine(Offset(right, bottom), Offset(right, bottom - len), paint);\n'
              '\n'
              '    final dotPaint = Paint()\n'
              '      ..color = color\n'
              '      ..style = PaintingStyle.fill;\n'
              '    canvas.drawCircle(Offset(size.width / 2, size.height / 2), 2.4, dotPaint);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  bool shouldRepaint(covariant _AimReticlePainter oldDelegate) {\n'
              '    return oldDelegate.color != color;\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// Small dark pill with white text used as the idle-state hint above the\n'
              '// bottom shutter button. Same look as the in-aim hint pill so the\n'
              '// transition idle → aim feels like the text just changes, not the chrome.\n'
              'class _IdleHintPill extends StatelessWidget {\n'

              '  const _IdleHintPill({required this.text});\n'
              '\n'

              '      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 6),\n'

              '        color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.55),\n'
              '        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '      child: Text(\n'
              '        text,\n'
              '        style: TextStyle(\n'
              '          color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.9),\n'
              '          fontSize: 12,\n'
              '          fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
