              '// ─── Bottom HUD: 140×140 captureButtonOrDome ──────────────────────────\n'
              '\n'
              'class _CaptureButtonOrDome extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  /// True between user\'s first tap (entering aim mode) and the lock\n'
              '  /// success that promotes to recording. Renders a checkmark instead\n'
              '  /// of the white-dot shutter.\n'
              '  final bool aiming;\n'
              '  final bool lockInProgress;\n'
              '  final bool enabled;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback? onTap;\n'
              '\n'
              '  const _CaptureButtonOrDome({\n'
              '    required this.aiming,\n'
              '    required this.lockInProgress,\n'
              '    required this.enabled,\n'
              '    required this.onTap,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final ring = SizedBox(\n'
              '      width: 140,\n'
              '      height: 140,\n'
              '      child: CustomPaint(painter: _WhiteRingPainter()),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '\n'
              '    // Idle and aim share the same pre-capture chrome (white ring +\n'
              '    // 119×119 black fill); only the central indicator differs:\n'
              '    //   • idle: 28×28 white dot (the classic shutter)\n'
              '    //   • aim:  white check icon — "tap to lock and start"\n'
              '    final Widget centerIndicator = lockInProgress\n'
              '        ? const SizedBox(\n'
              '            width: 34,\n'
              '            height: 34,\n'
              '            child: CircularProgressIndicator(\n'
              '              strokeWidth: 3,\n'
              '              color: Colors.white,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          )\n'
              '        : aiming\n'
              '        ? const Icon(Icons.check_rounded, size: 56, color: Colors.white)\n'
              '        : Container(\n'
              '            width: 28,\n'
              '            height: 28,\n'
              '            decoration: const BoxDecoration(\n'
              '              color: Colors.white,\n'
              '              shape: BoxShape.circle,\n'
              '            ),\n'
              '          );\n'
              '\n'
              '    return GestureDetector(\n'
              '      onTap: onTap,\n'
              '      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,\n'
              '      child: Opacity(\n'
              '        opacity: enabled ? 1.0 : 0.4,\n'
              '        child: SizedBox(\n'
              '          width: 140,\n'
              '          height: 140,\n'
              '          child: Stack(\n'
              '            alignment: Alignment.center,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              ring,\n'
              '              Container(\n'
              '                width: 119,\n'
              '                height: 119,\n'
              '                decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '                  color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.75),\n'
              '                  shape: BoxShape.circle,\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              centerIndicator,\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _WhiteRingPainter extends CustomPainter {\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {\n'
              '    final paint = Paint()\n'
              '      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke\n'
              '      ..strokeWidth = 4\n'
              '      ..color = Colors.white;\n'
              '    final r = (size.shortestSide - 4) / 2;\n'
              '    canvas.drawCircle(size.center(Offset.zero), r, paint);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  bool shouldRepaint(covariant _WhiteRingPainter oldDelegate) => false;\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '// ─── [spec §8.1] 自动采集的三件 UI:模式 toggle / 红录制键 / 开启提示 ──\n'
              '//\n'
              '// 三个类都放在文件**最末尾**,刻意避开 _ManualCaptureBar→_AlbumThumbButton\n'
              '// 与 _ShutterButton→_FinishArrowButton 这两段被既有契约测试逐字盯着的区间\n'
              '// (official_capture_frame_budget / manual_capture_bar_io /\n'
              '// official_highres_reconstruction),免得新代码误闯进别人的守门里。\n'
              '\n'
              '/// 手动 / 自动 模式切换键。RS 同款:快门**左侧**的一颗胶囊。\n'
              '///\n'
              '/// [2026-08-21 用户签决] 旧版是一颗 50×44 的单图标胶囊,点一下只换底色\n'
              '/// (手动灰 / 自动蓝)—— 用户原话「现在只是单纯的点击后变色」:静止时它\n'
              '/// 根本不说明"有两个模式",更不说明"另一个是什么"。现在是真正的分段开关:\n'
              '/// 相机 / 摄像机两个图标并排常驻,一颗高亮滑块**滑**到当前那一侧。\n'
              '///\n'
              '/// 颜色语义没动:滑块在自动侧时是 RS 的蓝(0xFF0A84FF),手动侧是浅灰。\n'
              '///\n'
              '/// ⚠️ 图标是摄像机,但模式名刻意避开"录像" —— 见 [OfficialCaptureMode]。\n'
              'class _CaptureModeToggle extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _CaptureModeToggle({required this.mode, required this.onTap});\n'
              '\n'
              '  final OfficialCaptureMode mode;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// null = 置灰不可点(只在收尾流程里)。\n'
              '  final VoidCallback? onTap;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 滑块的滑行动画。**沿用本页既有的那一档**(180ms / easeOut,红录制键\n'
              '  /// 「圆 ↔ 圆角方」的形变用的就是它)—— 同一排控件的直接操作反馈是同一种\n'
              '  /// 手感,不为这一颗另起一组数字。\n'
              '  static const Duration _slideDuration = Duration(milliseconds: 180);\n'
              '  static const Curve _slideCurve = Curves.easeOut;\n'
              '\n'
              '  /// 滑块相对胶囊的内缩。半格宽 34 - 2×3 = 28,滑块中心因此正落在图标中心\n'
              '  /// (左 17 / 右 51),不会差半格。\n'
              '  static const double _knobInset = 3;\n'
              '  static const double _knobHeight = kCaptureToggleButtonSize - _knobInset * 2;\n'
              '  static const double _knobWidth =\n'
              '      kCaptureModeToggleWidth / 2 - _knobInset * 2;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final auto = mode == OfficialCaptureMode.auto;\n'
              '    return Opacity(\n'
              '      opacity: onTap == null ? 0.4 : 1.0,\n'
              '      child: GestureDetector(\n'
              '        key: const ValueKey<String>(\'official-capture-mode-toggle\'),\n'
              '        onTap: onTap,\n'
              '        behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,\n'
              '        child: Container(\n'
              '          // 高度仍取既有的开关按钮常量:快门(76)仍是行内最高子项,\n'
              '          // kCaptureShutterRowHeight 的不变式一寸没动。只有宽度变了。\n'
              '          width: kCaptureModeToggleWidth,\n'
              '          height: kCaptureToggleButtonSize,\n'
              '          decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '            // 槽恒为半透明白;"当前是哪个模式"由滑块表达,不再靠整颗变色。\n'
              '            color: const Color(0x38FFFFFF),\n'
              '            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(kCaptureToggleButtonSize / 2),\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '          child: Stack(\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              // 高亮滑块:滑过去,不是瞬移。\n'
              '              AnimatedAlign(\n'
              '                duration: _slideDuration,\n'
              '                curve: _slideCurve,\n'
              '                alignment: auto ? Alignment.centerRight : Alignment.centerLeft,\n'
              '                child: Padding(\n'
              '                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(_knobInset),\n'
              '                  child: AnimatedContainer(\n'
              '                    duration: _slideDuration,\n'
              '                    curve: _slideCurve,\n'
              '                    width: _knobWidth,\n'
              '                    height: _knobHeight,\n'
              '                    decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '                      color: auto\n'
              '                          ? const Color(0xFF0A84FF) // RS 的蓝 = iOS system blue\n'
              '                          : const Color(0x59FFFFFF),\n'
              '                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_knobHeight / 2),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              // 两个图标常驻,压在滑块之上 —— 静止时也看得见"另一个模式"。\n'
              '              Row(\n'
              '                children: <Widget>[\n'
              '                  _CaptureModeToggleIcon(\n'
              '                    icon: Icons.photo_camera_rounded,\n'
              '                    selected: !auto,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                  _CaptureModeToggleIcon(\n'
              '                    icon: Icons.videocam_rounded,\n'
              '                    selected: auto,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ],\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '/// 分段开关里的一枚图标。占半格宽,选中侧全白、未选中侧半透明 —— 淡入淡出\n'
              '/// 与滑块同一档节奏,免得滑块已经到位了颜色还在原地跳。\n'
              'class _CaptureModeToggleIcon extends StatelessWidget {\n'
              '  const _CaptureModeToggleIcon({required this.icon, required this.selected});\n'
              '\n'
              '  final IconData icon;\n'
              '  final bool selected;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return Expanded(\n'
              '      child: Center(\n'
              '        child: AnimatedOpacity(\n'
              '          duration: _CaptureModeToggle._slideDuration,\n'
              '          curve: _CaptureModeToggle._slideCurve,\n'
              '          opacity: selected ? 1.0 : 0.5,\n'
              '          child: Icon(icon, size: 20, color: Colors.white),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '/// 自动模式的红色录制键 —— 它**就是**开始/停止键(RS 同款),自动模式下\n'
              '/// 没有另一颗手动快门。外形与 [_ShutterButton] 同尺寸同白环,只是芯是红的:\n'
              '/// 未开拍 = 红圆,开拍中 = 红圆角方(录制→停止的通用语)。\n'
              '///\n'
              '/// 指示器(spec §8)全部走**视觉状态**,一个字的说教都不上:\n'
              '///   · 落帧      → 白环向外脉冲一次([pulseToken] 每落一帧 +1)\n'
              '///   · 位移不够  → 白环转暗、静止(表达"在等你动")\n'
              '///   · 节奏拉长  → 不额外表达,脉冲之间自然变稀\n'
              'class _AutoRecordButton extends StatefulWidget {\n'
              '  const _AutoRecordButton({\n'
              '    required this.enabled,\n'
              '    required this.running,\n'
              '    required this.indicator,\n'
              '    required this.pulseToken,\n'
              '    required this.onTap,\n'
              '  });\n'
              '\n'
              '  final bool enabled;\n'
              '  final bool running;\n'
              '  final AutoCaptureIndicator indicator;\n'
              '  final int pulseToken;\n'
              '  final VoidCallback onTap;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  State<_AutoRecordButton> createState() => _AutoRecordButtonState();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _AutoRecordButtonState extends State<_AutoRecordButton>\n'
              '    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {\n'
              '  // ⚠️ 这是**纯显示**动画钟。它永远不回喂 AutoCaptureController ——\n'
              '  // controller 的唯一时钟是 ARPose.timestamp(ARFrame 时间轴)。\n'
              '  late final AnimationController _pulse = AnimationController(\n'
              '    vsync: this,\n'
              '    duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 420),\n'
              '  );\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void didUpdateWidget(_AutoRecordButton oldWidget) {\n'
              '    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);\n'
              '    if (widget.pulseToken != oldWidget.pulseToken) _pulse.forward(from: 0);\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void dispose() {\n'
              '    _pulse.dispose();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    final waiting = widget.indicator == AutoCaptureIndicator.waiting;\n'
              '    final ringAlpha = waiting ? 0.38 : 1.0;\n'
              '    const red = Color(0xFFFF3B30);\n'
              '    // 白环 4 + 内缩 5,与 _ShutterButton 的芯同尺寸。\n'
              '    const core = kCaptureShutterDiameter - 18;\n'
              '    return Opacity(\n'
              '      opacity: widget.enabled ? 1.0 : 0.4,\n'
              '      child: GestureDetector(\n'
              '        key: const ValueKey<String>(\'official-auto-capture-record-button\'),\n'
              '        onTap: widget.enabled ? widget.onTap : null,\n'
              '        behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,\n'
              '        child: SizedBox(\n'
              '          width: kCaptureShutterDiameter,\n'
              '          height: kCaptureShutterDiameter,\n'
              '          child: Stack(\n'
              '            alignment: Alignment.center,\n'
              '            children: [\n'
              '              AnimatedBuilder(\n'
              '                animation: _pulse,\n'
              '                builder: (context, _) {\n'
              '                  final t = _pulse.value;\n'
              '                  if (t <= 0 || t >= 1) return const SizedBox.shrink();\n'
              '                  final d = kCaptureShutterDiameter * (1 + 0.30 * t);\n'
              '                  return Container(\n'
              '                    width: d,\n'
              '                    height: d,\n'
              '                    decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '                      shape: BoxShape.circle,\n'
              '                      border: Border.all(\n'
              '                        color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: (1 - t) * 0.85),\n'
              '                        width: 3,\n'
              '                      ),\n'
              '                    ),\n'
              '                  );\n'
              '                },\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              AnimatedContainer(\n'
              '                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),\n'
              '                width: kCaptureShutterDiameter,\n'
              '                height: kCaptureShutterDiameter,\n'
              '                decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '                  shape: BoxShape.circle,\n'
              '                  border: Border.all(\n'
              '                    color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: ringAlpha),\n'
              '                    width: 4,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '              AnimatedContainer(\n'
              '                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 180),\n'
              '                curve: Curves.easeOut,\n'
              '                width: widget.running ? 28 : core,\n'
              '                height: widget.running ? 28 : core,\n'
              '                decoration: BoxDecoration(\n'
              '                  color: red,\n'
              '                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(\n'
              '                    widget.running ? 6 : core / 2,\n'
              '                  ),\n'
              '                ),\n'
              '              ),\n'
              '            ],\n'
              '          ),\n'
              '        ),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              '/// [spec §8.1] 顶部说明条 —— **一次性**,不是常驻。\n'
              '///\n'
              '/// 露出时机只有两个:挂上(= 进采集页,本组件只在 AR 会话建起来后才存在)、\n'
              '/// 以及 [mode] 变化(= 用户切了模式)。之后 3 秒自动淡出。\n'
              '///\n'

              '  const _CaptureModeTopHint({required this.mode});\n'
              '\n'
              '  final OfficialCaptureMode mode;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  State<_CaptureModeTopHint> createState() => _CaptureModeTopHintState();\n'
              '}\n'
              '\n'
              'class _CaptureModeTopHintState extends State<_CaptureModeTopHint> {\n'
              '  Timer? _fadeTimer;\n'
              '  bool _visible = false;\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void initState() {\n'
              '    super.initState();\n'
              '    // 首帧就可见,不能在 initState 里 setState。\n'
              '    _visible = true;\n'
              '    _armDismiss();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void didUpdateWidget(_CaptureModeTopHint oldWidget) {\n'
              '    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);\n'
              '    // ⚠️ 这个早退是必需的:父级每帧都可能重建(pose 流 20–60 Hz),没有它\n'
              '    // 每次重建都会把提示重新点亮 —— 那就等于常驻,只是绕了个圈。\n'
              '    if (widget.mode == oldWidget.mode) return;\n'
              '    setState(() => _visible = true);\n'
              '    _armDismiss();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  void _armDismiss() {\n'
              '    _fadeTimer?.cancel();\n'
              '    _fadeTimer = Timer(const Duration(seconds: 3), () {\n'
              '      if (mounted) setState(() => _visible = false);\n'
              '    });\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  void dispose() {\n'
              '    _fadeTimer?.cancel();\n'
              '    super.dispose();\n'
              '  }\n'
              '\n'
              '  @override\n'
              '  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n'
              '    return IgnorePointer(\n'
              '      child: AnimatedOpacity(\n'
              '        opacity: _visible ? 1.0 : 0.0,\n'
              '        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 250),\n'
              '        child: _IdleHintPill(text: autoCaptureTopHintText(widget.mode)),\n'
              '      ),\n'
              '    );\n'
              '  }\n'
